# 第 9 章　在我们的时代里

*2.2 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> In Our Time　·　在我们的时代里

## 069　The Great Train Robbery　火车大劫案

> 世界：Astral Express　|　区域：Parlor Car　|　角色：Dan Heng; Pom-Pom; Boothill; Acheron; Gopher Wood　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''A Walk Among the Tombstones'' completed

### Continue the story from the designated Express location

> *Despite Aventurine revealing The Family's dark secrets in Penacony to all at the cost of his own life, the countdown to the Charmony Festival continues thanks to the operations of The Family's united efforts. At the same time, the Astral Express seems to have welcomed an uninvited guest...*

[Recap] The Galaxy Ranger Boothill hijacked the Astral Express, saying he needed the help of the Nameless. To prove the Crew's identity, Dan Heng presented the jade abacus of allying oath, a gift from the Xianzhou Alliance to the Express. He persuaded Boothill to lower the Ranger's weapon.

12 system hours until Charmony Festival - Astral Express

**Male Voice:** Newsflash: Penacony's Charmony Festival has entered its countdown phase. Accompanied by Clockie's tick-tocks, after 12 system hours, this grand celebration will commence with much fanfare...

> **男声：**本台快讯：匹诺康尼「谐乐大典」已进入倒计时阶段。伴随着钟表小子的嘀嗒声，12个系统时后，这场庆典即将迎来盛大的开幕……

**Pom-Pom:** I already told you! We can talk things out!

> **帕姆：**都跟你说了！有什么事情可以好好商量！

**???:** I'm sorry, fluffy. I really have something urgent to attend to, so I had no choice but to resort to asking this favor of you all.

> **？？？：**不好意思了，毛茸茸的小家伙，我实在有些要紧事要办，不得不用这种方式请你们帮个小忙。

**Dan Heng:** Since you already know what you're doing, I'll also have to remind you of its risks.

> **丹恒：**既然你知道自己在做什么，那我也必须提醒你这么做的风险。

**???:** Hey partner, what's with the hostility? I thought pulling this thing out was just a way of saying hello?

> **？？？：**兄弟，搞这么剑拔弩张做什么，掏这玩意儿不就是为了打个招呼？

**Dan Heng:** For the last time, state your identity and purpose.

> **丹恒：**最后一次，表明你的身份和来意。

**???:** My name's Boothill, and I'm a Galaxy Ranger.

> **？？？：**我叫波提欧，是个「巡海游侠」。

**Dan Heng:** A Galaxy Ranger?

> **丹恒：**巡海游侠？

**Boothill:** You look like you've seen a ghost. Did you think we all went extinct? Haha! Well, that's the price you pay for being off-grid for too long.

> **波提欧：**至于吗？表情跟活见了鬼似的，以为我们已经死绝了是吧？哈哈！确实，销声匿迹太久了就这坏处。

**Dan Heng:** The righteous heroes of The Hunt would never hijack the Astral Express.

> **丹恒：**「巡猎」的义侠团体可不会劫持星穹列车。

**Boothill:** I ain't hijacked anything! What, chatting with someone while holding a gun is considered a hijacking?

> **波提欧：**我这不还没劫持呢么，拿着枪和人聊聊天就算「劫持」？

**Pom-Pom:** It probably is...

> **帕姆：**大概算的帕……

**Dan Heng:** Pardon my frankness, but there are plenty of rumors in the cosmos regarding the Galaxy Rangers' current status, and none of them are pretty stories. I have a hard time believing you.

> **丹恒：**恕我直言，银河中有许多关于巡海游侠现状的传闻，都不是什么美好的故事。我很难相信你。

**Boothill:** This is hilarious. The tale that this bunch of fools spin is getting out of hand — there's even a bit about the Galaxy Rangers being turned into gibbons by Dr. Primitive, and they're in some valley screwing around on swings.

> **波提欧：**笑死了，这帮傻宝编的故事越来越离谱，还说巡海游侠全都被原始博士变成长臂猿了，正在哪个山沟里荡秋千呢。

**Boothill:** Of course, I know you won't believe me. Which is also why, similarly, I have a hard time believing that you're the real Nameless.

> **波提欧：**当然，我知道你们不会信。所以同样地，我也不能轻信你们就是真的无名客。

**Boothill:** See the bullets in this gun? 9-millimeter, an eternal classic. I may need the Astral Express' help right now, but if you're an imposter just like that one... Tsk, then this bullet might just end up in my head.

> **波提欧：**瞧见这枪里的子弹了么？九毫米，永远的经典。眼下我需要星穹列车的帮助，但如果你们和那家伙一样是群冒牌货…啧，这子弹怕是要躺我脑门里了。

**Boothill:** I can't allow myself to be exposed to danger. That's just the way it goes. So, you all have to first prove yourselves... Huh, where are you going?

> **波提欧：**我可不能让自己身陷危险，是这个道理吧。所以你们得先证明自己…哎，往哪走呢？

**Dan Heng:** Recognize this?

> **丹恒：**认得这东西么？

**Boothill:** Hah? It's a... muddle-fudger... the Jade Abacus of Allying Oath!? The Xianzhou really gave this to you guys!?

> **波提欧：**哈？这是——他宝贝的，结盟玉兆？！仙舟真把这东西给你们了？！

**Pom-Pom:** *confused* M—Muddle-fudger?

> **帕姆：**宝、宝贝…？

**Dan Heng:** This is the jade abacus gifted to the Express by the Xianzhou Luofu's General Jing Yuan. Its presence onboard serves as the Xianzhou Alliance's official recognition of the Express.

> **丹恒：**这是仙舟「罗浮」景元将军赠予列车的玉兆。这物件在车上，便是仙舟联盟对星穹列车身份的认证。

**Dan Heng:** Is that enough?

> **丹恒：**足够了么？

**Boothill:** ...Not bad, kiddo.

> **波提欧：**…行啊，小子。

[Recap] Boothill responded to Dan Heng's questioning by stating that Galaxy Rangers gathered under a shared "baseline." He hijacked the Express to reach Penacony and confront Acheron, an Emanator of the Nihility.

**Boothill:** And across these sprawlin' stars, a gentle squeeze is all it takes to rustle up a whole legion of Cloud Knights. Now I reckon that'd be one fudgin' sight to behold.

> **波提欧：**无论银河浩瀚，只需轻轻一握便能召唤成千上万的云骑军…宝了个贝的，得有多壮观啊。

**Dan Heng:** Now, it's your turn.

> **丹恒：**现在轮到你了。

**Boothill:** Been ages since the Galaxy Rangers had the spotlight shone on 'em, and we ain't equipped with such fancy gadgets. But I've been around the block enough to know the way to handle these types of situations is easy as pie...

> **波提欧：**巡海游侠离开聚光灯太久了，没这种道上公认的好东西。但这种场合我见得多了，解决方式也很简单……

**Boothill:** Alright then, feel free to toss any question my way, and let's see if my answers can't earn your trust. If your gut tells you otherwise, it still ain't too late to show me the door.

> **波提欧：**来吧，你可以随便向我发问，看看我的回答能不能赢得你的信任。要是真觉得可疑，再让我离开也不迟。

**Dan Heng:** And why would I play along?

> **丹恒：**我凭什么配合你？

**Boothill:** If I truly am a real Galaxy Ranger, you stand to lose nothing.

> **波提欧：**如果我真是巡海游侠，这么做你绝对不会吃亏。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Dan Heng:** Alright then. Tell me, what kind of organization are the Galaxy Rangers?

> **丹恒：**说说看，巡海游侠是个怎样的组织？

**Boothill:** Ha, my friend, this question is a hard one. Don't think I can even consider us an "organization" to begin with.

> **波提欧：**哈，兄弟，这问题够难回答的。我甚至很难说我们是个组织。

**Boothill:** Everyone's on their own fated path along The Hunt, with their own resolute sense of righteousness, and not so... welcome among such so-called universal values.

> **波提欧：**无非是大家都走在「巡猎」的命途上，都有自己坚信的正义，都不是那么…受所谓「普世价值」的欢迎呗。

**Dan Heng:** This reply does not instill trust, and only makes your predicament more precarious.

> **丹恒：**这种回答得不到信任，只会让你的处境更加危险。

**Boothill:** I'm guessing you're gonna ask about some form of shared faith, right? But us Galaxy Rangers don't need that sort of thing. What brings us together is a shared bottom line.

> **波提欧：**我猜你是想问什么共同的「信念」吧？但巡海游侠用不着那种东西，我们聚在一起，靠的是共同的「底线」。

**Boothill:** Never bully the weak, never kill the innocent... These oaths aren't some lofty beliefs, but the fundamental bottom line that one must never cross as a person.

> **波提欧：**不可欺凌弱小、不可滥杀无辜…这些个誓言可不是什么高高在上的信念，而是做人绝不应该触犯的底线。

**Boothill:** As Galaxy Rangers, we strictly adhere to the bottom line — when someone crosses them, The Hunt's vengeance will surely come knockin'.

> **波提欧：**身为巡海游侠，我们只看底线，每当有人要践踏它时，「巡猎」的复仇就会找上门去。

**Boothill:** And in this moment, the other crucial meaning of "bottom line" comes into play — as long as you don't cross it, you're free to do whatever you please. You catch my drift?

> **波提欧：**而这时，「底线」的另一重意义就来了——只要不触碰它，你做什么都行，明白么？

**Dan Heng:** Second question: Why do you seek trouble with the Astral Express?

> **丹恒：**第二个问题，为什么要找星穹列车的麻烦。

**Boothill:** I already said that I seek no trouble. I must go to Penacony for a matter, but I don't have an invite, and I can't even enter The Family's hotel doors. If only... I could borrow the Nameless' identity. The entire cosmos knows you're guests of The Family.

> **波提欧：**都说了，没想找你们麻烦。我有事必须去趟匹诺康尼，但没有邀请函，家族连酒店门都不让进。这不…只能借用下无名客的身份。全银河都知道你们是家族的贵客。

**Pom-Pom:** Aren't the Galaxy Rangers also esteemed guests?

> **帕姆：**巡海游侠不也是吗？

**Boothill:** Oh! You've hit the nail on the head... This is why I'm here! It's fine if I tell you — the Rangers are pursuing an imposter, a son of a nice lady posing as one of us. She's on Penacony right now.

> **波提欧：**哦！你说到点子上了…我就是为此而来的！告诉你们也无妨，游侠们在追杀一个「冒牌货」，一个穿我们衣服冒名顶替的小可爱，她现在就在匹诺康尼。

**Boothill:** My informant is a Memokeeper. She's the same as all memetic organisms — appearing one moment, and gone the next. She scares the fudge outta me. Still, she gave me some vital info.

> **波提欧：**我的线人是个忆者。她就和所有的模因生物一样，突然出现，又突然消失。真他宝贝的吓人。但她提供了重要线索。

**Dan Heng:** That Galaxy Ranger imposter... Who is it?

> **丹恒：**那个冒充巡海游侠的人…是谁？

**Boothill:** Is that the third question?

> **波提欧：**这算是第三个问题了？

**Dan Heng:** Is it a hard question?

> **丹恒：**很难回答么？

**Boothill:** It isn't, just that you might not believe me. That person calls herself Acheron, and according to our informant, she could be... an Emanator of Nihility.

> **波提欧：**不难，只是说出来怕你不信。那家伙自称「黄泉」，根据我们的眼线，她很可能是一个…「虚无」的令使。

**Dan Heng:** That's impossible.

> **丹恒：**这不可能。

**Boothill:** That's what I said. Don't worry. When I first received the news, I had the exact same reaction as you.

> **波提欧：**看我怎么说的，放心，我刚得到这消息的时候，反应和你一模一样。

**Dan Heng:** IX never gives anyone so much as a first glance, and that's perfectly normal. What reason would THEY have to bequeath strength unto mortals?

> **丹恒：**Ⅸ从不瞥视任何人，这才符合常理。祂有什么理由授予凡人力量？

**Boothill:** Then you must know that Emanators can also conceal their own identities, which, for many people, is better that way. Otherwise, there'd be wanton bloodshed across the cosmos, or even... perhaps... turning their back on the Path they're supposed to follow.

> **波提欧：**那你一定知道令使也可以隐瞒自己的身份，甚至对不少人而言，这么做更好，不然就意味着走上银河厮杀大舞台，甚至…背叛所行的命途。

**Boothill:** I had the good fortune of running into an Elation Emanator — its appearance was no different than that of those clowns. If it weren't through sheer luck that I got it drunk, I would have never known of its eminent status!

> **波提欧：**我就有幸见识过一位「欢愉」令使，只看外表和那帮小丑根本没区别。要不是老子运气好把它灌醉了，还真不知道对面的身份这么「显赫」！

**Boothill:** Even in the purest Hunt, you'll find the Xianzhou Alliance under the spotlight and Galaxy Rangers lurking in the shadows. Paths are inevitably concepts created by people, and exist in planes beyond our understanding.

> **波提欧：**即便是最纯粹的「巡猎」也有灯光下的仙舟联盟和阴影中的巡海游侠。命途终究是由人创造的概念，一定存在超出你我认知的外界。

**Boothill:** To reckon that Nihility Emanators don't exist...? Maybe we just ain't nihilistic enough.

> **波提欧：**认为「虚无」不存在令使…没准只是因为我们还不够「虚无」呢。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Boothill:** So do you understand now? Your companions are in danger, and it's pretty harrowing. If you don't want to believe me, you'd best send a message to 'em. But I'd advise you to move fast...

> **波提欧：**所以明白了么，你的伙伴现在很危险，可以说是相当危险。如果还不相信，你大可给他们发个消息看看。但我会劝你动作快点……

**Boothill:** We don't know what's happening in the Dreamscape, or how much of what the Memokeeper said is true...

> **波提欧：**毕竟咱俩谁都不知道梦境里发生了什么，也同样不知道那忆者的话有几分是真……

**Boothill:** And that Acheron... Who knows what she intends to do.

> **波提欧：**以及那位「黄泉」…究竟打算做什么。

[Recap] To prevent the Dreamscape from being consumed by the Nihility, the Dreammaster issued an expulsion order to Acheron. She left as requested, but gave a warning: Penacony had strayed from the Harmony and was destined for destruction.

**Acheron:** ...I don't intend to do anything.

> **黄泉：**…我不打算做什么。

**???:** That's not up to you.

> **？？？：**这由不得你。

**???:** Did you know? People who come to the land of dreams for the first time, they'll subconsciously stop to reaffirm that they're still walking on solid ground. And then, they will unanimously raise their heads to gaze at the sky.

> **？？？：**知道么？第一次来到梦想之地的人，会下意识地停下脚步，确认自己仍站在坚实的土地上。然后，他们会不约而同地抬头望向天空。

**???:** Be it reality or dream, staring at the sky is instinctual for humanity. Since the day that the Golden Hour was completed, it's always been there, watching over every single night of decadence.

> **？？？：**无论现实或梦境，仰望天空是人类的本能。自「黄金的时刻」落成的那天起，它就一直在那里，守望每一个声色犬马的夜晚。

**???:** But now this night sky has been mercilessly severed, dyed with the mist of Nihility. And this whole event... happened within the course of a single slash of a blade.

> **？？？：**可如今这片夜空却被无情斩断，染上「虚无」的阴霾。而这个过程…仅仅只在一刀之间。

**Acheron:** A single slash of a blade... isn't really accurate. It was actually two blades, just that the second one was faster.

> **黄泉：**「一刀」…并不准确。其实是两刀，只是第二斩比较迅速。

**???:** That's not the point.

> **？？？：**重点不在于此。

**???:** Many guests who weren't supposed to be invited have gathered at this banquet. Even if the Harmony is all-embracing, I have no choice but to show some of them the door... for the sake of Penacony, and the peace.

> **？？？：**这场盛会聚集了太多不应被邀请的客人。纵使「同谐」包容万象，为了匹诺康尼的和平，我也不得不对其中的一些人下达逐客令……

**???:** The Planet of Festivities has no place for you, a puppet of Nihility. Those who live in the shadows do not bear the right to tread the illuminated stage.

> **？？？：**盛会之星容不下你，虚无的偃偶。活在阴影中的人，不应走上光亮的台前。

**Acheron:** Speaking of living in the shadows, there's probably not much difference between us. It's only polite to reveal your true self, at least when speaking to others...

> **黄泉：**就生活在阴影中这点，我们应该没有区别。至少在和别人对话时，你应该现出本貌……

**Acheron:** Penacony's Dreammaster.

> **黄泉：**匹诺康尼的「梦主」。

**"Dreammaster":** That's just another reason that you can't stay.

> **「梦主」：**不能让你留下的理由，又多了一个。

**"Dreammaster":** Whether you believe it or not, this is the real "me."

> **「梦主」：**但无论你是否相信，这就是真实的「我」。

**"Dreammaster":** We are one.

> **「梦主」：**不仅如此，每一位都是。

**Acheron:** Is this the "United as One" that The Family espouses?

> **黄泉：**这就是家族口中的「万众一心」？

**"Dreammaster":** My mortal shell has long since dissipated. The Oak Family's one hundred and seven thousand, three hundred and thirty-six offspring are now my eyes, ears and mouths, spreading joy across dreams when required, and in times of essential need... exiling evil from this haven in my stead.

> **「梦主」：**我的凡胎早已消散，橡木家系的十万七千三百三十六名孩子是我如今的眼、耳、口，在需要时代我将谐乐在这美梦中传扬，在必要时…替我将罪恶从这乐园里流放。

**Acheron:** From the sound of it, it seems like you're asking me to leave Penacony.

> **黄泉：**听起来，你要请我离开匹诺康尼了。

**"Dreammaster":** I am glad that you're an understanding one. Alas, I'm not "asking."

> **「梦主」：**很高兴你尚有自知之明，可惜，没有「请」。

**Acheron:** If you think you can.

> **黄泉：**你觉得自己能做到。

**"Dreammaster":** Are you threatening me?

> **「梦主」：**你是在威胁我么？

**Acheron:** Hmm... I ended it with a period. It was a statement, not a threat.

> **黄泉：**嗯…我用的是句号。这是一种…陈述。

**Acheron:** Knowing who I am and still showing such malice, you're not the first, nor will you be the last. This scene played out many times before — and usually, when faced with my questions, most people retort "Why can't I?"

> **黄泉：**在知晓我身份后仍能流露出如此恶意的人，你不是第一个，也不会是最后一个。此情此景发生过太多次，面对我的问询，人们大多会反问「有何不可」。

**Acheron:** The result has invariably been that they "can't."

> **黄泉：**但结果…一直都是不可。

**"Dreammaster":** You are confident, but be reminded: The Family is forgiving, but not weak. The chords of the Harmony extend across worlds — if you do not comply, when the blade is unsheathed for even a hair's breadth, you will never be able to escape the Eternal Centurion's wrath in all of your lifetime.

> **「梦主」：**你很自信，但请记得——家族谦逊，可从不软弱。「同谐」的弦音遍及寰宇，若你不从，只要那把长刀出鞘分毫，你终其一生都无法逃离「无限夫长」的怒火。

**"Dreammaster":** One hundred and thirty-seven individuals: That is how many heathens I have exiled since I became Dreammaster. Among them, were those who once severed my wings, and those who immolated my body...

> **「梦主」：**而在那之前…一百三十七人，这是自成为梦主以来，我亲手流放的外邦人。他们中曾有人折断我的双翼，曾有人将我的身躯焚毁……

**"Dreammaster":** And here I stand again, about to add another mark to the tally.

> **「梦主」：**但今天，我仍站在这里，不介意为这数字再添一笔。

**Acheron:** And you will die. I mean... all of you will.

> **黄泉：**然后你会死。我的意思是…「你们」都会。

**"Dreammaster":** ...

> **「梦主」：**……

**Acheron:** ...

> **黄泉：**……

**Acheron:** ...But that won't come to pass. I'll do as you ask. I'll leave.

> **黄泉：**…但那种事不会发生。就照你说的，我会离开。

**"Dreammaster":** A wise choice.

> **「梦主」：**十分明智的选择。

**Acheron:** I wasn't aware there was a choice.

> **黄泉：**似乎也没有别的选项。

**"Dreammaster":** To you, that surely is the only option. Please bear in mind, you and Penacony are of different worlds — those born on the far bank cannot seek solace across the river.

> **「梦主」：**于你而言，这的确是唯一的道路。请时刻记得，你和匹诺康尼不属于一个世界，生于彼岸者，无法在此岸寻得归处。

**"Dreammaster":** Leave, and never return. The radiance of the Planet of Festivities is overwhelmingly bright, luring in tricksters, wrongdoers, and criminals. But even the Harmony itself... will never welcome the Self-Annihilator of Nihility.

> **「梦主」：**离开，永远别再回来。盛会之星的光芒太过耀眼，吸引了太多骗子、恶徒、罪犯，但即便是「同谐」…也绝对不会欢迎「虚无」的自灭者。

**"Dreammaster":** And even more so, when this Self-Annihilator heralds the destruction of everything. Your strength is obviously a gift of the sleeping and shapeless, immeasurable and fathomless, like a tributary spawned from the abyss that brings death and sin to all.

> **「梦主」：**更何况这位自灭者还要带着周遭的一切入灭。你的力量分明是沉眠无相者的馈赠，深不见底，就像是深渊中淌出的一条支流，为众生带去死亡与罪恶。

**"Dreammaster":** Acheron... a befitting name.

> **黄泉：**把这当作来自彼岸之人的忠告吧：你比我更清楚，匹诺康尼已然背离了「同谐」。无论你们的目的是什么…我只能看见一种结局。

**Acheron:** Its future holds nothing but Nihility, just like all the worlds that have drowned in THEIR shadow.

> **黄泉：**它的未来是一片「虚无」，就和所有溺亡在祂阴影中的世界一样。

## 071　Small Town Grotesque　小城畸人

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Dreamflux Reef　|　角色：Trailblazer; Firefly; Welt; Misha; Clockie; Kaimee; March 7th; Jessie; Himeko; Micah; Robin; Grace; Gopher Wood; Sunday; Gallagher　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''The Fool Always Rings Twice'' completed

[Recap] Firefly confessed to you her identity as the Stellaron Hunter, SAM, and said she had reached another Memory Zone, the "Land of the Exiles," through "Death." Firefly asked you to close your eyes, saying she would go with you to witness the truth.

In the meantime...

**Firefly:** We're back to where it all began. You entered the Golden Hour from this place, and it is also from here where you will enter the true Penacony.

> **流萤：**一切回到了原点，你曾经从这里踏入「黄金的时刻」，也将从这里踏入真正的「匹诺康尼」。

**Firefly:** It's a pleasure to journey alongside you once more, but it's time I laid bare the entire truth before you. As you might have heard... I also go by another name: Stellaron Hunter, Sam.

> **流萤：**很高兴能再次与你同行。事到如今，我也终于能向你袒露全部的事实。如你所见…我的另一个名字是「星核猎手」萨姆。

- ▸ Are you... all right?

> ▸ 你…没事吗？

**Firefly:** I'm fine... Sorry, I hope I haven't scared you.

> **流萤：**我没事…抱歉，希望先前没有吓到你。

- ▸ You lied to me...

> ▸ 你欺骗了我…

**Firefly:** I'm sorry. I had no other choice...

> **流萤：**抱歉，我不得不这么做……

- ▸ Are you... Firefly's soul?

> ▸ 你是…流萤的灵魂？

**Firefly:** I know you have many questions... Do you remember when we encountered "Death" in that strange Dreamscape? When I was caught by that meme...

> **流萤：**我知道你有很多疑问…还记得我们在陌生的梦境里遭遇了「死亡」吗？那时我被迷因攥在手中……

**Firefly:** In that instant before it killed me, I saw the reflection of another Dreamscape in its ghastly pupils.

> **流萤：**在它即将行凶的瞬间，我从那只骇人的瞳孔里看见了「另一片梦境」的倒影。

**Firefly:** So, following the clues in the script, I came up with some theories about the meme. That's why I instructed Silver Wolf to issue invitations, drawing everyone to the dream's hotel...

> **流萤：**根据「剧本」的提示，我对那只迷因有了些猜想。于是我让银狼寄出邀请，将各位引导至梦中的酒店……

**Firefly:** I intended to call upon "Death" before you arrived, to solve the riddle using more direct means, and then invite you to join. However, contrary to my wishes, I couldn't defy the script, and I didn't get a chance to explain it to you.

> **流萤：**我本打算在你们面前唤来「死亡」，用更直接的手段揭示谜底，邀请你们入局。但事与愿违，我无法违背「剧本」，甚至来不及开口向你说明。

**Firefly:** It is how you see now — I was impaled by the bladed wings of "Death." The heavy pressure of concentrated memoria miasma... exploded in my mind, as if it was actually reality.

> **流萤：**就像你看到的，我被「死亡」的翼刃贯穿。浓稠忆质的重压…它们在脑海中炸开，恍若现实。

**Firefly:** But after the momentary numbness subsided, I found that my body was absolutely unscathed.

> **流萤：**但一瞬的麻痹散去后，我发现自己的身体完好无损。

**Firefly:** I was still alive, and it was just as I thought — I had arrived at a place starkly different from this beautiful dream. Beneath the Dreamscape of Penacony lies another more chaotic, more primal Memory Zone. Its name... Land of the Exiles.

> **流萤：**我果然还活着，且如我所想——抵达了与「美梦」截然不同的地方。匹诺康尼的梦境下埋藏着另一片更原始，也更混沌的忆域，它的名字是…「流放之地」。

**Firefly:** And so then I returned to the hotel in the Dreamscape, hoping to tell you of its existence. Yet I could not reveal my own identity, so I could only divert your party's attention and lure you away from the battlefield.

> **流萤：**于是我返回梦境中的酒店，想告诉你它的存在。但我还不能公开自己的身份，只能想办法转移与你同行之人的注意，将你带离战场。

**Firefly:** And after, all my attempts proved futile. It wasn't until not long ago, when a crimson blade of light shattered the high wall of the dream, causing you all to fall far into the abyssal depths of the Dreamscape, that I was able to awaken you and your companions one by one...

> **流萤：**而后，我的种种尝试皆以失败告终。直到不久前，一道血红的刀光劈碎了梦中的高墙，令你们跌落到这深层的梦境中，我才得以将你和同伴们一个个唤醒……

**Firefly:** And that's it, that is all that's happened so far.

> **流萤：**这就是此前发生的一切。

- ▸ I completely understand!

> ▸ 我完全理解了！

- ▸ I'm completely confused!

> ▸ 我完全糊涂了！

**Firefly:** I know it's tough to believe all this without reservations. I just want to say you're very close to the final answer — just one more thing needs to be done, and then I can prove it to you...

> **流萤：**我知道，要无条件地相信这些很难。我只是想说，你离最后的答案很近了。只需做一件事，我就能为你证明……

**Firefly:** Now, let's leave this place... Please close your eyes.

> **流萤：**接下来，让我们离开这里吧…请闭上眼。

**Firefly:** Take a deep breath, and visualize the Dreamscape's outline in your heart. And remember... you must not open your eyes at all times.

> **流萤：**然后深呼吸，在心里描绘梦境的轮廓，记得…绝对不要睁眼。

**Firefly:** Three...

> **流萤：**三……

**Firefly:** Two...

> **流萤：**二……

**Firefly:** One...

> **流萤：**一……

**Firefly:** Don't be afraid. The one who has come to greet us... has arrived.

> **流萤：**不用害怕，迎接我们的人…来了。

After a piercing screech, a thick and ferocious surge of memoria crashes into your chest, churning and ravaging. Your consciousness becomes like scraps of paper caught in a whirlpool, breaking apart, dissolving, and dispersing within the turbulent muddy current.

Innumerable voices resonate through the symphony of memoria like roaring thunder, and among them, one echo stands out with exceptional clarity. You know it comes from the girl beside you, your hearts beating to the same rhythm in silence, and then, more silence...

Until memories stir up ripples in that quiet darkness...

[Recap] Firefly and Blade discussed the "script" on the eve of the Jepella Rebellion. Blade followed the "script" mechanically while Firefly questioned the predetermined ending, seeking a sense of self beyond the mission of destruction.

...

**Firefly:** I never knew you could do this.

> **流萤：**我从来不知道，你还会做这种事。

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Firefly:** ...Do you have a driver's license?

> **流萤：**…你有驾驶证吗？

**Blade:** I do.

> **刃：**有。

**Firefly:** That is... surprising.

> **流萤：**这可真是…令人意外。

**Blade:** Why? Because this is Jepella, the city of sins?

> **刃：**为什么，因为这里是罪恶都市耶佩拉？

**Firefly:** Mhmm, no... it's nothing.

> **流萤：**嗯，不…没什么。

**Firefly:** I'm just thinking, you haven't slept in twenty system hours... Are you sure you're okay?

> **流萤：**我只是觉得，你已经二十个系统时没睡了…没问题吗？

**Blade:** I'm fine. I'll survive. Same goes for you.

> **刃：**没问题。死不了，我和你都一样。

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Firefly:** I'm not so sure about that. Slow down a bit.

> **流萤：**我可未必，还是开慢点吧。

**Blade:** Infiltration is over. Feel free to activate Sam anytime you like.

> **刃：**潜入已经结束了，你随时可以启动「萨姆」。

**Firefly:** There's still some time before the next part of the script unfolds. Let me stay a little longer in this body.

> **流萤：**离「剧本」下一幕还有些时间，让我再多待会儿吧，用这副身体。

A long silence ensued. Neither of them brought up any topic, seemingly accustomed to this silence.

It wasn't until much later that a soft sigh once again broke the quiet in the car.

**Firefly:** Such a long tunnel. Didn't feel this long when I set off.

> **流萤：**好长的隧道啊。出发的时候，没觉得它有这么长。

**Blade:** In half a system hour, it will lead us to Kafka, and then comes the downfall of the Jepella Brotherhood.

> **刃：**半个系统时后，它会带我们找到卡芙卡，接着就是耶佩拉兄弟会的覆灭。

**Firefly:** Is that also part of the script?

> **流萤：**这些也都是「剧本」？

**Blade:** It's in your script, too.

> **刃：**你的「剧本」里也有。

**Firefly:** Sorry, I didn't notice.

> **流萤：**抱歉，我没注意。

**Blade:** Their destiny won't change just because of your selective ignorance.

> **刃：**就算你选择性忽视，他们的命运也不会改变。

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Blade:** I told you before, it's a bad habit.

> **刃：**我说过，这是个坏习惯。

**Firefly:** What about you, then? Is this the moment you finally find the "Death" you've been looking for?

> **流萤：**那你呢？这一次，你能得到想要的「死亡」么？

**Blade:** As always, it's a blank slate. It's not on this planet. Why the sudden inquiry?

> **刃：**一如既往，一片空白。它不在这颗星球。为什么突然问这个？

**Firefly:** Because I'm currently in a car with a sleep-deprived driver... I just want to get there in one piece.

> **流萤：**因为我正在一辆疲劳驾驶的车上…我希望它能安全抵达。

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Blade:** This car has full self-driving capabilities. I'll just put my hand on the steering wheel. Will that do?

> **刃：**这车有自动驾驶，我只是把手搭在方向盘上，行了吧？

**Firefly:** Hey, don't take everything so seriously.

> **流萤：**开玩笑的啦。

**Firefly:** ...Elio would always say there's only one type of destiny: The inescapable type. He can see the future, and we... likewise are aware of our "predetermined end."

> **流萤：**…艾利欧总说命运只有一种，谁也无法绕开。他能看见未来，而我们…同样知晓自己「既定的结局」。

**Firefly:** But before that moment arrives, we can still choose what we do... We all have this right, don't we?

> **流萤：**但在那一刻到来前，人依旧能为自己选择去做些什么…我们都有这个权利，对吧？

**Blade:** After today, Jepella's name will disappear from cosmic history, and the Ever-Flame Mansion will take its place. In the not-too-distant future, you'll receive an invitation...

> **刃：**今天过后，[https://www.bilibili.com/video/BV1Nu411H7C6?p=1 耶佩拉]的名字就会从银河历史中消失，而永火官邸将取而代之，在不远的未来收到一封[https://www.bilibili.com/video/BV1UK411e7tY?p=1 邀请函]……

**Blade:** That's your next stop.

> **刃：**那就是你的下一站。

**Firefly:** Land of the Dreams, Penacony.

> **流萤：**梦想之地，匹诺康尼。

**Blade:** I hope you find whatever you seek there, be it answers... or salvation.

> **刃：**祝你在那里找到想要的答案…或者，解脱。

...

[Recap] Together, you encountered Welt and learned that the Crew had already arrived at Dreamflux Reef. "Death" served as the guardian of the Land of the Exiles, and from the words of those around you, a familiar name surfaced — Gallagher.

**Welt:** (Trailblazer), glad to see you're safe and sound.

> **瓦尔特：**开拓者，很高兴看见你平安无事。

- ▸ Is this...

> ▸ 这里是…

- ▸ That voice just now...

> ▸ 刚才的声音是…

- ▸ Mr. Yang, you're here too?

> ▸ 瓦尔特先生，你也在？

**Firefly:** "Close your eyes"... This is the answer. Isn't it incredible? The monster that we have always known as "Death," is actually the guardian of the land of the exiles. It abides by a certain rule, abducting people from their dreams and bringing them here.

> **流萤：**「闭上眼睛」…这就是答案。很不可思议吧，一直被我们视作「死亡」的怪物，其实是流放之地的守卫。它遵循某种特定的规律，将美梦中的人掳走，带往这里。

**Welt:** The question that has been perplexing us — "Does death really exist in the Dreamscape?" — appears to be a cognitive trap. It was laid by those orchestrating events from the shadows to cover up the truth behind the disappearances, and the existence of this fortress known as Dreamflux Reef.

> **瓦尔特：**我们此前一直在困惑的「梦境中是否存在真正的死亡」，现在看来完全是幕后主使设下的思维陷阱，为了掩盖人们失踪的真相，以及…这座名为「流梦礁」的城寨。

**Welt:** Every emergence of that meme is related to the Watchmaker. Since Dreamflux Reef is where it brings its captives, it's likely that many of our longstanding questions will be answered in this place.

> **瓦尔特：**那只迷因的每一次出现都与「钟表匠」有关，既然流梦礁是它将众人掳走的目的地，想必不少困扰我们许久的问题，都能在此地得到启发。

**Firefly:** The atmosphere here is starkly different from the beautiful dream. There are no regulators here like The Family, and they all look like they're mildly dazed.

> **流萤：**这里的氛围和美梦截然不同，人们生活得极其松散，不存在家族那样的管理者，精神状态也有些微妙的恍惚。

**Firefly:** But from the whispers of the residents, I've heard a familiar name... Gallagher.

> **流萤：**但从居民们的只言片语中，我得到了一个熟悉的名字…「加拉赫」。

**Welt:** It's that man again, always in the right place at the right time... though that does save us the trouble of looking for him.

> **瓦尔特：**又是这个男人，总是恰到好处地出现在每一个关键的地方…倒也为我们省去了寻人的麻烦。

**Welt:** Himeko and March have already made a move. (Trailblazer), get ready. We're about to set off.

> **瓦尔特：**姬子和三月已先一步动身了。开拓者，调整下状态，我们这就出发。

### Advance down the narrow alley

> *Firefly explains to you the deeper reasons behind her various inexplicable behaviors, and guides you to dive deeper into a place declared as a "nightmare" by The Family... The primitive Memory Zone outside the sweet dream: "Dreamflux Reef." This move also exposes some of the truths behind "Death" — It is nothing but a tamed memetic entity that assists specific people who wish to travel between dreams. "Closing your eyes" is merely a strange invocation used to summon them. After a brief moment of discomfort, you realize that you have arrived at an abandoned old town. Once you have spoken with everyone and prepared yourself, alight and begin your adventure at this abandoned station.*

**(Trailblazer):** (The real Dreamscape, the land of the exiles... Before we go, should we speak to everyone first?)

> **开拓者：**（真正的梦境，流放之地…出发前，要再和大家聊聊么？）

[Recap] Before departure, you discussed with Welt about Acheron's strike, the information provided by Gallagher, and Firefly's identity. Welt hoped you'd calmed down and would set out once you were ready.

*(Talk to Welt, optional)*

**Welt:** Something on your mind? Let's talk about it.

> **瓦尔特：**有什么担忧，不妨说出来一起讨论讨论。

- ▸ Acheron's blade slash...

> ▸ 为什么不说出真实身份？

**Welt:** No wonder Miss Acheron is so averse to drawing her blade... it's hard to imagine such terrifying power could reside in an ordinary sheath. If it weren't for the fact that Aventurine's power originated from the Preservation, the entire Dreamscape would have been affected.

> **瓦尔特：**难怪黄泉小姐始终不愿拔刀…难以想象那朴素的刀鞘里竟蕴含着如此恐怖的力量。若非砂金的力量源自「存护」，恐怕整片梦境都会受到波及。

**Welt:** Don't feel burdened by this. Even without that Stellaron inside you, Aventurine would still have found other methods to accomplish his goal. Let's just believe in Miss Acheron. And given her prowess, I don't think we've got anything to worry about.

> **瓦尔特：**不必为此感到负担。即便没有你体内的那颗星核，砂金也会找到其他方法达成目的。眼下就相信黄泉小姐吧。考虑到她的实力，我们应该也无需担心。

- ▸ What are your thoughts on Gallagher?

> ▸ 星核猎手也想要遗产吗？

**Welt:** During your investigation, he shared a vital piece of information. Mikhail, the former Watchmaker who collaborated with The Family to construct the Penacony we're familiar with today, had a falling out with The Family for specific reasons.

> **瓦尔特：**在你们开展调查时，他提供了关键信息：米哈伊尔——曾经的「钟表匠」——与家族一同建立起如今的匹诺康尼，又因为某些原因与家族决裂。

**Welt:** But this is precisely where the problem lies — you were clearly investigating a murder. So then why, as a security officer, is he changing the subject to talk about his past with the Watchmaker?

> **瓦尔特：**但问题正在于此，你们明明是在侦办凶案，身为治安官的他为何顾左右而言他，开始讲述自己和「钟表匠」的过去？

**Welt:** And now with Firefly mentioning his name again... it's hard not to be suspicious.

> **瓦尔特：**现在这个名字又出现在流萤小姐口中…很难不令人心生怀疑。

- ▸ Regarding Firefly and the Crew...

> ▸ 所以萨姆究竟是…

**Welt:** Before we found you, she'd already revealed her Stellaron Hunter identity, and shared a lot of information.

> **瓦尔特：**在找到你之前，她已向我们表明了星核猎手的身份，并分享了许多信息。

**Welt:** Who would've thought that the Molten Knight's true identity was actually a young girl... For her, this is a secret that she cannot allow others to know. That being the case, I think we can believe she's willing to cooperate.

> **瓦尔特：**那位「熔火骑士」的真身竟是一位少女…于她而言，这断然是个不可轻易告人的秘密。从这点来看，可以相信她有合作的意愿。

**Welt:** But, she didn't reveal all her secrets. I just can't shake the feeling that her situation is different from that of the typical dreamer... And I hope that doesn't lead to any dangerous predicaments.

> **瓦尔特：**不过，她也有些秘密没有完全袒露。我总有种感觉，那女孩的状态与一般的入梦者不同…但愿不会造成危险吧。

- ▸ Let's call it a day.

> ▸ 先谈到这里吧。

**Welt:** I hope you've regained a little composure. We'll move out when you're ready.

> **瓦尔特：**希望你已平复了心情。等你做好准备，我们就出发。

[Recap] Firefly said she was constrained by "the script" and could only be honest with you about everything now. Besides that, she wanted to journey with you as "Firefly" rather than "Sam."

*(Talk to Firefly, optional)*

**Firefly:** I'm really sorry for waiting until now to tell you everything.

- ▸ Why couldn't you reveal your true identity?

> ▸ 没关系，我没放在心上。

**Firefly:** Two reasons: Firstly, the script — in the future that Elio saw, Sam and the Astral Express' confrontation was inevitable. I tried to break the shackles of the prophecy, but this is as far as I could go.

**Firefly:** That aside... there were also my personal motives. I wished to travel with you as Firefly... and not Sam.

- ▸ It's all right. I didn't take it personally.

> ▸ 我还是不能接受。

**Firefly:** ...Thank you.

- ▸ This is still hard for me to accept.

> ▸ 黄泉斩下的一刀…

**Firefly:** I... I get it. Being cautious around a Stellaron Hunter isn't a bad thing.

- ▸ Do the Stellaron Hunters also want a piece of the legacy?

> ▸ 如何看待加拉赫…

**Firefly:** Elio only gave me one instruction: Allow the Astral Express to pursue the Grand Legacy. It means that the Watchmaker's Legacy holds great significance to Trailblazing, and to you.

**Firefly:** Elio's scripts used to revolve and expand around certain specific Stellarons. But with your appearance, this condition has apparently ceased to be appropriate. Perhaps, he also saw the "impossible" in the future.

- ▸ So, who exactly is Sam...

> ▸ 关于流萤和列车组…

**Firefly:** Do you still remember the medical cabin I told you about? Well, that's Sam — It belongs to the Iron Cavalry of Glamoth's Firmament Frontline, a Fyrefly Type-IV Strategic Assault Mech.

**Firefly:** It is the cradle of my vitality, and the meaning of my birth. And for the longest time, it was... how I should have looked to the rest of the world.

- ▸ Let's talk later.

> ▸ 就这样吧。

**Firefly:** The timescale of Dreamflux Reef differs from reality, so we mustn't lower our guard. You're sensitive to memoria — A slight misstep, and you could get lost in this Memory Zone.

*(Talk to Muddled Vagrant, optional)*

**Muddled Vagrant:** Welcome to heaven... dreams, true or false... all are ephemeral...

**Muddled Vagrant:** Might as well drink more SoulGlad instead...

*(As the player moves down the narrow alley)*

**Firefly:** Keep going straight down this alley and it'll lead to an elevator. It'll take us to the center of the land of the exiles.

> **流萤：**沿着这条巷道走，能看见一座升降梯。它会带我们去流放之地的中心。

*(Talk to Melancholic Vagrant, optional)*

**Melancholic Vagrant:** Did you also get kicked out of the beautiful dream? I want to go back... but then, I don't want to go back.

**Melancholic Vagrant:** Tell me what I should do. Oh... I don't know what to do...

*(Talk to Happy Vagrant, optional)*

**Happy Vagrant:** Hi there, friend from a distant land!

**Happy Vagrant:** I hear the Watchmaker's Legacy is right here! Let's say we go treasure hunting?

### Wait for the elevator to arrive

> *Firefly explains to you the deeper reasons behind her various inexplicable behaviors, and guides you to dive deeper into a place declared as a "nightmare" by The Family... The primitive Memory Zone outside of the sweet dream: "Dreamflux Reef." This move also exposes some of the truths behind "Death" — It is nothing but a tamed memetic entity that assists specific people who wish to travel between dreams. "Closing your eyes" is merely a strange invocation used to summon them. As you ascend in the elevator, the sound of the old car parts creaking and groaning sounds like the lamenting cries of long-dead souls. Just what could be awaiting you in the depths of this "nightmare"?*

*(When the player arrives near the elevator)*

**Welt:** What a huge Clockie... Looks like the Watchmaker also left his mark on Dreamflux Reef.

> **瓦尔特：**如此巨大的钟表小子…看来那位「钟表匠」也在流梦礁留下了浓墨重彩的一笔。

*(While riding up the elevator)*

**Welt:** Unbelievable... to think there's a settlement of this size within the Dreamscape... and all beyond The Family's reach.

> **瓦尔特：**难以置信，在家族的视线外…梦境中还坐落着如此规模的聚居地。

[Recap] Welt led you to regroup with Himeko and the others. Firefly proposed splitting up to investigate. While the Crew discussed things, she would try to locate Gallagher. On the way, you and Welt encountered Misha.

*(Upon stepping out of the elevator)*

**Welt:** The atmosphere in this fortress is... pretty different from that in the beautiful dream.

> **瓦尔特：**这座城寨的气氛…和美梦大不相同啊。

**Firefly:** When I first saw it, I was in awe too. The sky here, it's like... a reflection of the twelve Dreamscapes.

> **流萤：**第一次看见时，我也很惊讶。这里的天空，就像是…十二梦境的倒影。

**Firefly:** What's even more bizarre, is that this place is also separated into trade and residential areas. The layout may be simple, but the facilities are very comprehensive. It seems that there are quite a number of people living here.

> **流萤：**更奇怪的是，这里也分成了贸易区和居住区，尽管朴素，却十分完备。看来有相当数量的人在此生活。

**Welt:** Though both Dreamscapes have distinct styles, the architectural designs are quite similar — works of the same hand, perhaps. Hard not to speculate on the connection...

> **瓦尔特：**两处梦境虽风格各异，建筑的样式却相差不大，很像是同一位设计师的作品。这种相似背后的联系也引人遐想……

**Welt:** But there's no point in overthinking things. Let's meet up with Himeko and the others first.

> **瓦尔特：**嗯…多想无益，先去和姬子她们会合吧。

**Firefly:** Take a right turn at the end of this road and you'll reach the trade district. There are more people there, and perhaps someone knows where she is.

> **流萤：**走过这条路右转就是贸易区，那里人多一些，也许有人知道她们在哪。

**Welt:** Not coming with us?

> **瓦尔特：**你不与我们同行么？

**Firefly:** The Astral Express likely needs room for some internal deliberation. In the meantime, I'll try and locate Gallagher.

> **流萤：**星穹列车应该需要一些内部讨论的空间。趁这段时间，我会先试着寻找加拉赫。

**Welt:** ...Sure, let's reconnect later.

> **瓦尔特：**…嗯，那我们稍后联系。

**Welt:** Letting her go was the right decision — further observations are needed before we decide whether to trust her. But first, there's someone I need to talk to.

> **瓦尔特：**让她去吧。在判断她是否值得信任前，我们也需要观察。在此之前，我还想找一个人聊聊。

**Welt:** Let's go, (Trailblazer). I'm sure you've already noticed him.

> **瓦尔特：**一起吧，开拓者。我想你应该注意到了。

- ▸ Yep, I saw him right away...

> ▸ 刚才就看到了…

**Welt:** He's right over there...

### Confirm the situation with Misha

> *As the door opens, a cold and uninspiring plaza comes into view, along with two skyscrapers that had long past their glory days. Sensing something was amiss, you decided to go and speak with him, lest you lose sleep at night over this inexplicable phenomenon.*

[Recap] Misha said that both he and Clockie were residents of Dreamflux Reef. The Memory Zone Meme known as "Sleepie," which transported people between dreams, was raised and maintained by Gallagher. Misha volunteered to be your guide.

> **瓦尔特：**你说，这里是你的家？

> **米沙：**是呀。美梦的工作结束后我就会回家。以前交通还算方便，但自从没办法自由通行后，就一直是眠眠带着人们在两座梦境之间往返。

> **瓦尔特：**那位「眠眠」又是…你能形容下它的长相吗？

> **米沙：**眠眠是只忆域迷因，长得凶凶的，有许多只大眼睛。但它其实很听话，一直是加拉赫在照顾眠眠。

> **瓦尔特：**从描述来看，那只迷因毫无疑问就是「死亡」。虽然被家族视作梦魇，但在这边的居民眼中…事实似乎截然不同。

**Misha:** D—Death? Not in a dream, surely. Sleepie's just a little aggressive, and sometimes messes up by fetching the wrong guests, but it would never hurt anyone!

> **米沙：**死、死亡？梦里怎么会有死亡呢，眠眠是比较凶，偶尔也会错把无辜的旅客带回来，但它绝对不会害人！

> **米沙：**这样啊…那你们应该和加拉赫谈谈，不过他正在接待橡木家系的访客，特意嘱咐大家不要打扰。

**Misha:** Oh, I haven't... But please rest assured — Dreamflux Reef is a small place, and it's not as bustling as the beautiful dream, but its safety is unmatched.

> **米沙：**不如这样吧！既然各位客人是初次拜访流梦礁，我来担任向导，先带你们找到同伴，再一起去拜访知更鸟小姐。

### Follow Misha and explore Dreamflux Reef

> *Misha claims Dreamflux Reef to be his home... If that is the case, you can only imagine how bad the treatment must have been at The Reverie. Although, the cute and naïve Misha has not taken that to heart. In fact, seeing that you are in trouble, he offers to lead you on a search for your lost companions and give you a tour of the desolate town along the way. Not only that, you may even have a chance to meet with an old friend... Robin. Before this, however, you need to regroup with March 7th and Himeko. There is no time to lose. Better hurry after Misha.*

[Recap] Misha took you to see the most spectacular sights of Dreamflux Reef, and Welt recognized that the scenery was formed from condensed memoria.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Misha:** Everyone, look! From here, you can see the most spectacular view of Dreamflux Reef—

> **米沙：**各位，请看！这里能够见到流梦礁最壮观的景色——

**Welt:** A black hole? No... an accretion disk formed from consolidated memoria? Was Dreamflux Reef built on such unstable memoria?

> **瓦尔特：**黑洞？不，是忆质凝聚形成的吸积盘么，流梦礁竟然建立在如此不稳定的忆质上。

**Misha:** Oh? So Mr. Yang is also versed in memoria dynamics. I was just trying to figure out how to explain this huge hole to everyone. I bet you guys have a lot in common with Miss Kaimee!

> **米沙：**咦？原来瓦尔特先生也了解忆质动力学，我正发愁怎么向各位介绍这座大空洞呢。既然如此，各位一定和科玫小姐很有共同语言！

*(Upon reaching Kaimee)*

**Misha:** Look, there she is.

> **米沙：**看，她就在那儿。

[Recap] You spoke with Kaimee, who said that based on Razalina's memoria measurement method, something was disturbing the Memory Zone at the "Golden Hour."

*(Talk to Kaimee)*

**Kaimee:** My calculations are finally done. In another ten system hours, the above dream will swallow the dream below! My hypothesis was correct — this place will cease to exist, as the dream devours everything...

> **科玫：**我终于算出来了，再过十个系统时，上面的美梦就会吞掉下面的美梦！我的猜测是对的，这里将不复存在，美梦会吞噬一切……

**Kaimee:** Hmm? Who are you all, and why haven't you left yet? This place is about to disappear!

> **科玫：**嗯？你们是谁，这里要消失了，你们还不走吗？

- ▸ What are you doing out here?

> ▸ 你在这里做什么？

- ▸ Then why aren't you leaving?

> ▸ 你怎么不走？

- ▸ Who are you?

> ▸ 你是谁？

**Kaimee:** I'm Kaimee, a dreamscape surveyor specializing in memoria dynamics, and this is my life's work that I'm researching.

> **科玫：**我是梦境测绘员科玫，专业是忆质动力学，正在钻研自己的毕生课题。

**Kaimee:** See that huge gaping hole? It was just a narrow rift many years ago, but now it's grown into a giant hole. The surrounding memoria has been flowing towards the other end of the hole at a constant velocity, slowly but surely.

> **科玫：**看见这大空洞了么？很多年前它还只是一道缝隙，现在已经长成了一个大洞。这附近的忆质一直以某个恒定的速度缓慢流向空洞的另一端。

**Kaimee:** But the scary part is... according to my calculations, the flow rate of memoria has recently changed, and it's faster than ever before — it's almost... almost as if something is sucking it in from the other side!

> **科玫：**但可怕的是…根据我的测算，最近忆质涌流的速度开始变化了，并且前所未有地快——简直…简直就像是有什么东西在从大口那边吮吸一样！

**Kaimee:** By constantly improving upon Madam Razalina's memoria measurement method, I've finally obtained accurate results—

> **科玫：**通过不断改进拉扎莉娜女士的忆质测量法，我终于得到了准确结果——

**Kaimee:** After ten system hours, the Dreamflux Reef will cease to exist! It will disintegrate like a melting glacier and then fuse with the sweet dream fragments on that side of the void!

> **科玫：**十个系统时后，流梦礁将不复存在！犹如冰山消融，一切都将土崩瓦解，和空洞那边的美梦碎块融为一体！

**Misha:** Uhh, please don't worry, this sort of thing has happened many times before... Miss Kaimee isn't a bad person, she's just a bit... lost in her own world. She'll probably realize she's wrong soon enough.

> **米沙：**呃，请不要担心，这种事发生过很多次了…科玫小姐人不坏，就是有些…沉浸在另一个世界里。她应该很快就会发现自己算错了。

**Welt:** You don't say... There was something else that piqued my interest. Who is Madam Razalina?

> **瓦尔特：**比起这个，有另一件令我在意的事。请问你口中的「拉扎莉娜女士」是？

**Kaimee:** Oh, do you know her, too? Or are you also a fan of memoria dynamics?

> **科玫：**哦，你也认识她？还是你也喜欢忆质动力学？

**Welt:** We're very interested in Madam Razalina's achievements. Could you tell us a little more about them?

> **瓦尔特：**我们对拉扎莉娜女士的成就很感兴趣，可否展开讲讲？

**Kaimee:** Why, Of course! She's an excellent scholar of memoria dynamics, and the first person to apply memoria rate measurement methodology on interstellar travelers.

> **科玫：**当然！她可是忆质动力学的杰出学者，是第一个将忆质速率测量法应用于星际旅行的人。

**Kaimee:** Regrettably, due to the presence of the Garden of Recollection, ordinary people don't pay much attention to the nature of memoria. She departed this world without much fame, leaving only a few thin journals behind...

> **科玫：**可惜，由于流光忆庭的存在，普通人不怎么关心忆质的性质。最后她籍籍无名地离世，只留下几本薄薄的笔记……

**Kaimee:** I came to Penacony to learn more about my idol, and went to great lengths to seek out Dreamflux Reef, all because this is her final resting place. Prodigies always meet their demise prematurely... If only Madam Razalina had more time, she would've discovered a way to reverse the flow of memoria!

> **科玫：**我慕名前来匹诺康尼，费尽千辛万苦找到流梦礁，只因为这里是她的逝世之地。天妒英才啊…如果拉扎莉娜女士还有时间，她一定能找出逆转忆质流动的方法！

**Kaimee:** I felt it, the source is in... the Golden Hour! There is a certain anomalous presence stirring the currents of the Memory Zone. I must uncover more concrete proof... I must convince everyone...

> **科玫：**我感觉到了，源头就在…「黄金的时刻」！那里有某种异常的存在在搅动忆域的海流。我必须给出更加直观的证明…我一定能说服大家……

**Welt:** ...(Trailblazer), does the name Madam Razalina sound familiar to you?

> **瓦尔特：**…开拓者，还记得「拉扎莉娜」这个名字吗？

- ▸ It was one of the Nameless mentioned by the conductor.

> ▸ 是列车长提到的无名客之一。

**Welt:** That's right. It seems like she did a great deal of research and calculations in Dreamflux Reef... before abruptly passing away.

> **瓦尔特：**没错。看来她在流梦礁进行了大量的研究与测算，而后匆匆离世。

- ▸ Who?

> ▸ 谁？

**Welt:** Before we came to Penacony, the conductor had given us a list of Nameless to look into — her name was mentioned. It seems like she did a great deal of research and calculations in Dreamflux Reef... before abruptly passing away.

> **瓦尔特：**在启程来到匹诺康尼前，列车长拜托我们打听的几位无名客里就有她的名字。看来她在流梦礁进行了大量的研究与测算，而后匆匆离世。

**Misha:** Miss Kaimee regularly mentions her. I hear Madam Razalina passed away during the Prison War... If she could see the Penacony of today, its people building homes in the Memory Zone... I bet she'd be really happy.

> **米沙：**科玫小姐经常提起她。听说拉扎莉娜女士在监狱战争时期便去世了…要是她能见到如今的匹诺康尼，看着大家在忆域里建设家园…一定会非常开心吧。

**Welt:** ...Perhaps.

> **瓦尔特：**…也许吧。
