[Recap] Frightened by the battle, The Family member fainted. Misha explained that the residents of Dreamflux Reef did not see Memory Zone Memes as dangerous monsters, but right now, it was important to get the unconscious Family member to safety.

*(After the battle)*

**Welt:** He passed out. His intense negative emotions attracted the nearby Memory Zone Memes.

> **瓦尔特：**他昏倒了。想必是负面情绪太过强烈，引来了附近的忆域迷因。

**March 7th:** I see... but why aren't the other people around here scared?

> **三月七：**喔…不过，怎么路上的行人都不害怕呢？

**Misha:** Unlike in the sweet dream, people here don't see Memory Zone Memes as dangerous monsters... and even if they pose a threat, people can easily escape by forcing a wake-up call.

> **米沙：**和美梦不一样，在这里大家不把忆域迷因当作危险的怪物…就算真的有危险，也可以通过强制唤醒脱离梦境。

**Misha:** But we can't just leave this man here... Can we take him somewhere safe?

> **米沙：**不过这位先生…不能晾在街上不管，能带他去个安全的地方吗？

### Find Aunt Jessie

> *Misha claims Dreamflux Reef to be his home... If that is the case, you can only imagine how bad the treatment must have been at The Reverie. Although, the cute and naïve Misha has not taken that to heart. In fact, seeing that you are in trouble, he offers to lead you on a search for your lost companions and give you a tour of the desolate town along the way. Not only that, you may even have a chance to meet with an old friend... Robin. You were able to reunite with March 7th safely. However, before you can go in search of Himeko, you will need to find a local to look after this poor soul that was scared unconscious by the pink spirit of Dreamflux Reef. This Aunt Jessie that March 7th mentioned sounds like a solid candidate.*

**March 7th:** We can ask Jessie for help! I've gotten to know many locals while waiting for you guys.

> **三月七：**那就拜托翠丝阿姨吧！等你们的时候，我已经认识了好多当地人。

**March 7th:** Everyone here is living a self-sufficient life... I don't know how to describe it, but this place feels like the real Dreamscape...

> **三月七：**人们过着自给自足的生活，不知该怎么形容，但总觉得这里才像真正的梦中世界……

[Recap] You spoke with Jessie, who mentioned that more and more unfamiliar faces had been showing up in Dreamflux Reef lately, likely a sign of the "sweet dream's collapse."

*(Talk to Aunt Jessie)*

**March 7th:** Evening, Jessie! Uhm... is it evening?

> **三月七：**翠丝阿姨，晚上好！呃…现在算晚上吗？

**Jessie:** Welcome, Miss March! Who might these be?

> **翠丝：**是三月七小姐啊，欢迎。这几位是……

**March 7th:** These two are my friends! As for the man lying on the ground... he's a scaredy-cat who fainted from fright.

> **三月七：**这两人是我的同伴！至于躺地上这位…是个吓晕过去的胆小鬼……

**Jessie:** Haha, I see, another poor guy who accidentally ended up here. I'll take care of him.

> **翠丝：**哈哈，懂了懂了，又一个不当心闯入这里的人，把他留在这吧。

**Jessie:** There have been a lot of new faces lately. Things must be tough in the beautiful dream. *sigh* The few remaining havens of freedom in Asdana will soon face trouble...

> **翠丝：**最近生面孔是越来越多了，美梦中肯定发生了不少事吧。阿斯德纳仅剩的几片自由之地，也要不得安宁了啊……

**Welt:** Do such things often happen here?

> **瓦尔特：**这种事很常见吗？

**Jessie:** Not really, but they're becoming more frequent now. Guess it's one of the signs of the sweet dream's collapse.

> **翠丝：**说常见也算不上，但频率是越来越高了。这也是*美梦崩溃*的征兆之一吧。

**Jessie:** This man has had quite the shock. Could you help me find a Halovian lady, March? Her songs can heal mental wounds.

> **翠丝：**这先生受了不小的冲击，三月七小姐，能帮我去找一位天环族少女么？她的歌声可以治愈精神创伤。

**March 7th:** A Halovian lady...?

> **三月七：**天环族少女…？

**Welt:** That must be Robin. She's also here, in Dreamflux Reef.

> **瓦尔特：**想必说的是知更鸟。此刻，她也在流梦礁。

**March 7th:** Huh!? Robin? But I thought she... Oh, right! If Firefly is here safe and sound, then it means Robin must be okay, too.

> **三月七：**诶？！知更鸟小姐？她不是——对哦，如果流萤姑娘平安无事，知更鸟小姐一定也经历了同样的遭遇。

**Welt:** Misha is about to take us to her to find out what happened, but before that, let's meet up with Himeko. You were with her earlier, right?

> **瓦尔特：**这位米沙正要带我们去见她，弄清个中原委。不过在此之前，我们先和姬子会合吧。你之前应该和她在一起？

**March 7th:** We met some stowaways in the residential area. Most of them came from neighboring star systems.

> **三月七：**我们之前在居住地，遇见了好些偷渡客。他们大多是从邻近星系来的。

**March 7th:** I heard that places like Dreamflux Reef are scattered throughout the Memory Zone of Asdana, like islands in the ocean. They existed before The Family arrived.

> **三月七：**据说像流梦礁这样的地方，在阿斯德纳的忆域中不止一处，就像大海里的小岛，在家族到来前，它们就已经存在了。

**March 7th:** I also heard that when Dreamflux Reef took shape, it was the center of all Dreamscapes in Penacony.

> **三月七：**我还打听到一种说法：据说流梦礁初具规模的时候，这里才是匹诺康尼梦境的中心呢！

**Welt:** If that's true, it's no wonder there are so many similarities between this place and the sweet dream.

> **瓦尔特：**如果这说法属实，美梦与此地的诸多相似之处就说得通了。

**March 7th:** Himeko must be gathering information. Let's hurry up and get going.

> **三月七：**姬子应该还在向那人打听消息，那我们就赶紧出发咯？

### Search for Himeko

> *Misha claims Dreamflux Reef to be his home... If that is the case, you can only imagine how bad the treatment must have been at The Reverie. Although, the cute and naïve Misha has not taken that to heart. In fact, seeing that you are in trouble, he offers to lead you on a search for your lost companions and give you a tour of the desolate town along the way. Not only that, you may even have a chance to meet with an old friend... Robin. Now that the brief episode of the spirit of Dreamflux Reef has been put aside for now... You can finally go in search of Himeko! You must miss her, right?*

*(Talk to Jessie again, optional)*

**Jessie:** There have been a lot of new faces lately. Things must be tough in the beautiful dream. The few remaining havens of freedom in Asdana will soon face trouble...

*(Upon arriving at the marked location)*

**March 7th:** This is where we split up... She can't be too far away!

> **三月七：**我们刚刚就是在这儿分开的…嗯，不远了！

[Recap] You encountered Himeko and Micah, the "Gravekeeper" of Dreamflux Reef. Micah was responsible for looking after Dreamchasers who had wandered into the area, either guiding them back or teaching them how to survive. He invited you to meet an important guest.

*(Approach Micah)*

**Himeko:** So that's how it is... I never imagined we'd gather the remaining details here.

> **姬子：**是这样啊…真没想到会在这里补全剩下的信息。

**Micah:** Hehe... to borrow Gallagher's catchphrase, "what an unpredictable twist of fate."

> **米凯：**呵呵…用加拉赫先生的口头禅来说，就是「天意弄人」吧。

**March 7th:** Himeko! Here they are.

> **三月七：**姬子，我把他们带来了！

**Himeko:** Perfect timing. Now that everyone's here, I'd like to introduce everyone to Micah, who's partly in charge of the land of the exiles. Micah, these are my companions.

> **姬子：**看来人都到齐了啊。正好，见见米凯先生吧，流放之地的负责人之一。米凯先生，他们就是我的同伴。

**Micah:** It's a pleasure to meet the Nameless.

> **米凯：**瓦尔特先生，三月七小姐，开拓者先生/小姐…各位无名客，幸会。

**March 7th:** You know us?

> **三月七：**您认识我们？

**Micah:** I've been keeping an eye on you since the day you arrived in Penacony. We would have met under more appropriate circumstances if Dreamflux Reef hadn't been isolated from the Twelve Dreamscapes.

> **米凯：**从你们踏入匹诺康尼的那天起，我就一直在关注各位的动向。可惜，若非流梦礁与十二梦境早已隔绝，我们本该以更体面的方式相见。

**Micah:** Please allow me to formally introduce myself. I'm Micah, the Gravekeeper of Dreamflux Reef.

> **米凯：**正式认识一下吧，我是流梦礁的「守墓人」，米凯。

**March 7th:** Gravekeeper...?

> **三月七：**守墓人…？

**Micah:** Life in Dreamflux Reef is pretty liberating. Everyone here mostly keeps to themselves, without meddling in others' affairs. My daily task involves... cleaning a few tombstones.

> **米凯：**流梦礁的生活十分自由，也没什么组织可言。大家各过各的，井水不犯河水，我能做的…也只是每天定时打扫几座墓碑罢了。

**Misha:** You're too modest, Micah. When lost Dreamchasers stumble upon this place, you're the one who takes care of them, guiding them back to the sweet dream or showing them how to survive the wild dream chaos.

> **米沙：**米凯先生太谦虚了。每当有迷途的逐梦客被带入这里，一直是米凯先生担起守护者的责任，或是将他们送回美梦，或是教会他们如何在混沌的梦境中生存。

**Welt:** So, a guardian of sorts!

> **瓦尔特：**原来是位大家长啊。

**Micah:** Hmm? Were you talking to me, Mr. Yang?

> **米凯：**嗯？瓦尔特先生，您是在和我说话么？

**Welt:** ...Hmm?

> **瓦尔特：**…嗯？

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Welt:** ...On that note, Mr. Micah, which tombstones are you referring to? We didn't come across any graveyard when we arrived.

> **瓦尔特：**…话说回来，米凯先生，您所说的墓碑是指？我们来时似乎没有看见墓园。

**Micah:** Heh, they're actually just symbolic stones. But since you're curious, Mr. Yang, I'll take you there. I have a feeling you might find something of interest there.

> **米凯：**呵呵，说是坟墓，也只是几座象征意义的衣冠冢罢了。既然瓦尔特先生问起，我们不妨去实地看看。如果我没猜错，各位应该能在那里得到不小的收获。

**Micah:** But before that, you'll have an important guest joining you.

> **米凯：**不过在此之前，还有位重要的客人要与你们一同前往。

### Meet with the special guest

> *When you find Himeko, she is speaking with a local named Micah. Coincidentally, this mild-mannered man who calls themselves the "Gravekeeper" is actually one of the people responsible for this land of Dreamflux. Your appearance in this land doesn't seem to surprise him in the slightest. He says that he would be able to take you to get a deeper understanding of Dreamflux Reef, but you must travel alongside a certain "special guest"... If you are not mistaken, this "guest" is quite likely Miss Robin.*

**March 7th:** An important guest? Who could it be...?

> **三月七：**重要的客人？难道就是……

**Micah:** This way, please. The roads here in Dreamflux Reef are a bit run-down, so watch your step.

> **米凯：**这边走，各位。流梦礁的道路年久失修，注意脚下。

*(Upon arriving at the marked location)*

**Micah:** There she is.

> **米凯：**就是她了。

**March 7th:** It really is her... Miss Robin!

> **三月七：**那真的是…知更鸟小姐！

[Recap] Robin was teaching children to sing and discussing the meaning of dreams with Grace. No matter how long one flies through the dream, they will one day return to reality. And if their wings are broken, then they should lend each other their wings.

*(Approach Robin)*

**Robin:** Everyone sang so wonderfully! It's not often that I try this music style, but I've gained some valuable insights from it!

> **知更鸟：**大家唱得真棒，我很少尝试这种曲风，也学到了很多！

**Grace:** I can't thank you enough, Robin. These kids have made incredible progress in only a few days.

> **格莉莎：**真不知道该如何感谢你，知更鸟小姐，才短短几天，孩子们的进步就这么大了。

**Robin:** It was nothing, Grace. I merely taught them how to sing. It was you who brought hope into their lives.

> **知更鸟：**格莉莎女士，我只是教会了他们如何发声，但教会他们对生活怀抱希望的人，是您。

**Robin:** ...Life must be quite difficult for them in reality, I imagine.

> **知更鸟：**…现实里的日子一定很辛苦吧？

**Grace:** That's right.

> **格莉莎：**你看出来了啊。

**Robin:** Whenever it's time to say goodbye to these kids, they're reluctant to leave. But, I've explored every corner of Dreamflux Reef, talked to everyone I met, and they all told me the same thing... This shattered dream is not worth clinging to.

> **知更鸟：**每次告别时，孩子们都对这片流梦之地依依不舍。可我走遍了流梦礁的每一处角落，探访了每一个人，他们都告诉我这场破碎的梦…不值得留恋。

**Grace:** Hehe, it seems you truly are a child of the Harmony.

> **格莉莎：**呵呵，不愧是众望所归的谐乐之子。

**Grace:** Emma and Andy are orphans I took under my wing. Carol, there, with her blind eyes, used to work at a nutrition center in the outer ring of Penacony. And as for Gary, he's been living with autism since he was a child. They're not old enough to enter the sweet dream managed by The Family.

> **格莉莎：**爱玛和安迪都是我收养的孤儿，双目失明的卡萝在匹诺康尼外环的营养房做工，加里从小便患有孤独症。他们尚未成年，也无法去往家族的美梦中。

**Grace:** If we compare people to birds, these kids are like fledglings with impaired wings. But in this dream, they can fly freely. Even if they stumble along the way, they're still relying on their own strength.

> **格莉莎：**如果把人比作鸟儿，这些孩子都是生来羽翼残缺的雏鸟。但在这片梦里，他们能获得完整的翅膀。尽管飞得跌跌撞撞，但也是在凭借自己的力量翱翔。

**Grace:** And me? An old lady with no legs... Without this dream, I couldn't even walk toward them.

> **格莉莎：**至于我，一个失去了双腿的老人…要是没有这片梦境，我甚至都无法走到他们面前。

**Robin:** I'm glad that you found a new life here in Penacony, it's just...

> **知更鸟：**很高兴你们在匹诺康尼的梦中开始了新的生活。只是……

**Grace:** Don't worry, Robin. Dreams have their significance, but they aren't everything. Both the children and I understand this. No matter how long we fly through this dream, we will one day return to reality.

> **格莉莎：**不用担心，知更鸟小姐。梦有意义，但它不是一切，我和孩子们都明白这点，无论在梦里飞翔多久，最后总要飞回现实。

**Grace:** But you know what? Emma and Gary aren't plagued by their insecurities anymore. No — and Carol is learning how to cope with her blindness. And Andy is livelier than ever. Well, even I've become more optimistic.

> **格莉莎：**但是你看，现在爱玛和加里不再自卑，卡萝逐渐懂得如何面对失明的困扰，安迪比从前活泼了许多，甚至连我都变得更加乐观了。

**Grace:** You see, in dreams, we learn how to live. Once we return to reality... we learn how to survive.

> **格莉莎：**我们在梦里学会生活，然后回到现实…学会生存。

**Robin:** ...And should our feathers be damaged, then we share our wings with one another.

> **知更鸟：**…如果羽翼不幸残缺，那就把翅膀借给彼此。

**Robin:** There's no need to covet an illusory sky in dreams, because we have the right and the ability... to fly towards a broader horizon.

> **知更鸟：**不必贪恋梦中虚幻的天空，因为我们有权利，也有能力…飞向更广阔的天地。

[Recap] Robin said her loss of voice was one of the signs of the sweet dream's collapse, and that a traitor to the Harmony had appeared within The Family. Until the truth came to light, Robin was willing to give up her chance to perform on the stage of the Charmony Festival.

**Himeko:** It's a relief to see you safe and sound, Miss Robin.

> **姬子：**很高兴看见你平安无事，知更鸟小姐。

**Robin:** It's nice to see you all again, Astral Express crew. I heard my disappearance caused quite the commotion out there... I'm really sorry about that.

> **知更鸟：**星穹列车的各位，又见面了。听说我的失踪在外界引发了不小的骚乱…非常抱歉。

**Himeko:** Since you're here... can we assume that you're fully aware of the situation in Penacony?

> **姬子：**既然你身在此处…我们可否认为，知更鸟小姐已经充分知晓了匹诺康尼的现状？

**Robin:** Ever since I returned to Penacony, my voice started to change, until it gradually faded away. At first, I thought it was a temporary ailment, perhaps due to having been away too long. I thought maybe it'd just take some time for my body to acclimate to the high concentration of memoria in Asdana.

> **知更鸟：**自回到匹诺康尼起，我的嗓音就变得异常，逐步演变为失声的折磨。我本以为只是场意外，也许是在外旅居久了，不习惯阿斯德纳高浓度的忆质环境。

**Robin:** But now it seems... the root of the problem goes way beyond me. There are elements around me that don't align with the Harmony... and losing my voice is just one of the signs of the sweet dream's collapse.

> **知更鸟：**但现在看来…源头并不在我。我的身边存在着与「同谐」不合的事物…失声也是美梦正在崩溃的信号之一。

**March 7th:** The sweet dream's collapse... that Memokeeper mentioned the same thing. So, it's real.

> **三月七：**美梦崩溃…那个忆者也说过同样的话，原来是真的啊。

**Robin:** While I was away from Penacony, the boundaries of the Twelve Dreamscapes kept expanding outward. But whenever I mentioned the anomalies in the dream... all The Family heads refused to talk about it. Only my brother was willing to respond...

> **知更鸟：**在我离开匹诺康尼的这段时间，十二梦境的边界不断向外扩张。可每当我谈及梦中的异象，却总能感受到家主们三缄其口，只有兄长愿意解答……

**Robin:** Later, I discovered the secret letters from the IPC ambassador, which further convinced me that there are hidden secrets beneath the surface of Penacony. So, following the clues in the Oak Family's dossiers, I found my way here...

> **知更鸟：**之后公司的使节暗中投来密信，更让我确信匹诺康尼的光芒下潜伏着某种不可告人的秘密。最后，我通过橡木家系卷宗中的几条线索，找到了这里……

**Robin:** ...The land of the exiles, concealed by The Family under the guise of "Death," a dream within a dream where Penacony's past is buried.

> **知更鸟：**…被家族用「死亡」名义掩盖的流放之地，埋藏了匹诺康尼过往的梦中之梦。

**Himeko:** Hearing you speak, it sounds as if your voice has made somewhat of a recovery.

> **姬子：**现在听来，知更鸟小姐的嗓音似乎有所恢复？

**Robin:** I hate to admit it, but the "Harmony" in this place resonates more broadly than within the sweet dream...

> **知更鸟：**虽然残酷，但这是无可争辩的事实，这里的「同谐」反而比美梦中传扬更广……

**Robin:** It's regrettable, but The Family has experienced betrayal. The traitor, or traitors abandoned their original principles and, using the name of Harmony, exploited people's weaknesses to turn Penacony into the Planet of Festivities, trapping everyone in the illusion of the sweet dream...

> **知更鸟：**我很遗憾，是家族出现了背叛者，他…或者他们，舍弃了最初的信念，以「同谐」之名利用人性的弱点，将匹诺康尼变成了沉沦于虚幻美梦的「盛会之星」……

**Robin:** This is not "the strong defending the weak," but rather "the strong exploiting the weak." A world without equality won't ever be favored by the Harmony, and naturally, those voices blessed by THEM... have lost the ability to sing.

> **知更鸟：**这根本不是「以强援弱」，而是「以强制弱」。一个失去了平等的世界注定不会再受「同谐」眷顾，受祂赐福的声音…自然也无法歌唱了。

**Welt:** Could there be another force influencing The Family's shift in philosophy, Miss Robin?

> **瓦尔特：**知更鸟小姐，是否存在另一种可能——家族理念的变化，是有另一股势力参与其中？

**Welt:** Considering what happened with Acheron, it's difficult to conceive of another entity within the realm of the Harmony capable of influencing everyone, unless a power surpassing that of an Emanator is involved...

> **瓦尔特：**毕竟从黄泉小姐的例子来看，除非有令使以上的力量介入…否则很难想象在「同谐」的属地，会存在另一种能够影响所有人的意志。

**Robin:** I'd heard about what happened to the Xianzhou Alliance. But as far as I'm aware, The Family hasn't faced any such external interventions. Who knows, perhaps I've just been away too long and missed something.

> **知更鸟：**仙舟联盟的遭遇我也有所耳闻。但就我所知，不存在外部势力干预家族的情况。也可能是我离乡太久，有太多看不见的地方。

**Robin:** Regardless, I cannot accept my home is moving towards the very opposite of what the Harmony represents, while still claiming to uphold it.

> **知更鸟：**无论如何，我不能接受自己的故乡以「同谐」的名义走向「同谐」的反面。

**Robin:** I must uncover the reason why Mikhail cut ties with The Family, and who exactly it was who betrayed us all. Do you remember our arrangement, Mr. Micah? Well, here's my answer...

> **知更鸟：**为了弄清「钟表匠」米哈伊尔为何会与家族决裂，又究竟是谁做出了背叛的决定…米凯先生，还记得我们的交易吗？现在是我作出答复的时候了——

**Robin:**— I've decided to forego my role, and never step foot on the Charmony Festival's stage again.

> **知更鸟：**——我愿意放弃，不再登上「谐乐大典」的舞台。

## 072　The Public Enemy　国民公敌

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Dreamflux Reef　|　角色：Trailblazer; Gallagher; March 7th; Himeko; Welt; Firefly; Misha; Micah; Sunday; Robin　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Small Town Grotesque'' completed

[Recap] A young Sunday and Robin once found a wounded bird that had fallen from the sky. Robin believed that birds belonged to the sky and should not be caged, but Sunday wished for the bird to survive no matter what.

11 system hours until Charmony Festival - The other side of Dreamflux Reef

**Robin:** Look here, brother! A little bird!

> **知更鸟：**哥哥，你看！这里有一只小鸟。

**Sunday:** Looks like a fledgling Charmony Dove.

> **星期日：**看起来是只小谐乐鸽。

**Sunday:** But Charmony Doves don't live here. So how did this little bird get here? Maybe its parents abandoned it?

> **星期日：**可附近不是谐乐鸽的栖息地，为什么它会孤零零地在这里？是被父母遗弃了吗？

**Robin:** It looks weak and frail... Why don't we find something soft and make a nest for it?

> **知更鸟：**它看起来一点力气都没有…我们去找个软垫，给它做个鸟巢吧？

**Sunday:** This place is too dangerous for a fledgling. Let's take it with us — we can put it on the wooden shelf in front of your window.

> **星期日：**这里太危险了。我们还是先带它回去吧，就放在你窗前的木架上。

**Robin:** Okay! A bird like that must have a beautiful singing voice. But where will it live?

> **知更鸟：**好呀，它歌唱的声音肯定很动听。不过，它要住在哪里呢？

**Sunday:** I'll ask the family head to build a cage for it.

> **星期日：**我会去拜托家主，给它造个鸟笼。

**Robin:** A cage... but then it won't have the freedom to fly, right?

> **知更鸟：**笼子…那它不就没办法自由飞翔了吗？

**Kind Voice:** Let's see... what is it that has captured the attention of the two best interpreters of the Great One, to the point that they've forgotten how to enjoy their dessert?

> **温和的声音：**让我瞧瞧…是什么让神主最优秀的两位诠释者如此好奇，竟然连餐后甜点都忘了品尝？

**Kind Voice:** Oh... poor little thing. Doesn't look like it's doing well. Do you want to rescue it?

> **温和的声音：**哦…可怜的小东西，情况恐怕不乐观。你们想救它吗？

**Robin:** I do... but I don't want to lock it up in a cage.

> **知更鸟：**想…但我觉得不能把它关起来。

**Kind Voice:** Why?

> **温和的声音：**为什么？

**Robin:** Even if it's small and not fully feathered, and can't sing... it didn't come into this world just to be locked up in a cage.

> **知更鸟：**虽然它还小，连羽毛都没长齐，也不会唱歌…可它不是为了在笼子里生活才破壳而出的。

**Robin:** Birds... belong to the sky.

> **知更鸟：**鸟儿…生来就属于天空。

**Kind Voice:** That's quite the romantic idea. And what about you, our young scholar? Do you agree with your sister?

> **温和的声音：**很浪漫的想法。那你呢，小博士，你认可妹妹的观点吗？

**Sunday:** I think she's right... but if we leave it out in the wild, it won't survive for more than a few days at best.

> **星期日：**我觉得妹妹说得对…但如果我们把它留在野外，它过不了几天就会死的。

**Kind Voice:** Ah, I see. It seems our little scholar is still a bit unsure. Well, let me tell you youngsters a story.

> **温和的声音：**呵呵，看来我们的小博士还有些迷茫。这样吧，孩子们，我来为你们讲一个故事。

**Kind Voice:** As you probably know, Charmony Doves can fly through the air. When they fly really high, the friction caused by the flapping of their feathers against the atmosphere creates amazing lights, so that they look like shooting stars.

> **温和的声音：**你们应该知道，谐乐鸽是一种能够飞越大气层的鸟类。在高空飞行时，它们的羽毛会因为摩擦产生的高温发出壮丽的光芒，就像是一道道流星。

**Kind Voice:** We've seen this spectacle so many times that we think it's just something they can naturally do... but that's not the truth. Their radiant display is the result of countless struggles against nature over generations.

> **温和的声音：**我们对这种美丽的风景屡见不鲜，以为那是它们与生俱来的本领…可惜，那流星般的光彩，是它们同大自然斗争了数百个琥珀纪的结果。

**Kind Voice:** Their ancestors were too weak to survive on the ground. So, to escape predators, they started seeking new opportunities in the air...

> **温和的声音：**谐乐鸽的祖先太过弱小，无法在地面的生存斗争中胜出。为了免于被捕猎的命运，它们开始仰望天空，振翅跃起……

**Kind Voice:** After countless attempts by many generations, one of them finally figured out how to fly. It soared into the sky and never looked back at the ground again.

> **温和的声音：**就这样，经过数千代、数万代的尝试，族群中的一只鸟儿终于学会了如何正确地挥动翅膀。它成功飞起，从此告别了大地。

**Robin:** So, you mean, birds aren't born to fly, but they find a way to do it through their determination, right?

> **知更鸟：**所以，其实鸟儿天性是不会飞的，但它们靠自身的意志做到了，对吗？

**Kind Voice:** Well, that's an idealistic way of putting it. So, what are your thoughts, Sunday?

> **温和的声音：**呵呵，这确实是一种理想主义的解读。那么，星期日，你又是怎么想的？

**Sunday:** I...

> **星期日：**我……

**Sunday:** I think people believe birds are meant to fly because... they've never seen those birds crashing to their death.

> **星期日：**我觉得，人们之所以会认为飞翔是鸟类的天性…是因为他们从没见过坠亡的鸟儿。

**Kind Voice:** That's an interesting perspective. So, have you decided what to do with the bird now?

> **温和的声音：**这个思路也很有趣。那你现在应该想明白如何对待这只小鸟了吧？

**Sunday:** For now... I'll keep it in a cage until it can take care of itself because...

> **星期日：**我会…嗯，先将它放在笼子里，至少在它能独自活下去前先这样。因为……

**Sunday:** I... I want it to live, no matter what.

> **星期日：**我…无论如何都希望它能活着。

**Kind Voice:** Well said, kids. It seems each of you has found your own answer. Your insights are truly remarkable, and I hope they come true in their own way.

> **温和的声音：**很好，孩子们，看来你们心里已经各有答案了。你们的愿景无比美满，我衷心企盼它们能以各自的方式实现。

**Robin:** We will take good care of it, won't we, brother?

> **知更鸟：**我们会好好照料它的。对吧，哥哥？

**Sunday:** Yeah, but Mr. Gopher Wood, there's one thing I don't quite understand...

> **星期日：**嗯。不过，歌斐木先生，我还有一件事没想明白……

**Kind Voice:** And what might that be, my son?

> **温和的声音：**说吧，孩子。

**Sunday:** What if this little Charmony Dove never learns to fly in the end?

> **星期日：**如果这只小谐乐鸽到最后都学不会飞，该怎么办？

**Sunday:** I mean, if there are fledglings in this world that can never fly throughout their lives...

> **星期日：**我是说，如果这世上确实有些雏鸟，终其一生都无法飞翔……

**Sunday:** Should we let them go back to the sky... only to see them crash to the ground and die?

> **星期日：**那我们还应该让它们回到天空…再眼睁睁看着它们坠亡在地吗？

...

**Gallagher:** Talking in your sleep, birdie? Time to wake up.

> **加拉赫：**梦话说得不错，小鸟，该醒了。

[Recap] Gallagher brought Sunday to Dreamflux Reef and proposed that they work together to find the traitor within The Family. Firefly arrived shortly after, and Gallagher said she could lead Sunday to Robin.

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Gallagher:** You look a little unsteady. Need a hand?

> **加拉赫：**怎么，站不稳，要我搀你一把？

**Sunday:** I'm still alive?

> **星期日：**我没死？

**Gallagher:** Yeah, happy about that?

> **加拉赫：**哼，开心吗？

**Sunday:** ...Where is Robin? Tell me. Now.

> **星期日：**…告诉我知更鸟在哪里。现在。

**Gallagher:** Heh, I knew that was going to be the first thing you'd ask.

> **加拉赫：**呵，我就知道你第一反应肯定是她。

**Gallagher:** Don't worry. Your sister is here, safe and sound. She's... probably walking around the streets now.

> **加拉赫：**她就在这地方，不用担心，你妹妹很安全，现在…估计还在街巷里走访吧。

**Gallagher:** If I were you, I'd be more worried about myself... After all, right in front of you is the guy who just stabbed you in the chest with a dagger.

> **加拉赫：**如果我是你，在这个节骨眼上会更关心自己…毕竟面前可站着个刚给你胸口来了一刀的混蛋。

**Sunday:** If you wanted to kill me, you wouldn't give me the chance to speak... Just tell me your demands, lackey of the Watchmaker.

> **星期日：**如果你真打算下死手，倒也不必给我寒暄的机会…说出你的来意，「钟表匠」的走狗。

**Gallagher:** So, you've figured out who I am, huh? No wonder you had the guts to go against the Dreammaster and the four Families. Looks like I made the right choice.

> **加拉赫：**哼，这都被你看出来了？难怪你敢跟梦主和四大家系对着干，看来我的选择是正确的。

**Sunday:** Choice?

> **星期日：**选择？

**Gallagher:** You are aware of my plan and see through my Fictionology. Time is running out, so let's drop the charades...

> **加拉赫：**你知道我要做什么。我的「虚构」也已被你看穿，不剩多少时日了，没必要再虚与委蛇……

**Gallagher:** I'm suggesting we cooperate.

> **加拉赫：**我想和你合作。

**Sunday:** Cooperate? What makes you think I'd cooperate with you?

> **星期日：**你凭什么觉得能和我合作？

**Gallagher:** The fact that the famous Robin has chosen my side. Plus, some clues about a traitor, and a bright future for Penacony... Any of that catch your interest?

> **加拉赫：**就凭那位大名鼎鼎的知更鸟也选择站在这边。再加上一位叛徒的线索，和匹诺康尼的光明未来——这样的特饮能满足你的胃口么？

**Sunday:** I find it hard to believe a man who's full of deception.

> **星期日：**我难以相信一个全身上下满是谎言的人，嘴里能有几句真话。

**Gallagher:** Fine, you don't have to trust me. What you should trust is...

> **加拉赫：**没事，你不用相信我，你该相信的……

**Gallagher:** The sense of justice inside of you.

> **加拉赫：**是你心底里的公义。

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Sunday:** ...Show me Robin first.

> **星期日：**…先让我见到知更鸟。

**Gallagher:** Alright, as you wish. Here she is...

> **加拉赫：**好啊。如你所愿，她来了……

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Sunday:** What's your trick this time?

> **星期日：**你又在耍什么花招？

**Gallagher:** Haha, just kidding. I mean, this lady will lead us to Robin, right?

> **加拉赫：**哈哈，开个玩笑。我是说，这位小姐会带我们去见知更鸟的，对吧？

**Firefly:** And the Crew too. There are too many people who you owe... an explanation.

> **流萤：**当然，还有星穹列车和我。需要你给出解释的人…太多了。

**Gallagher:** Haha, that'd be great. Please follow me, honorable Oak Family Head. Now all the actors... are on the stage.

> **加拉赫：**哈哈，那可真是太好了。这边请，尊贵的橡木家主。这下所有的演员…就都到齐了。

[Recap] Micah led the group to the graves of Razalina and Tiernan, recounting how the two Nameless had sacrificed themselves during the "trailblazing" of Penacony. Firefly then arrived with Gallagher and Sunday.

**Welt:** ...

> **瓦尔特：**……

**Micah:** This is the monument I mentioned earlier. The names inscribed on it... should be familiar to all of you.

> **米凯：**这就是我之前所说的纪念碑，上面的名字…各位应该不陌生。

**Welt:** Razalina and... Tiernan.

> **瓦尔特：**「拉扎莉娜」，还有…「铁尔南」。

**Micah:** When Penacony was known as a frontier prison, it was the Trailblazers who connected it with the stars. They were the heroes who saved Asdana, and their names deserve to be immortalized — not just on this small stone tablet, but in the annals of history for all of time.

> **米凯：**在匹诺康尼还被叫做「边陲监狱」的年代，是「开拓」将它和群星相连。他们都是拯救了阿斯德纳的英雄，名字理应被刻在岁月的丰碑，而不是这小小的石头上。

**Micah:** However, today the Planet of Festivities is nothing but sweet dreams. That heavy piece of history is all a distant memory... just like that prison.

> **米凯：**然而现在，盛会之星只剩下了美梦，沉重的历史和那座监牢一样…早已无迹可寻了。

**Robin:** If their names are inscribed here, then that means...

> **知更鸟：**他们的名字被刻在碑面上，这也就意味着……

**Himeko:** According to Micah, they died long ago.

> **姬子：**据米凯先生所说，他们在很久以前就去世了。

**Micah:** Razalina was killed during the War of Independence. She ventured alone into the heart of the star system to investigate the flow of memoria, but she never returned...

> **米凯：**拉扎莉娜是在独立战争中牺牲的。她为了弄清楚忆质的流向，独自驾驶穿梭机深入星系中心，再也没有回来……

**Micah:** Tiernan was a skilled gunslinger, strong and reliable. He led the people through countless battles, but he didn't live long enough to witness the arrival of true peace.

> **米凯：**铁尔南则是一位善用枪械的牛仔，强大可靠，他带领人们挺过了惨烈的对外战争，却没能坚持到和平真正来临的那天。

**Micah:** In the decade following the War, Penacony faced challenges internal and external. To protect Asdana, Tiernan picked up the way of the Trailblaze again, and led the Lampmoth Family to explore beyond the system, only to be surrounded... and wiped out by the Swarm.

> **米凯：**战后的十年，匹诺康尼深陷内忧外患。为了阿斯德纳，铁尔南重拾「开拓」之道，带领灯蛾家系向星系外探索，却惨遭虫群包围…全军覆没。

**March 7th:** Though I had expected as much, the tales of these heroes... truly are sorrowful.

> **三月七：**虽然心里早有预期，但前辈们的故事…还是很令人遗憾啊。

**Himeko:** True to the title of Trailblazer, they spent their lives venturing into the unknown. But... what about this tablet? There are no names carved on it.

> **姬子：**他们的一生都走在迈向未知的进行时上，无愧于「开拓」之名。但…这块没有字的纪念碑又是…？

**Gallagher:** When Dreamflux Reef was created, its owner was still alive. However, he insisted on erecting a monument for himself, saying that "it will happen someday."

> **加拉赫：**在流梦礁诞生时，它的主人还没过世。但那人说着「总得有这么一天」，硬是给自己立了块无字碑。

**Gallagher:** Here we meet again, everyone from the Astral Express.

> **加拉赫：**星穹列车的各位，又见面了。

**Sunday:** Robin...

> **星期日：**知更鸟……

### Ask Gallagher about the situation

> *You originally thought you would just be meeting with the Express Crew, but the group you have now amassed is massive... Gallagher, Micah, Misha, Firefly, Robin, Sunday... And it seems that the person who led to the creation of this lively scene was precisely the seemingly indifferent Gallagher himself. If this isn't just because he's lonely and wants to spend some time with everyone, then he must be secretly formulating some kind of shocking conspiracy... Fortunately, he doesn't seem to bear you any ill will. Perhaps you will be able to grasp the full picture of your surrounding situation peacefully.*

*(Talk to Robin and Sunday, optional)*

**Robin:** Brother...!

> **知更鸟：**哥哥…！

**Sunday:** ...There's no need for words. You're safe, and that's all that matters.

> **星期日：**…不用多说，你没事就好。

*(Talk to Micah, optional)*

**Micah:** Well I did my job — I gathered everyone here. Gallagher will explain the rest to you... Take care.

> **米凯：**人我带来了，剩下的部分，就由加拉赫亲自向你们诉说吧…各位，保重。

*(Talk to Misha, optional)*

**Misha:** Huh? The atmosphere sure has livened up with all these people in here...

> **米沙：**咦？突然变得热闹起来了，有好多人……

*(Talk to Firefly, optional)*

**Firefly:** I brought Gallagher here. It's time... to face the "truth."

> **流萤：**我把加拉赫带来了，是时候…面对「真相」了。

[Recap] Gallagher revealed that he was the Watchmaker's assistant and the true sender of the invitations. He had invited the various factions because the current "Planet of Festivities" was the Stellaron Disaster of the Asdana system.

*(Talk to Gallagher)*

**Gallagher:** I promised to give the siblings some privacy, so let's talk about our business first. What do you say?

> **加拉赫：**我答应了给那对兄妹一点私人空间，先聊咱们的事吧，各位意下如何？

**Welt:** That sounds sensible enough. Since you went through the trouble of gathering The Family head, the Crew, and the Stellaron Hunters, I'm guessing you have something important to say, Mr. Gallagher?

> **瓦尔特：**无妨。不过加拉赫先生费尽心思将家族话事人、星穹列车，和星核猎手汇聚一处，想来是有重要的事要交代吧？

**Gallagher:** Oh, is it that obvious?

> **加拉赫：**嚯，连你们也看出来了？

**March 7th:** The look on your face is practically screaming "I'm the one behind all this..."

> **三月七：**你都快把幕后黑手四个字写在脸上啦……

**Gallagher:** Hahaha, you're right, Mr. Yang. It is indeed time to come clean on everything.

> **加拉赫：**哈哈哈，瓦尔特先生说的不错，确实到了该开诚布公的时候。

**Gallagher:** The siblings already know what they need to, and they've made their choice. But you Nameless arrived a bit late, so it's only fair that I answer your questions.

> **加拉赫：**那对兄妹该知道的都知道了，怎么选他们心里有数。各位无名客到得比较晚，我理应花些时间为各位答疑解惑。

**Gallagher:** Before we begin, let me reintroduce myself. I'm the founder of Dreamflux Reef, the deputy of the Watchmaker, and... the one who sent out that invitation...

> **加拉赫：**在开始前，重新做个自我介绍吧——流梦礁的建立者、「钟表匠」的副手，同时也是寄出那份邀请函的人……

**Gallagher:** As Gallagher the History Fictionologist, I humbly extend my greetings to you all.

> **加拉赫：**「虚构史学家」加拉赫，向各位致意。

**March 7th:** History Fictionologist? So, what? everything you told us was made up?

> **三月七：**好你个虚构史学家，合着之前跟咱们说了那么多，都是编的呗？

**Gallagher:** Well, don't worry. Almost everything I shared was true... well, except for the part about "The Family accepting me back."

> **加拉赫：**这点我可以打包票，先前告诉各位的故事全都是真的…呃，大部分吧，除了「家族重新接纳了我」那段。

**Himeko:** I double-checked with Micah, and everything he said about The Family, the Watchmaker, and Mikhail is true.

> **姬子：**我向米凯先生确认过，有关家族、「钟表匠」和那位米哈伊尔的事迹，都核验无误。

**Gallagher:** Thank you for your understanding! Now, let's get down to business. I'm sure you're all wondering why I went through the trouble of setting up this battle for the legacy, inviting different factions, and stirring up a ruckus all over Penacony...

> **加拉赫：**理解万岁，那咱们就能敞开聊了。想必各位一定很想知道，我为何要大费周章地布置一场遗产争夺战，向这么多派系发出邀请，把匹诺康尼搅得鸡犬不宁……

**Gallagher:** Well, it all boils down to something very familiar to all of you... the Stellaron.

> **加拉赫：**答案其实很简单，各位也非常熟悉，一切的根源…都是「星核」。

**Welt:** The Stellaron? But how is that possible? Penacony is a free-flowing interstellar hub. There are no signs of contamination whatsoever.

> **瓦尔特：**星核？匹诺康尼畅通无阻，是四通八达的星际枢纽，看起来也不存在任何遭受污染的迹象，怎会和星核有关？

**Gallagher:** You're totally correct. So, care to take a guess at what that means?

> **加拉赫：**你说的完全正确，所以不妨猜猜看，这意味着什么吧？

- ▸ The Stellaron has been completely sealed?

> ▸ 星核已经被封印了？

**Gallagher:** I wish that were the truth! But, if that were the case, I wouldn't have invited all of you.

> **加拉赫：**我就当这是个美好的愿景吧。若果真如此，我也不必向各位发出邀请了。

- ▸ Someone is manipulating the Stellaron?

> ▸ 有人在操纵星核？

**Gallagher:** How keen! Well, what should I say? I expected nothing less from the person here who is the most familiar with Stellarons.

> **加拉赫：**很敏锐啊。该怎么说，不愧是在场对星核最熟悉的人？

- ▸ You're pulling our leg again!

> ▸ 你又在扯谎了！

**Gallagher:** The sweet dream doesn't come out of thin air. If you think of the Memory Zone as the sea, creating the land of the dreams is like filling that wild ocean with earth to make an island.

> **加拉赫：**美梦可不是凭空变出来的。如果把忆域比作大海，建立一片梦想之地就是在汹涌的汪洋中填土造陆。

**Gallagher:** To achieve this feat, without the help of an Emanator of Remembrance or Enigmata, the only way is to use a Stellaron.

> **加拉赫：**要实现这一壮举，若非「记忆」或「神秘」的令使出手，就只有使用星核一条路。

**Gallagher:** And that's not something you can achieve with a simple wish. It requires vast quantities of knowledge, time, and manpower. I'm sure you get what I'm hinting at...

> **加拉赫：**而且，这可不是简单许个愿就能搞定的事，必须具备相当程度的知识，加上大量时间和人力，才能做得这么滴水不漏。话说到这份上，你们该明白了吧……

**Gallagher:** In Asdana, the Planet of Festivities itself is a Stellaron disaster.

> **加拉赫：**如今的「盛会之星」，就是阿斯德纳的星核之灾啊。

**March 7th:** The Planet of Festivities itself... is a Stellaron disaster?

> **三月七：**盛会之星…就是星核之灾？

**Gallagher:** It all started a long time ago. Back when the Watchmaker and his crew liberated the frontier prison, they faced countless challenges as they began building Penacony from the ground up. That's when the idea of using the Stellaron came into play.

> **加拉赫：**故事要从很久以前说起，「钟表匠」一行解放了边陲监狱后，对如何从无到有建设匹诺康尼一筹莫展，内忧外患也没完没了。这时便有人打起了星核的主意。

**Gallagher:** The Stellaron first entered the Asdana system during the war. The Nameless warned everyone against the folly of attempting to tamper with such a power, and most heeded their words. But, there are always people in the shadows with ulterior motives.

> **加拉赫：**这颗星核本是战争年代落在阿斯德纳的，当时在无名客的呼吁下，人们打消了沾染这种力量的念头。但一直有别有用心之人在暗中蠢蠢欲动。

**Gallagher:** The turning point came after Tiernan's death. With two of the Nameless gone, the Watchmaker had to go to the front lines. It was at that moment his rivals saw an opportunity.

> **加拉赫：**一切的转折点发生在铁尔南牺牲后。两位无名客同伴先后离世，令「钟表匠」不得不奔赴拓荒一线。而这次远行，让他的对手抓住了机会。

**Gallagher:** By the time a representative from the Montour system's Family arrived at the Watchmaker's call, the Stellaron had already been activated and was seeping into the primordial Synesthesia Dreamscape.

> **加拉赫：**等到蒙托尔星系的家族代表响应「钟表匠」的号召前来时，星核早已被人激活，渗入了原始的联觉梦境中。

**Himeko:** And I suppose The Family happened to possess the knowledge to seal the Stellaron?

> **姬子：**我猜，家族恰好掌握着封印星核的知识？

**Gallagher:** Not just that! They knew far more about the Stellaron than the average person. They helped Mikhail swiftly quell the civil unrest and played a part in building Penacony, under the disguise of the Harmony.

> **加拉赫：**何止！他们对星核的了解比常人更甚，迅速帮米哈伊尔平息了内乱，又以「同谐」的名义加入到匹诺康尼的建设中。

**Gallagher:** Those three eras were known as the Age of Dreaming. The Watchmaker, who had been left in the dark, sent out invitations across the universe, spreading the hype around the land of the dreams.

> **加拉赫：**那是被称作「逐梦时代」的三纪，被蒙在鼓里的「钟表匠」向全宇宙发出邀请，掀起了名为梦想之地的热潮。

**March 7th:** Then... how did they turn against each other?

> **三月七：**那后来…他们又是怎么反目成仇的？

**Gallagher:** Remember the island-in-the-ocean metaphor? The truth is, the Stellaron was never truly sealed. It existed in a different form within the Dreamscape. Think about this — what would it cost to create and maintain such a lavish dreamland?

> **加拉赫：**还记得那个「填海造陆」的比喻吧？真相是星核从来没有被封印，只是换了种形式存在于梦中。好好想想，要构筑并维持如此庞大的「美梦」，代价是什么？

**Gallagher:** It's people's lives. The opulent dream is built upon the decay of spirits, with a toxic elixir called "pleasure" flowing through the Dreamscape. It tempts people to indulge in the Dreamscape, and gradually their minds succumb, becoming nourishment for the sweet dream.

> **加拉赫：**是生命，小姑娘。富丽堂皇的美梦建立于精神的死亡之上，名为「快乐」的毒酒淌过梦境，令人们沉溺其中，心智缓缓流向同一个终点，最终变成美梦的胎盘。

**Gallagher:** Confusion, laziness, and cowardice... weaknesses that plague humanity were magnified and nourished by The Family. Penacony became a new kind of prison, even more impenetrable than the previous one.

> **加拉赫：**迷茫、怠惰、懦弱…这些人性中随处可见的弱点，被家族放大、滋养，将匹诺康尼变成了另一种监狱，并且远比过去那座更坚不可摧。

**Gallagher:** Sadly, we realized this far too late. By then, The Family had a firm grip on Penacony, swiftly quelling any opposition that arose.

> **加拉赫：**我们发现得太晚了。那时家族已经手眼通天，反对者很快遭到了控制和驱逐。

**Gallagher:** At my wit's end, I had to use the power of the Enigmata and sought refuge in this chaotic Memory Zone. Over the years, I fabricated a meme within this dream for our use...

> **加拉赫：**走投无路，我只得借助「神秘」的力量躲入这片混沌的忆域，又耗费数年时间在梦中「虚构」出一只迷因，为我们所用——

**Gallagher:** "Dormancy," that's its real name. We exploited a loophole — you see, regular people can't fall asleep again while they are inside the Dreamscape.

> **加拉赫：**「沉眠」，这就是它真正的名字。常人无法在梦境中再度沉睡，我们才有机会钻这个空子。

**Himeko:** So, this is the true meaning of "the impossible." You sent out invitations in the Watchmaker's name to find forces capable of resolving the Stellaron disaster and draw them in to Penacony to uncover the truth?

> **姬子：**原来这就是「不可能之事」的真正含义。所以，你以「钟表匠」的名义发出邀请函，是为了找到能够解决星核危机的派系，吸引他们前来匹诺康尼发现真相？

**Gallagher:** It's not just that. Above all, I wanted to see what happens when the major factions engage in a struggle for the legacy. Since this is the Watchmaker's first announcement in decades, the traitor within The Family is bound to reveal themselves.

> **加拉赫：**不仅如此，我更想看到的是各大派系为遗产争斗不断的样子，再加上「钟表匠」销声匿迹十余年来的首次发声，家族中的*叛徒*一定会露出马脚。

**March 7th:** So, the legacy is just a facade...

> **三月七：**所以，「遗产」真的只是个幌子……

**Gallagher:** If you want to consider the Stellaron as the legacy, I'm totally fine with that.

> **加拉赫：**如果你要把星核当做遗产，我也没意见。

**Himeko:** If that's the case, where is the Stellaron now?

> **姬子：**如此说来，那颗星核现在在哪儿？

**Gallagher:** That's a question for Mr. Wings. The Stellaron is still under The Family's control, and as the head of the Oak Family, I'm sure he holds all the answers.

> **加拉赫：**这就该问那个翅膀头小子了。星核一直处于家族的控制下，他又是橡木家系的牌面，心里肯定一清二楚。

*(Talk to Gallagher, optional)*

**Gallagher:** Go ahead. I'm sure Mr. Wings holds all the answers.

> **加拉赫：**去吧，那翅膀头小子肯定知道答案。

*(Talk to Firefly, optional)*

**Firefly:** As I suspected, the core of this issue lies within... the Stellaron.

> **流萤：**不出所料，事件的核心依然是…「星核」。

*(Talk to Misha, optional)*

**Misha:** You all look quite serious... Is there anything I can do to help?

> **米沙：**大家的表情都好严肃…有什么我能帮忙的吗？

### Learn the whereabouts of the Stellaron from Sunday

> *You originally thought you would just be meeting with the Express Crew, but the group you have now amassed is massive... Gallagher, Micah, Misha, Firefly, Robin, Sunday... And, it seems that the person that led to the creation of this lively scene was precisely the seemingly indifferent Gallagher himself. He shares the true nature of the Planet of Festivities with the Express Crew: It is a deliberate and controlled Stellaron disaster. However, as for the current location of the Stellaron, he cannot be sure... Now, all you can do is hope that the seemingly rebellious Family leader, Sunday, will cooperate with your work.*

[Recap] Sunday explained that the Stellaron was none other than the Penacony Grand Theater itself, and that the one who orchestrated it all was the Dreammaster. Sunday and Robin would head to Dreamjoy immediately and try to confront the Dreammaster.

*(Talk to Robin and Sunday)*

**Gallagher:** Are you done talking? So, will you tell us where the Stellaron is?

> **加拉赫：**你们聊得差不多了吧？怎样，愿意告诉我们那颗「星核」在哪儿了么？

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Sunday:** It... is the Penacony Grand Theater itself.

> **星期日：**它…就是匹诺康尼大剧院本身。

**Robin:** As I suspected. It's the embodiment of The Family, the edifice that first materialized within the sweet dream... That's what turned Penacony into its current state.

> **知更鸟：**果然是这样，家族的化身，最早出现在美梦中的建筑…它就是匹诺康尼变成这幅样子的罪魁祸首。

**Sunday:** As for the person who employed its power... It is in fact Mr. Gopher Wood, the current Dreammaster.

> **星期日：**而利用星核完成这一切的人…恐怕是歌斐木先生，也就是如今的「梦主」。

**Gallagher:** Well, that was easier than I thought. Did you conduct your own investigation already?

> **加拉赫：**哦，比我想得更顺利嘛，这么快就锁定嫌犯了。还是说，你事先调查做得挺充分？

**Sunday:** Correct. When I was trying to track down the person who murdered my sister, apart from you... Gopher Wood was my second suspect.

> **星期日：**你说得没错。在追查杀害妹妹的「凶手」时，除了你…我其次怀疑的就是他。

**Gallagher:** Confronting me first turned out to be a smart move on your part.

> **加拉赫：**看来你先找我对质是个相当正确的决定。

**Sunday:** I didn't have other options. The Dreammaster has been elusive, and even the heads of the Families can hardly get an audience with him.

> **星期日：**我没有其他选择。梦主从来都是神龙见首不见尾，就连各位家主都很难见到他。

**Sunday:** Moreover... Mr. Gopher Wood has been kind to my sister and me, and I didn't want it all to end like this.

> **星期日：**而且…歌斐木先生对我和妹妹有恩，我实在不愿面对这样的结果。

**Himeko:** What do you mean by that?

> **姬子：**此话怎讲？

**Robin:** To be honest, my brother and I are also victims of the Cancer of All Worlds. We grew up as orphans and were adopted by The Family when they came to help. Mr. Gopher Wood recognized our potential and brought us to Penacony.

> **知更鸟：**实不相瞒，我和哥哥也是万界之癌的受害者。我们从小便是孤儿，被前来救济的家族收养长大。歌斐木先生见我们有资质，就把我和哥哥带来了匹诺康尼。

**Robin:** But I can't just stand by and watch Mr. Gopher Wood become an enemy of the Harmony. I won't use my voice to support an evil cause...

> **知更鸟：**但是我们不能眼睁睁看着歌斐木先生走向「同谐」的对立面，我更不可能用自己的歌声去赞美罪恶的事业——

**Robin:** I won't step on that stage and sing, no matter who the traitor is or what orders they give me. I won't let the Charmony Festival become an event that destroys Harmony itself...

> **知更鸟：**无论家族的叛徒是谁，无论他向我下达怎样的指令，我都不会登台献唱。我们绝对不能把谐乐大典变成毁灭「同谐」的仪式……

**Robin:** For the paradise in our dreams.

> **知更鸟：**为了我们理想中的乐园。

**Sunday:** Indeed... for the paradise in our dreams.

> **星期日：**嗯…为了我们理想中的乐园。

**Sunday:** As the head of the Oak Family, I'm responsible for ensuring Penacony's promising future. Robin and I will head into the sweet dream and confront the Dreammaster, and if it turns out that The Family has truly strayed from the Harmony...

> **星期日：**身为橡木家系的家主，为了匹诺康尼的光明未来，我义不容辞。我和知更鸟会即刻前往美梦，想办法找到梦主对质。如果家族真的偏离了「同谐」……

**Sunday:** I'll fight alongside you. We'll put the Charmony Festival on hold and make sure Mr. Gopher Wood pays for his blood debt.

> **星期日：**我将与各位站在同一战线，中止谐乐大典，并亲自偿还歌斐木先生欠下的血债。

**Gallagher:** The enemies you are about to face aren't like this old dog here, who can barely even bark...

> **加拉赫：**你们即将面对的敌人，或者说，敌人们，可不像我这条老狗这么好欺负，动一动就会自己散架……

**Gallagher:** Since our interests are aligned, why don't we team up? Maybe, just maybe, we'll have a shot at success.

> **加拉赫：**既然各位有共同的目标，不如同心协力，或许还有一线成功的机会。

[Recap] Following in the footsteps of their Nameless predecessors, the Express Crew could not stand idly by in the face of Penacony's crisis. Welt declared that he would represent the Astral Express and accompany Sunday and Robin to negotiate with the Dreammaster.

**Himeko:** We have always been following in the footsteps of our Nameless predecessors, and there's no reason to stop now.

> **姬子：**我们追寻前辈无名客的脚步来到此地，没有理由不继续跟随他的足迹。

**March 7th:** Yeah, we Nameless won't back down from a challenge! Isn't that right, (Trailblazer)?

> **三月七：**嗯，无名客可不是碰见困难就会退缩的人！开拓者，你说是吧？

- ▸ Ain't nothing to fear no more!

> ▸ 已经没什么好怕的了！

**March 7th:** Ah... that line actually makes me a bit nervous...

> **三月七：**噫，你说这种台词，我反而有点慌了……

- ▸ Heroes never back down.

> ▸ 英雄可不能临阵脱逃啊。

**March 7th:** Exactly! It's time for the Crew to save the world once more!

> **三月七：**说得对，又到咱们星穹列车拯救世界的时候了！

- ▸ Going up against the Dreammaster? Nah, I'd surrender.

> ▸ 要对付梦主啊？我先投降。

**March 7th:** Come on. Don't crack jokes at such a serious moment.

> **三月七：**哎呀，这么有仪式感的场合，就不要开玩笑啦！

**Welt:** Rest assured — sitting on the sidelines isn't in our nature. Mr. Sunday, Miss Robin, I'm willing to accompany you on behalf of the Astral Express. Having a third party present should help with negotiations... and could make all the difference if things get ugly.

> **瓦尔特：**列车组不会对这种事袖手旁观，星期日先生，知更鸟小姐，我愿意代表星穹列车与你们同行。有第三方在场，谈判应当会更顺利些。如有危险…多一个人也总是好事。

**Sunday:** Thank you, Mr. Yang.

> **星期日：**那就有劳瓦尔特先生了。

**Robin:** Thank you all very much.

> **知更鸟：**非常感谢各位。

**Sunday:** The Charmony Festival is about to start, and time is against us. We must hasten.

> **星期日：**谐乐大典开幕在即，时间已经十分紧张，各位——我们必须出发了。

### See what Welt has to say

> *All related parties present have reached a consensus to eliminate the Stellaron. The righteous and upstanding Halovian siblings have even expressed willingness to utilize their identities to seek out a resolution from inside The Family itself. Though things seem to be developing satisfactorily, Welt still has some doubts... Go and see his views on the matter.*

**Welt:** Everyone, let's gather over here — we still need to make some preparations.

> **瓦尔特：**大家，请到这边来。临行前我们还有些准备要做。

[Recap] Welt asked you for Aventurine's chip, saying that if negotiations failed, he would use it to relay the message to the IPC. Before departure, Himeko asked Gallagher about the Nameless known as Legwork.

*(Talk to Welt)*

**Welt:** I may have been quick to step up to the plate, but confronting the Dreammaster could be a very dangerous affair.

> **瓦尔特：**虽然是我主动提出随行，但此行面对梦主…恐怕凶多吉少。

**March 7th:** Huh? Even you're saying that, Mr. Yang? How powerful is that Dreammaster?

> **三月七：**啊？连杨叔都这么说，这梦主得有多厉害啊？

**Himeko:** He's the leader of the Families of Penacony, and he has the entire power of the Harmony behind him... not to mention the Stellaron in his possession. We must proceed with great caution.

> **姬子：**身为匹诺康尼的分家领袖，梦主背后恐怕是整个「同谐」势力…何况星核也在他手中，必须做好最坏的打算。

**March 7th:** Maybe you can just stay behind this time, Mr. Yang...?

> **三月七：**噫，要不杨叔还是别去了吧……

**Welt:** No, that won't do. Even if we count Robin as an ally, something felt off about Mr. Sunday just now, although I can't quite put my finger on it... I have to make sure he won't turn against us when things start to get dicey.

> **瓦尔特：**这也不行。先不谈知更鸟小姐，我总觉得方才星期日先生的态度有些不对劲，但说不上来是为什么…至少得确保他不会临阵倒戈。

**Welt:** You're still carrying the keepsake the IPC envoy gave you, right, (Trailblazer)? May I borrow it for a moment?

> **瓦尔特：**开拓者，你身上应该带着公司使节给你的那件信物吧，可否借我一用？

*(Submit Aventurine's Token)*

**Welt:** Huh... I knew it.

> **瓦尔特：**呵…果然。

- ▸ What do you need it for?

> ▸ 要这个做什么？

- ▸ I don't think this'll bring us any luck....

> ▸ 这玩意应该不能当护身符…

**Welt:** As I suspected, this chip Aventurine gave to you... is actually a miniature transmitter.

> **瓦尔特：**如我所想，砂金给你的这枚筹码…是个小型发信器。

**Welt:** If I'm right, he intends to use it to track your location or contact you when needed. As it so happens, this may actually be of some use in the current situation.

> **瓦尔特：**他恐怕是打算用这个装置来追踪你的动向，或是在需要的时候与你联络。没想到竟在这种场合帮上了大忙。

**March 7th:** Aventurine? Is he still alive...? And what does he have to do with our mission to confront the Dreammaster?

> **三月七：**砂金？他真的还活着么…找梦主谈判又和他有什么关系？

**Welt:** Remember what I said earlier? Working with the IPC is a way to keep The Family in check.

> **瓦尔特：**还记得我说过的话么？与公司合作不失为一种制衡家族的方法。

**Welt:** If negotiations go south and The Family show their true colors by going after the Stellaron, I'll use this transmitter to send a message to the IPC. It'll be just the opportunity the IPC Ambassador was hoping for.

> **瓦尔特：**一旦谈判出现变故，相当于坐实了家族染指星核的意图，这可是那位使节梦寐以求的「突破口」。届时，我会利用这枚发信器将消息传给公司。

**Welt:** The only question mark in all this is Aventurine's current status... but the IPC is always listening, especially senior Strategic Investment Department heads like him. Getting the message across shouldn't be a problem.

> **瓦尔特：**唯一要赌一把的，就是砂金是否还平安无事了。不过公司线路向来有专人密切监听，更不用说他是战略投资部的重要干部，单是传递消息应该足够了。

**Himeko:** ...Good luck to you, Welt.

> **姬子：**…祝你好运，瓦尔特。

**Welt:** Well, you take care too. If anything goes wrong... don't worry about me. Just make sure to seal the Stellaron.

> **瓦尔特：**嗯，你们也多保重。如果有任何闪失…不用管我，一定要封印星核。

**Gallagher:** Spoken like a true hero.

> **加拉赫：**视死如归啊，那男人是个真英雄。

**Gallagher:** Even if the Dreammaster is innocent, The Family's corruption runs deep. I won't make the same mistake Mikhail did. Let's wish him the best of luck...

> **加拉赫：**就算梦主是清白的，家族的腐败也已根深蒂固。米哈伊尔犯过一次的错误，我不会再犯第二次。祝他好运吧……

**Himeko:** Don't you have something else to tell us, Gallagher?

> **姬子：**加拉赫先生，你应该还有话要对我们说吧？

**Gallagher:** Why would you say so?

> **加拉赫：**为什么这么觉得？

**Himeko:** Before we departed, the conductor asked us to inquire about the three Nameless in Penacony. We've already collected intel about Razalina and Tiernan, so the only one left is Legwork.

> **姬子：**临行前，列车长曾拜托我们在匹诺康尼打听三位无名客的消息。现在我们已经知晓了拉扎莉娜女士和铁尔南先生的事迹，只差最后一位「拉格沃克」了。

**Himeko:** If I'm not mistaken... we've already met him somewhere, haven't we?

> **姬子：**如果我没猜错…我们早就见过他了，对么？

**Gallagher:** Hm... it's not enough to say "meet," but the answer is pretty obvious. After all, I've hinted at it in quite an evident way.

> **加拉赫：**哼…说「见过」还谈不上，但答案确实很好猜。我之前的提示，够明显了。

**Gallagher:** I've been watching over you ever since I received the reply from the Astral Express, and I've seen the great effort you all put into linking different realms together across the cosmos. And now, after getting this far all in one piece, you have truly proven yourselves.

> **加拉赫：**自从收到星穹列车的回复，我就一直在关注你们的消息，包括各位为联结诸界付出的巨大努力。而现在，你们又安然无恙地走到了这里，已然证明了自己的实力。

**Gallagher:** Miss Himeko, were you the one who repaired the Express and got it sailing through the cosmos again?

> **加拉赫：**姬子小姐，是你修复了列车，令其重新驶于银河？

**Himeko:** Yes.

> **姬子：**是的。

**Gallagher:** And you two young Nameless... you have very interesting life stories and extraordinary skills.

> **加拉赫：**而这两位年轻的无名客…身世离奇，身怀绝技。

- ▸ Awww, you're making me blush with these flatteries....Keep them coming'!

**March 7th:** I don't know much about my life story... but I do have extraordinary skills!

> **三月七：**身世…我还不知道啦，但本姑娘确实身怀绝技！

- ▸ Ha, of course, my reputation precedes me. Believe in the Galactic Baseballer supremacy!

> ▸ 那你知道本球棒侠的其他称号么？

**March 7th:** What...? Hey, you can't just make up "titles" like that!

> **三月七：**你这家伙…哪有什么乱七八糟的称号啦！

**Gallagher:** Heh, you're full of energy. Please send my regards to... the conductor, Pom-Pom. Please let them know that their friend has fond memories from his time aboard the Express, which he reminisces on every time he has a good drink.

> **加拉赫：**呵呵，真有活力啊。至于那位名叫帕姆的列车长…请代我向它问好，它的朋友总是会在喝醉以后，念叨起列车上的时光。

**Gallagher:** As for the last Nameless... he embarked, disembarked, and embarked again... traveling in a great circle and ending up back where he started.

### Meet with the "Watchmaker"

Beneath the sea surface of Memory Zone, in a garden closest to the full moon in the water, an elderly man rests on a recliner, enveloped in utter silence.

The Watchmaker, Mikhail Char Legwork, has passed into that endless, timeless dream, where no sound could ever awaken him.

**March 7th:** Sure enough... the Watchmaker is the third Nameless. Even I could guess that one.

> **三月七：**果然…「钟表匠」就是第三位无名客，连我都猜到了。

**Himeko:** Well, let's have a look.

> **姬子：**嗯，让我们来看看吧。

As the words cease, Himeko nods ever so slightly in your direction. You take a deep breath, steady your mind, and turn your gaze towards the Watchmaker...

- ▸ Touch the dream bubble in Mikhail's hand.

> ▸ 触摸米哈伊尔手中的梦泡。

You press your hands against the dream bubble and the thick viscous memoria converges under strain, then stretches outward from your fingertips. As if weaving a delicate web, it gently cradles your palm.

A chill travels from your fingertips, carrying with it a myriad of vibrant and intertwined memories, as experience would suggest...

But this time, you see nothing at all.

- ▸ How could this be?

> ▸ 怎么会这样？

- ▸ Try to focus and capture memories...

> ▸ 尝试集中注意力，捕捉记忆…

- ▸ Take a deep breath and try to read the dream bubble again...

> ▸ 深呼吸，尝试重新读取梦泡…

The dream bubble is clearly extraordinary. Perhaps the approach was wrong, you think, holding your breath and closing your eyes. With one knee on the ground, you press your forehead against the thin film coated in memoria.

Yet, before you, there remains an abyss of darkness. No crimson sun descending upon snow-capped mountains, no gentle laughter, no twinkling stars, no echoes of swords clashing, and most of all, no traces of "trailblaze"...

...There is nothing and nothing is there. Indubitably, this is but an empty dream bubble.

### Ask around the lobby for information and wait for Dan Heng

> *Meanwhile, you, the cowboy who is a little late to the party, bring unfortunate news to Dan Heng and Pom-Pom who have stayed guard aboard the Express... There is a strong chance that the Crew members who have entered the dream are in danger, because an Emanator of Nihility is roaming the Dreamscape. You quickly succeed in convincing Dan Heng to accompany you into the Dreamscape and provide assistance, but run into a serious setback as you enter the hotel lobby: Due to strict hotel regulations, you are forbidden from entering Penacony. The astute Dan Heng seems to notice something out of the ordinary and suggests that he goes ahead to investigate alone. You wholeheartedly agree with his view and suggest splitting up to reassure him... Now, the instincts you developed over your many years of being exposed to dangerous situations are telling you that as you have time on your hands, perhaps collecting some information couldn't hurt.*

There is a strong chance that the Crew members who have entered the dream are in danger, because an Emanator of Nihility is roaming the Dreamscape. You quickly succeed in convincing Dan Heng to accompany you into the Dreamscape and provide assistance, but run into a serious setback as you enter the hotel lobby: Due to strict hotel regulations, you are forbidden from entering Penacony. You and Dan Heng reach a consensus: Whether this is reality or a dream, nothing about Penacony in its current state can be described as "normal." Fortunately, you always keep a backup plan ready for yourself (though it may not always lead to good things...) It's time for you to use your "street smarts"!*

**Dan Heng:** How do we get into the VIP lounge?

> **丹恒：**我们要怎么进入贵宾室？

**Boothill:** This is where my street smarts come into play.

> **波提欧：**这算什么事，交给江湖智慧。

[Recap] Claiming to be a Nameless, Boothill stirred up trouble with the lobby manager, Dennis. Dennis asked both Boothill and Dan Heng to remain calm and escorted them to the VIP lounge to wait.

*(Talk to Dennis)*

**Boothill:** You're the lobby manager, right?

> **波提欧：**你是这儿的大堂经理，没错吧？

**Dennis:** Yes, I am. How may I assist you?

> **丹尼斯：**正是。请问有什么可以为您效劳的？

**Boothill:** We're the Nameless from the Astral Express! We wanted to check in, but there's something wrong with your system! The lady at the front desk said she would contact the manager, but now she's nowhere to be found!

> **波提欧：**我们是星穹列车的无名客，过来办入住的，一直都没回应！你们前台那小姑娘说要帮我们联系管事的，结果人也没影了！

**Boothill:** We've been waitin' here forever, without any food or water! What the fork, man? Is this how The Family treats its guests?

> **波提欧：**咱兄弟俩在这儿干瞪眼大半天，一没水喝二没饭吃，宝了个贝的，家族就是这么招待人的？

**Dan Heng:** Is this your idea of "street smarts"? Starting an altercation?

> **丹恒：**你的「江湖智慧」就是找茬？

**Boothill:** It's called "standing up for your rights."

> **波提欧：**这叫争取正当权益。

**Dennis:** I apologize for the inconvenience. Please wait while we try to contact Mr. Sunday. I'll arrange two premium seats in the VIP Lounge, so you can rest there while you wait.

> **丹尼斯：**两位稍安勿躁，来龙去脉我都知道，酒店需要先和星期日先生取得联系。这样吧，我安排两个贵宾室的酒水位，请二位边休息边等待。

**Boothill:** See? Just like that.

> **波提欧：**看，这不就成了。

**Dan Heng:** Just... just don't call yourself a Nameless next time.

> **丹恒：**…下次别再自称无名客了。

### Take a seat in the VIP Lounge drinks table

> *You used your "street smarts" to win your deserved rights. That poor lobby manager crumbled in the face of your intimidation tactics and organized refreshments for you in the VIP lounge... Though it may look like all they have offered is thoroughly insufficient emotional compensation, you know this is just one part of your backup plan... What now? Let's pray that things can go a little more smoothly from here.*

**Boothill:** Wow! This bar is something else — certainly worthy of the Planet of Festivities.

> **波提欧：**嚯！真不愧是盛会之星，现实里的酒吧都这么气派。

[Recap] At the drink lounge, Boothill requested the pre-arranged "Asdana's White Oak." However, the drinksmith said there was no reservation for that particular drink. Boothill decided to wait for contact from Black Swan while sharing drinks with Dan Heng.

*(Talk to Anderson)*

**Anderson:** Good evening, gentlemen.

> **安德森：**两位英武的先生，晚上好。

**Boothill:** Hey... I have an order for a bottle of Asdana's White Oak. Can you help us find it?

> **波提欧：**嗯…哥们儿，我在这里预留了一瓶「阿斯德纳白橡木」，能帮我们找找吗？

**Anderson:** Asdana's White Oak? I think there might be a misunderstanding. We don't serve that here.

> **安德森：**阿斯德纳白橡木？二位应该是记错了，我们这里没有这款酒。

**Boothill:** No way. Are you sure you're not mistaken?

> **波提欧：**不可能，你再好好想想？

**Anderson:** If someone had reserved such a beverage, I would definitely remember it. It sells for hundreds of thousands of credits per bottle, after all... I couldn't afford to cover for such an item if it were broken or lost.

> **安德森：**这么名贵的酒品如果有人预留，我一定会记得。毕竟这酒一支要卖几十万信用点…要是打碎或弄丢了，我可赔不起啊。

**Boothill:** That's strange... could it be that the Memokeeper couldn't afford it?

> **波提欧：**怪了…难不成那忆者买不起？

**Dan Heng:** Then what should we do now?

> **丹恒：**现在怎么办？

**Boothill:** No need to rush. Let's grab some drinks first. Maybe I arrived too early and she hasn't come yet.

> **波提欧：**不急，咱们先喝几杯。也许是我来得太快了，她还没赶上。

**Boothill:** Let's see what kind of juice malts you all have here... Well, give me a glass of Hinnom Valley 40 Years. I'll have it neat, no ice.

> **波提欧：**我看看你们这有什么单一麦芽果汁…给我来杯「欣嫩子谷40年」吧，纯饮，不加冰。

**Anderson:** Well, that's the most expensive one on the list. You have a taste for the finer things.

> **安德森：**这可是酒单上最贵的一款。您可真是内行啊。

**Boothill:** Hah, it's on the house anyway...

> **波提欧：**嘿，送的不喝白不喝……

**Anderson:** What can I get for you?

> **安德森：**您要来点什么吗？

**Dan Heng:** Anything you recommend is fine.

> **丹恒：**随意，我都可以。

**Anderson:** Then I would recommend today's special, Glass Village — it's Classic SoulGlad mixed with Leboom juice. It's refreshing and suits your cool demeanor. Just one minute.

> **安德森：**那就来杯今日特调「玻璃村庄」吧，经典苏乐达配上爆炸柠果汁，口感清爽，也符合您清冷的气质。请二位稍等。

Soon, Anderson brings two cups of wine...

**Boothill:** Ahh! This flavor... Dynamite Barbecue with Rocket Fuel... It really hits the spot! Truly the finest sherry cask-aged malt juice in the cosmos.

> **波提欧：**呼哈！这浓郁的黑火药烤肉蘸双氧水味，真是…绝了！真不愧是全银河最好的雪莉桶熟成麦芽果汁。

**Dan Heng:** "Dynamite Barbecue with Rocket Fuel"... Is that really something that humans enjoy?

> **丹恒：**「黑火药烤肉蘸双氧水味」…真的是人类会喜欢的味道吗？

**Boothill:** Hey, this guy doesn't know anything at all.

> **波提欧：**嘿嘿，他根本就不懂。

**Anderson:** As long as you are satisfied, dear guests. Please enjoy.

> **安德森：**二位满意就好，请慢用。

[Recap] Boothill and Dan Heng analyzed the situation and opined that Acheron has another purpose in pretending to be a Galaxy Ranger. Dan Heng thought the invitation may not have been sent by The Family, and that it was intriguing that they insisted on holding the Charmony Festival.

**Boothill:** Let's give the Memokeeper another half system hour. If she doesn't show up, we'll need to come up with a new plan. In the meantime, let's take stock of the situation. What do you think?

> **波提欧：**我们等那忆者半个系统时，如果她还不出现，就再想其他办法。这时间足够咱俩好好盘盘现状了，依你之见，问题出在哪？

**Dan Heng:** The situation is unclear. Something must have happened on the Planet of Festivities, but the public is unaware of it. Someone in a position of power within The Family must be covering it up.

> **丹恒：**情况确实不甚明朗。盛会之星一定发生了什么，但公众却对此毫无认知。想必是有家族的位高权重者在掩盖事态吧。

**Dan Heng:** It's unusual for the followers of the Harmony to invite other factions, let alone the IPC and the Masked Fools... If what you said about the Emanator of the Nihility is true, the situation in Penacony is... a little complicated, to say the least.

> **丹恒：**「同谐」向其他派系发出邀约本就不同寻常，况且还有公司和愚者参与其中，如果你口中的「虚无」令使也确有其事，匹诺康尼当下的形势…有些过于复杂了。

**Boothill:** Actually, there's something else I'm concerned about — regarding Acheron.

> **波提欧：**其实关于「黄泉」，还有件令我在意的事。

**Boothill:** As you know, the faction that follows the Path of The Hunt are some of the most dangerous folks in the Cosmos to mess with. I mean, who in their right mind would impersonate the Xianzhou Alliance or the Galaxy Rangers? It's like asking for a death wish...

> **波提欧：**你也清楚，信仰「巡猎」的派系是银河里最惹不起的一帮人。任何一个神志清醒的家伙都不敢冒充仙舟联盟或巡海游侠，这完全是在找死……

**Boothill:** Isn't there a saying among the Xianzhou people that "the Reignbow sect lets their Lux Arrow do all the beating..."

> **波提欧：**仙舟人不是有句话吗，帝弓仅以光矢宣其轮椅——

**Dan Heng:** Talking. Do all the talking.

> **丹恒：**纶音，宣其纶音。

**Boothill:** Well, you know what I mean. Even though the Galaxy Rangers have been out of sight for years, we've been keeping an eye on this region. Even the dumbest criminals know better than to mess with the Annihilation Gang, much less the Rangers.

> **波提欧：**无所谓，你懂我意思就行。总之，别看巡海游侠销声匿迹那么多年，我们一直盯着这片宇宙呢。很多傻宝宁可冒犯泯灭帮，也不敢得罪游侠。

**Boothill:** But that Acheron lady... she doesn't seem like a lunatic at all. On the contrary, she's highly logical and organized. She knows exactly when to hold back and when to strike without mercy.

> **波提欧：**但那个黄泉…至少从她做的事来看，不像是个疯子。正相反，她很有逻辑和条理，该留手时就留手，该杀伐果决时也绝不手软。

**Dan Heng:** And you believe that someone like her would have an ulterior motive for impersonating a Galaxy Ranger?

> **丹恒：**你认为这样一个人借用巡海游侠的名号，别有目的。

**Boothill:** I'm not entirely sure, but I do have my suspicions. Maybe she knows a Galaxy Ranger, or perhaps she's trying to lure us out for some reason... which I can't figure out.

> **波提欧：**我没这么断定，只是觉得蹊跷，也可能她真的认识巡海游侠，也可能是她出于某种原因要引我们现身…不知道。

**Dan Heng:** Anyway, what worries me more are the anomalies within the Family. They've summoned followers from various paths for the festival. No matter how generous The Family might be, this move seems highly unusual.

> **丹恒：**我还是更关注家族的异样。因为这场盛会，十数条命途的行者被召集至匹诺康尼。家族再怎么包容，也不会做出如此反常的举动。

**Dan Heng:** Unless the invitations weren't sent by them. If that's the case, it's even more intriguing that The Family insists on organizing the Charmony Festival... despite the chaos.

> **丹恒：**除非邀请函不是由他们寄出，那家族在这种局面下依然坚持举办谐乐大典…就更耐人寻味了。

**Boothill:** Maybe it's Xipe the Harmony pulling the strings. You find it beyond human understanding because it wasn't arranged by humans at all.

> **波提欧：**没准这一切就是「同谐」希佩的指示呢。你觉得这事不合理，那是因为人做不出来。

**Boothill:** People do stupid things out of irrational impulses. They abandon their principles when self-interest is involved. They believe in things they know they shouldn't, and fudge— they even break their own rules!

> **波提欧：**人会因为非理性的冲动做出傻事，会在触及自身利益的时候放弃原则，也会相信一些明知不该相信的东西，宝了个贝的，人甚至会打破自己刚定下的规矩！

**Boothill:** But Aeons don't. THEY stick to their determined path and never turn back, even if THEY reach a dead end!

> **波提欧：**但星神不会。星神只会一条路走到黑，不撞南墙不回头——撞了也不回头！

**Dan Heng:** You think Xipe's will is behind all this?

> **丹恒：**你认为这起事件背后，是希佩的意志在推动？

**Boothill:** It may not necessarily be Xipe, but there's definitely some higher entity involved. I know it may sound pessimistic, but if human free will were reliable, why would we even need Galaxy Rangers?

> **波提欧：**也不一定就是祂，但肯定有个形而上的意识在作祟。别说哥们悲观，如果人类的自由意志可信，那还要巡海游侠做什么？

**Boothill:** It's much simpler when you boil it down to the Aeons and Paths, like how Lan always follows the Path of The Hunt or how the Express stays true to the Trailblaze despite Akivili's disappearance.

> **波提欧：**把一切归结于星神和命途，事情就没那么复杂了。就像岚永远走在「巡猎」的道路上，就像阿基维利虽然不知所踪，但列车依然行驶在开拓的路上，永不脱轨。

**Dan Heng:** But in my opinion, Akivili's fall holds significance for the Nameless.

> **丹恒：**但我认为，阿基维利的陨落，同样是祂为无名客留下的宝贵遗产。

**Boothill:** Oh? So you're saying the Nameless now have to take responsibility for their own choices because their "absolutely right" leader is gone?

> **波提欧：**喔，我猜是因为失去了「绝对正确」的领导者后，无名客们必须为自己的选择负责了？

**Dan Heng:** Exactly. I believe the purpose of the journey isn't just about following a path that's always considered "absolutely right"...

> **丹恒：**是的。我认为旅途的意义，并不是为了走上一条「绝对正确」的道路……

**Dan Heng:** It's more about doing your best to choose the "right path" for yourself among countless possibilities, even with limited insight and judgment.

> **丹恒：**而是为了在不可胜数的道路中，凭借自己有限的见识和判断，尽力选择「适合自己的道路」。

**Boothill:** I don't know what you've been through, but I agree that people must take responsibility for their choices, because no one else can do it for them.

> **波提欧：**虽然不知道你经历了什么，但人要为自己负责，这点我赞同，任何人都没法代劳。

**Boothill:** That's why the Galaxy Rangers need to uncover the impostor... and figure out her true intentions.

> **波提欧：**也正因如此，巡海游侠必须亲自找到那个冒牌货…弄清楚她究竟要做什么。

[Recap] Boothill was going to steal other guests' invitations when the drink Black Swan had reserved was finally presented. The bottle only had one message: "I'll be waiting for you on the Astral Express."

**Boothill:** Just in case, I have a backup plan if the Memokeeper doesn't show up... this is my final backup plan, I promise.

> **波提欧：**以防万一，如果那忆者始终不出现，我还有个备用计划…这是最后一个了。

**Dan Heng:** You sure have a lot of cards up your sleeve.

> **丹恒：**你的备用计划真不少。

**Boothill:** Well, going back to my old career would make things a lot easier.

> **波提欧：**我呢，其实不太擅长弯弯绕绕，如果能干回老本行，很多事都会变得简单许多。

**Boothill:** By the way, when you were walking around the hotel, did you happen to see any... important-looking guests?

> **波提欧：**丹恒兄弟，你之前在酒店转了一圈，有没有看见哪位客人，或哪几位客人…身份比较尊贵？

**Dan Heng:** ...What's your plan?

> **丹恒：**…你想做什么？

**Boothill:** It's simple. We just grab some hostages and use them as bargaining chips with The Family. Or maybe we can even take their identities...

> **波提欧：**很简单，咱们要悄无声息地绑上几个人质，后面就什么都好办了，既可以拿来和家族交涉，也可以拿走他们的身份——

**Dan Heng:** No need for that. We'll return to the Express now.

> **丹恒：**不必了，现在就返回列车。

**Boothill:** Wait! Are you getting scared? Draw your weapon! Let's make a big scene!

> **波提欧：**怎么！难不成你怕了，兄弟？掏出你的枪来，咱们该干票大的了！

**Anderson:** Are you leaving, esteemed guests? Would you like to cancel that Asdana's White Oak you just ordered?

> **安德森：**二位是要走了吗？那刚才点的阿斯德纳白橡木要退掉吗？

**Boothill:** ...Huh? Asdana's White Oak? But didn't you just say...

> **波提欧：**…啊？阿斯德纳白橡木？可你刚才不是说……

**Anderson:** Looks like you are a bit intoxicated, esteemed guests. You ordered a bottle of Asdana's White Oak just a moment ago.

> **安德森：**哈哈，看来二位是有些醉了。就在半分钟前，您刚跟我点了一瓶阿斯德纳白橡木。

**Dan Heng:** Looks like... your Memokeeper friend has finally arrived.

> **丹恒：**看来…你的忆者朋友来找你了。

**Boothill:** ...Oh, right! Sorry, my memory's not the best. You know, too many modifications and all. Anyway, let me check... Well fork me... It's Asdana's White Oak alright, and there's a note...

> **波提欧：**…哦对对对，瞧我这脑子，义体改造多了就是会这样，健忘！让我看看…他宝贝的，真是阿斯德纳白橡木，上面有行字……

**Boothill:** ..."I'll be waiting for you on the Astral Express."

> **波提欧：**…「我在星穹列车上等你。」

**Dan Heng:** No mistake, that's her message to you. She knew the hotel wasn't safe, so she suggested we find another place.

> **丹恒：**是她给你的留言，错不了。她知道我们在一起行动，也知道酒店并不安全，要找个掩人耳目的地方。

**Boothill:** Well, looks like we took a detour, but now it's back to the Astral Express. So, what are we waiting for? Let's go!

> **波提欧：**结果绕了一大圈，还是回到了星穹列车。那还等什么，赶紧走吧！

[Recap] Acheron claimed that she had been ejected by Penacony, and that the Astral Express should immediately warp away from the system. The reason Acheron impersonated a Galaxy Ranger was to get in touch with a real Ranger to return relics.

**Pom-Pom:** You're back? Two guests just boarded, saying they were looking for Boothill, so I told them to wait in the Parlor Car.

> **帕姆：**你们回来了？前面有两个人上车，说要找波提欧乘客。我让她们先在观景车厢等候了帕。

**Boothill:** Oh! Just in time!

> **波提欧：**哦！来得正好。

**Dan Heng:** ...Two guests?

> **丹恒：**…两个人？

**Pom-Pom:** She's one of them. Look, we welcome all passengers on the Astral Express, but sneaking in like that... You have no regard for etiquette!

> **帕姆：**就是她。虽说星穹列车欢迎每一位乘客，但一个个都偷偷摸摸潜进来…你们是不是太不拿自己当外人了帕！

**Black Swan:** My apologies, conductor. It was an oversight on my part. I assumed you were already acquainted with the Garden... Given the chaotic situation in Penacony, the Nameless are the only ones we can truly trust right now.

> **黑天鹅：**对不起，列车长，是我失了礼数，误以为列车对忆庭已经很熟悉了…眼下匹诺康尼局势错综复杂，也只剩下各位无名客值得信任。

**Dan Heng:** You are the Memokeeper?

> **丹恒：**你就是那位忆者？

**Black Swan:** Pleased to meet you, Dan Heng. I've seen you in others' memories. And as for Boothill... This is our first face-to-face meeting. I hope you enjoyed that bottle of Asdana's White Oak. You sure have a refined taste.

> **黑天鹅：**初次见面，丹恒先生，我在其他人的记忆中见过你。而波提欧先生…我们也算初次见面，希望你喜欢那瓶阿斯德纳白橡木。你真会点些不好找的酒。

**Boothill:** Finally, Memokeeper! Well, let's cut to the chase. Spill everything you know.

> **波提欧：**可真是让我一通好找啊，忆者！我就开门见山了，把你知道的都告诉我。

**Black Swan:** That's precisely what I intend to do. But before that, please allow me to introduce myself. My name is Black Swan, and I serve the Garden of Recollection as a Memokeeper. As for Acheron's story...

> **黑天鹅：**正有此意。但在此之前请容我先做个介绍，我叫黑天鹅，是流光忆庭的忆者。而关于那位黄泉女士的故事……

**Black Swan:** I'm sure she knows it better than I do.

> **黑天鹅：**也许她本人比我更清楚。

**Acheron:** Greetings. I'm Acheron.

> **黄泉：**二位好，我就是黄泉。

**Boothill:** What!? You Garden of Recollection shirtbag, you betrayed me!?

> **波提欧：**什么？！他宝了个贝的——流光忆庭的，你出卖我？！

**Acheron:** I apologize, but she did that at my request. Due to certain... reasons, I have been exiled by The Family. Thankfully, this Memokeeper came to my aid and helped me escape their surveillance unnoticed.

> **黄泉：**抱歉，这是我的请求。出于一些原因，我遭到匹诺康尼放逐，所幸这位忆者一路随行，我才有机会悄无声息地摆脱家族的控制。

**Black Swan:** To be honest, it was more like stalking than helping, and the process was far from "unnoticed"... But we did escape.

> **黑天鹅：**实际上并非随行，而是跟踪。过程也绝对谈不上「悄无声息」…但算了，就依你吧。

**Acheron:** I asked her to guide me to a place beyond The Family's reach and to contact a few trustworthy individuals, namely all of you.

> **黄泉：**我请求她带我去一个家族视线之外的地方，联系几个值得信任的人，也就是各位了。

**Boothill:** Trustworthy? Ha... hahahahaha! Son of a nice lady! You think I'm dumb or something?

> **波提欧：**信任？哈哈，哈哈哈哈哈…小可爱，你是拿我当疯子还是傻子？

**Boothill:** How about this? I'll put a few bullet holes in your head and see what secrets spill out, then we can talk about "trust"...

> **波提欧：**要不这样，先让我在你身上开几个窟窿，看看里面藏着的究竟是什么，然后咱们再来谈信任……

**Acheron:** It doesn't have to be like that. I'm willing to answer all your questions, but not right now... If my cover hadn't been blown, we might have had more time, but at the moment, we don't have any other options.

> **黄泉：**不用如此吧。你想知道的我会悉数告知，但不是现在…如果我的身份没有败露，或许还有更多时间，但眼下我们只能这么做了。

**Boothill:** No other options? What do you mean?

> **波提欧：**只能？什么只能？

**Acheron:** This is the only way I can ensure everyone's safety...

> **黄泉：**唯有如此，我才能保障各位的安全……

**Acheron:** I kindly request an immediate warp jump out of the Asdana star system.

> **黄泉：**请星穹列车立即跃迁，离开阿斯德纳星系。

**Dan Heng:** ...

> **丹恒：**……

**Pom-Pom:** This passenger is requesting...

> **帕姆：**这位乘客的意思是……

**Black Swan:** As far as I can tell, she's not a threat and seems to be telling the truth.

> **黑天鹅：**依我之见，她并无恶意，并且说的是实话。

**Acheron:** I've briefly traveled with your companions and know their whereabouts, Dan Heng. Please rest assured, your Nameless companions are safe, but they need our help.

> **黄泉：**丹恒先生，我曾与你的同伴短暂同行，也知晓他们身在何方。请相信各位无名客仍平安无事，但也同样需要我们的帮助。

**Acheron:** As for Boothill, as you may have guessed... I've been waiting for you. Galaxy Rangers are known for their elusive nature and limited contact with each other, so this is the only way I could "reach out" to you.

> **黄泉：**而波提欧先生，你或许已经猜到了…我在等待你的到来。巡海游侠行踪不定，彼此之间也往来甚少，原谅我只能通过这种方式与你们取得「联系」。

**Acheron:** Only by doing this can I find a true Galaxy Ranger and fulfill a long-standing promise...

> **黄泉：**只有这样，我才能找到真正的巡海游侠。也唯有如此，我才能兑现一个久远的承诺……

**Acheron:** ...To return "his" relics to their rightful owner.

> **黄泉：**…将「他」的遗物物归原主。

...

**Acheron:** Someone once told me... that every rainfall is like a gift from the heavens, a sign of their mercy upon the world.

> **黄泉：**曾有人这么对我说…每一场雨都是苍天对世界的怜悯。

**Acheron:** Raindrops are said to be the tears of the Gods, shed in response to the sorrows of the world. Their constant pouring is a reminder that the Gods haven't abandoned us yet.

> **黄泉：**雨露是神的眼泪，因人世的悲伤而淌下。但正因它们还会落下，代表众神尚未对这个世界失去希望。

**Acheron:** So...

> **黄泉：**所以……

[Recap] Acheron stood with an old man upon the Sea of Nihility. The old man fought against the fate of Nihility by purifying Sin Thirsters, indifferent to whether it held any meaning. Acheron offered her aid, seeking to understand the meaning of the Nihility itself.

**Acheron:** How long has this rain been going on for?

> **黄泉：**这场雨，持续多久了？

**???:** I used to believe, just like you, that it would eventually stop. Years and decades passed... and in the end, such hope faded away before the rain did.

> **？？？：**曾经，我也和你一样期待它会在某一天停息，就这么过去了几年、几十年…最后，「希望」比这场雨更早迎来终点。

**???:** Looks like the God you mentioned doesn't exist after all.

> **？？？：**看来你口中的神并不存在。

As he spoke, the old man's gaze remained fixed on the distance. Amidst the fine drizzle of black rain, countless shadowy hands emerged from the sea, shrouded in an ethereal mist, reaching out towards the sky one by one.

**???:** Well, let me share a story with you...

> **？？？：**换我为你讲个故事吧……

**???:** Mortals who walk the Paths are like sailors on a vast ocean, leaving behind a trail that creates countless ripples of possibilities. These ripples last longer than the fleeting lifetimes of humans.

> **？？？：**凡人走在命途上，就像坐着小船渡过水面，留下一条蜿蜒的行迹，推开无数可能性的涟漪。相较人类转瞬即逝的一生，这些波浪久久不会平息。

**???:** And for some, their presence leaves such a strong mark that it's reflected in the waves.

> **？？？：**而其中有些人，他们存在的痕迹过于强烈，以致在这一簇簇浪花里留下了自己的倒影。

**Acheron:** Like... those shadows on the ocean.

> **黄泉：**就像是…海面上的那些影子。

**???:** Sin Thirsters... the obsessions of the Pathstriders. They emerge from the depths of IX, seeing themselves as masters of their own destiny, unknowingly repeating the actions of their past lives.

> **？？？：**「血罪灵」…命途行者的执念，它们从IX的阴影中诞生，将自己视作事主，不自知地重复着逝者生前的行为。

**???:** They emerge from the Nihility and head toward it, leading purposeless lives. However, these hollow phantoms...

> **？？？：**它们从「虚无」中诞生，向着「虚无」而去，度过毫无意义的一生。但就是这么一群空虚的幻影……

**???:** ...They were once my dear companions, a group of Galaxy Rangers.

> **？？？：**…却曾经是我重要的同伴，一群巡海游侠。

**Acheron:** Are you watching over them?

> **黄泉：**你是在守望它们吗？

**???:** Watching over them? No, I'm guiding them toward transcendence.

> **？？？：**守望？不，我是在超度它们。

**???:** It was a brutal war, a crusade that shook the universe. The universe witnessed the fall of Zulo the Lord Ravager, but it came at a price... A price so hefty that only those who were there still remember.

> **？？？：**那是一场惨烈的战争，一场轰动寰宇的讨伐，宇宙见证了绝灭大君「诛罗」的陨落，但代价…除了亲历者，没人会记得代价。

**???:** The unwavering determination of The Hunt followers persists even in death, so someone must guide these lost souls to their life beyond. They were heroes in their time, and they shouldn't be reduced to mere puppets of the Nihility.

> **？？？：**「巡猎」的死志直至生命终结也不会平息，所以总得人来引渡这些亡魂，他们生前都是英雄，死后不该沦为「虚无」的傀儡。

**???:** As for me... I've suffered too many losses on that battlefield to advance any further, and that makes me the most fitting person to carry out this task.

> **？？？：**至于我…我同样在那片战场上失去了太多，无法再度踏上征程了，反而变成了最适合完成这件事的人。

**Acheron:** But you know, these Sin Thirsters... They are not who they used to be.

> **黄泉：**但你知道，这些血罪灵…毕竟不是他们。

**???:** Does this seem pointless to you?

> **？？？：**在你看来，这件事没有意义吗？

**???:** Well, some tasks have to be done, even if they are pointless.

> **？？？：**但有些事即便没有意义，也总是要去做的。

**Acheron:** ...

> **黄泉：**……

**Acheron:** I can help you.

> **黄泉：**我可以帮你。

**???:** For what?

> **？？？：**为了什么？

**Acheron:** For the meaning of the Nihility... that's what I've been seeking.

> **黄泉：**「虚无」的意义…那同样是我的所求。

**???:** ...I see. After all, this realm is off-limits to ordinary souls, right?

> **？？？：**…也对，常人怎么可能踏足此地呢？

**???:** Thank you, stranger. I wish that you find what you seek.

> **？？？：**谢谢你，陌生人。祝你能在这趟旅途中找到答案。

**Acheron:** Before we part ways, I have one more question...

> **黄泉：**在那之前，我还有一个问题……

**Acheron:** It is true that their actions and even their entire lives may seem pointless from our perspective, but if, and it's just an "if"...

> **黄泉：**诚然，血罪灵的行为，乃至它们的一生，在我们的视角下都毫无意义。可如果，我是说如果……

**Acheron:** If this is what the departed ones expected, should we try to change it?

> **黄泉：**如果这正是逝者们期望的结果，我们还应令它做出改变吗？

**???:** A good question, and a profound one... I don't know the answer.

> **？？？：**这是一个好问题，也很难回答，至少…我不知道答案。

**???:** What I do know is that one day, I too will pass away. I hope that when that day comes...

> **？？？：**但我知道的是，总有一天，我也会与世长辞。我发自内心地希望，在那个时候……

**???:** Someone will stand at my grave and place a bouquet of flowers.

> **？？？：**会有人在我的坟前，也献上一束花。
