> **星期日：**看来有人需要帮助…我们去看看吧。

*(Talk to Welt, optional)*

**Welt:** Thank you for everything you've done. I'll be waiting here.

[Recap] Sunday encountered a drunken guest who had sold off all his belongings just to join Penacony's dream. The guest claimed that the Penacony Grand Theater looked better than the moon of his homeland.

*(Talk to Drunken Guest)*

**Drunken Guest:** Oh, Mr. Sunday... Hey there! Okay, see the moon in the sky? It's about the size of the cap on my SoulGlad bottle!

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**星期日先生…你好呀！瞧见了吗？那天上的月亮，就和我手中的苏乐达瓶盖相同大小！

**Drunken Guest:** If I just reached out my hand... I could grab the moon, couldn't I?

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**是不是…只要我伸出手，就能把月亮抓在怀里了？嗯？对吧？

**Sunday:** The moon... You mean the Grand Theater?

> **星期日：**月亮…呵呵，你想说的是大剧院吧？

**Drunken Guest:** Oh, yeah! Haha, look at me! I've been away from home for too long... I must be missing that moon... *hiccup*

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**哦，哦！哈哈，你瞧我，一定是离家太久，太想念那轮月亮了…嗝。

**Drunken Guest:** But... it's no big deal! The Grand Theater here looks much better than the moon back home... It's just... magnificent!

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**不过…无所谓！咱们匹诺康尼的大剧院可比月亮亮堂多了…好看！养眼！

**Drunken Guest:** They told me not to sell everything I had just to come to Penacony... Tsk, how short-sighted!

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**他们当年还劝我不要把家当全都卖掉，一门心思向着匹诺康尼…呸，真是目光短浅！

**Robin:** Selling everything you had? Why would you go to such lengths?

> **知更鸟：**「把家当全都卖掉」…为什么要做到这份上？

**Drunken Guest:** Why? Can't you see...? Life back home is miserable... It's not really living at all.

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**为什么？还能为什么…老家日子太苦，活得连个人样都没了。

**Drunken Guest:** It's better to be here in Penacony! No pain, no worries about tomorrow, just sweet dreams! You can do whatever you want! That's what I call living... *hiccup* Yeah, now this is the life!

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**还是匹诺康尼好啊，只有美梦，没有苦头，不用为明天烦恼，想做什么就做什么，这才叫活着呢…嗝，舒坦！

**Robin:** Is this... truly "living"...?

> **知更鸟：**这…真的能算是活着吗……

**Drunken Guest:** *hiccup* Huh...? What did you just say, young lady...? I didn't quite catch that...

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**嗝…嗯？小姑娘，你刚才问了什么？我没听清……

**Sunday:** Oh, it's nothing, sir. You see, the traffic on Glaux Avenue can be dangerous. How about I ask a Bloodhound Family member to escort you to Aideen Park over there so you can continue enjoying your sweet dream?

> **星期日：**没什么，先生。你看，格拉克斯大道上车流湍急，容易发生危险。艾迪恩公园就在附近，我让猎犬家系成员带您到那边继续享用美梦，如何？

**Drunken Guest:** Oh... yeah, that's a great idea! Wow, no wonder you're the leader of this sweet dream! You're totally a lifesaver!

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**哦…好啊，你说得对。不愧是美梦的领导人，我的大救星！

**Drunken Guest:** See you around, Mr. Sunday! And... It was nice chatting with you... *hiccup*

> **醉醺醺的宾客：**那回见了，星期日先生。谢谢你陪我聊天…嗝。

[Recap] Robin felt that Dreamchasers indulging in illusory happiness weren't truly living. Sunday believed that when people are obsessed with fulfilling their sense of self-worth, it inevitably breeds exploitation and oppression, leading to a tragic law of the jungle.

**Robin:** ...

> **知更鸟：**……

**Sunday:** What's up, sister?

> **星期日：**怎么了，妹妹？

**Robin:** This is the land of the dreams, but why do they live like this?

> **知更鸟：**明明是在梦想之地，为什么人们却会过上这种生活呢？

**Robin:** The man we ran into... he doesn't seem happy at all.

> **知更鸟：**刚才那位先生…一点都不幸福。

**Robin:** Even though sweet dreams are nice, they're just illusions. But for him, they're the only way to survive, even if it means giving up on reality... That's not really living at all.

> **知更鸟：**美梦再怎么甜蜜，也是看不见摸不着的幻象，却被他当作生存的唯一选择，甚至为此放弃了现实中的未来…这根本算不得「生活」。

**Sunday:** I suppose you have a point... but in my opinion, that's how most people live their lives.

> **星期日：**嗯，这样啊…可在我看来，这反倒是常人该有的活法。

**Robin:** ...Why do you say that?

> **知更鸟：**…为什么这么说？

**Sunday:** You think that man is not actually "living," but that's not quite accurate. Even without Penacony, people create their own illusions called "self-value."

> **星期日：**你刚才说，那位先生以美梦维生算不得「生活」，其实不然。就算没有匹诺康尼，人们也会活在各自的幻觉中，这幻觉名为「自我价值」。

**Sunday:** People believe they have a predetermined value to fulfill. Gaining value means gaining power, and those deemed worthless are seen as the weak.

> **星期日：**人们总以为自己命中注定要实现某种价值。为自己挣得价值便意味着强大的力量，相反，无价值的人则被贬为弱者。

**Sunday:** However, value doesn't come out of thin air, and there's a limit to it. To accumulate value, people have to take from others. So, the weak get exploited and oppressed...

> **星期日：**然而价值并非是由人们凭空创造的，其总和亦有上限。要想实现所谓的价值，人就必须从他人手中掠夺。就这样，弱者们被剥削、被压迫……

**Robin:** Are you suggesting that... this is not how things should be?

> **知更鸟：**你是想说…事情不应该是这样的，对吧？

**Sunday:** Exactly, but ironically, people don't think there's anything wrong with it, because they uphold the illusory notion of self-value, and even the weak believe in it.

> **星期日：**正是。然而讽刺的是，人们不觉得这么做是错的。因为他们始终将那虚无缥缈的「自我价值」奉为圭臬，就连弱者也是这么认为。

**Sunday:** Survival of the fittest... that's where all the tragedies in the world come from. People come to the sweet dream in Penacony to escape from that reality and find solace.

> **星期日：**适者生存，弱肉强食…世间的一切悲剧皆源于此。而匹诺康尼之所以为美梦，正是因为它为任何想要从中脱身的人提供了一片安眠之地……

**Sunday:** No tragedies exist here, only happiness, although in its nascent form. Isn't that the same paradise we yearn for in our dreams?

> **星期日：**这里没有悲剧，只有幸福——虽然只有雏形，但这不正是我们心目中乐园的样子么？

**Robin:** ...

> **知更鸟：**……

**Robin:** Perhaps that man is just an exception. Let's not jump to conclusions. We should experience the Dreamscape ourselves, just as I did at Dreamflux Reef.

> **知更鸟：**也许刚才那位先生只是特例吧。在得出结论前，我想还是能在梦境里亲眼看看为好，就像流梦礁那时一样。

**Sunday:** Yes, seeing is believing. I'll accompany you. The Dreammaster hasn't shown up yet, so we have some time for a stroll.

> **星期日：**嗯，眼见为实，我陪你一起。梦主还未大驾光临，我们还有时间到处转转。

### Learn more about the dream before the Dreammaster's arrival

> *The drunken guest stumbles unsteadily as they disappear into the depths of the dream. As you watch his figure from behind, you fall into deep reflection: Is it really right to rely on something as intangible and invisible as a fantasy as your only option in life? Seeing your concern, Sunday suggests exploring the area before the Dreammaster arrives... Perhaps, there will be an answer to your doubts to be found?*

[Recap] Robin encountered a cheerful guest who could not bear the dullness of real life and would rather devote her brief existence entirely to the pleasures of dreams.

*(Talk to Cheerful Guest)*

**Cheerful Guest:** Glad to meet you again, Robin! How are the preparations for the Charmony Festival coming along? We're all so excited about it!

> **欢快的宾客：**知更鸟小姐！我们又见面了。谐乐大典的准备还顺利吧？我们都很期待呢。

**Robin:** It's going smoothly. Thanks for making the trip to join the Festival.

> **知更鸟：**很顺利，谢谢你，也辛苦你不远万里前来参加谐乐大典。

**Cheerful Guest:** You're too kind, Robin. It's a pleasure to have guests from all over the universe celebrating day and night!

> **欢快的宾客：**哈哈，知更鸟小姐真是太客气了。能和宇宙各地的客人们一起没日没夜地狂欢，哪里辛苦！

**Cheerful Guest:** I can't stand being lonely or bored, so this jubilant Dreamscape is perfect for me.

> **欢快的宾客：**我讨厌孤独，忍受不了无聊的生活，而这片充满乐子的梦境正适合我。

**Robin:** But if this went on forever... would it get boring too?

> **知更鸟：**要是这样的日子持续到永远…你会觉得厌倦吗？

**Cheerful Guest:** Nah, not at all! Who would get tired of having so much fun?

> **欢快的宾客：**当然不觉得！谁会嫌快乐太多呢？

**Cheerful Guest:** Every day, you get to wear fancy clothes, explore all sorts of dream bubbles, indulge in delicious food without gaining weight, and you never get old or sick... As long as you can afford a room, this place is the ultimate paradise!

> **欢快的宾客：**每天都能穿漂亮衣服，随心所欲地体验各式各样的梦泡，怎么吃都不会胖，不会生病，不会变老…只要付得起房费，这里就是最棒的乐园！

**Robin:** But... you know that only a few things can be brought back from the Dreamscape to reality, right?

> **知更鸟：**但…你应该也知道，能从梦境里带回现实的东西非常有限。

**Cheerful Guest:** That's exactly why I don't plan on bringing anything back! Just enjoying the dream itself is good enough for me! I mean, I'm not one of those long-living species. I only have around sixty or seventy years in this lifetime, and there's so much to worry about... Being happy here is pure bliss.

> **欢快的宾客：**所以我就不带回去嘛！在梦里过过瘾足够了。我不是长生种，一辈子也就六七十年，要顾及的东西又太多…能开开心心过好日子不容易了。

**Cheerful Guest:** Only in this sweet dream can I truly feel like I'm in control of my life and fate... Who would want to go back to reality after experiencing this bliss?

> **欢快的宾客：**只有在这片美梦里，我才能做自己的主人，真正地掌控自己的生活…尝过这种甜头，哪里还会想回去呢？

**Robin:** ...I see. I genuinely wish you all the happiness in the world.

> **知更鸟：**…我明白了，衷心祝愿你能过得幸福。

**Cheerful Guest:** And I wish you a fantastic performance, Robin! I'm off to the Blue Hour for the ball. See you later!

> **欢快的宾客：**也祝知更鸟小姐的演出圆满成功！我先去蓝调的时刻参加舞会了，回见。

**Robin:** *sigh*

> **知更鸟：**唉……

**Sunday:** Seems like that guest's perspective didn't resonate with you either.

> **星期日：**看来方才那位宾客的说辞也没能让你满意。

**Robin:** She had a valid point, and I could sense her genuine happiness. It's just that...

> **知更鸟：**不，她说的有道理，我也能感觉到她打心底里感到幸福，只是……

**Sunday:** What you're trying to say is... she thinks she's in control of her life, but in reality, she's just escaping from reality and seeking solace in this sweet dream. Once she steps out of this sanctuary, everything will be lost.

> **星期日：**你是想说…她以为自己成为了生活的主人，可也不过是抛弃现实逃遁到美梦中，失去庇护便会瞬间现出原形。

**Robin:** Well, she did make mention of being able to "afford a room"... didn't she?

> **知更鸟：**嗯。毕竟，她也提到了「房费」…不是吗？

**Sunday:** However, the paradise in our dreams... it doesn't have to end.

> **星期日：**但我们心目中的那座乐园…不应该有终点。

**Robin:** No, and the paradise we yearn for... shouldn't be just a fleeting dream either.

> **知更鸟：**我们心目中的那座乐园…也不应该只是一场虚无缥缈的梦。

### Continue to learn more about the dream before the Dreammaster's arrival

[Recap] Robin then met a quiet guest who had lost everything in war. His body was now fixed within the Dreampool, sustained by life support. To him, even a false dream was paradise.

*(Talk to Quiet Guest)*

**Quiet Guest:** ...

**Robin:** The scenery in this Dreamscape is truly breathtaking, isn't it?

> **知更鸟：**这片梦境的风景真的很美，对吧？

**Quiet Guest:** Oh, Robin? Can't believe I'm meeting you in person here... What an honor.

> **安静的宾客：**哦？竟然是知更鸟小姐…真是荣幸。

**Quiet Guest:** You're right. Even though time stands still in this Dreamscape, it always feels fresh... I find something new every time.

> **安静的宾客：**你说得对。虽然梦境里的时间不会流动，但常看常新…我每次都会有些新的感触。

**Robin:** A philosophical mind... I hope I'm not intruding.

> **知更鸟：**一位善于思考的哲学家…但愿我没有打扰到您。

**Quiet Guest:** Not at all. With little time left, I yearn for meaningful conversations, especially with someone as esteemed as you. Do you mind if we chat?

> **安静的宾客：**不会，我时日无多，反倒希望有人能陪我说说话——更何况是像您这样尊贵的女士。方便的话，想聊聊吗？

**Robin:** It's my pleasure. No need to be formal — just speak your mind. You said "with little time left"... Please forgive me for being blunt, but is that why you came to Penacony?

> **知更鸟：**乐意效劳，那就随便聊聊吧。您方才提到「时日无多」…恕我冒昧，您是为了这个才来到匹诺康尼的吗？

**Quiet Guest:** Hehe, yeah... I was part of a war, and while escaping from the Sukhousian mothership, I got exposed to some radioactive materials.

> **安静的宾客：**呵呵，是啊…我上过战场，从苏克乌的母舰里爬出来的时候，顺便在脑袋里塞了点带辐射的铁疙瘩。

**Quiet Guest:** And then? All my comrades died, and my hometown was wiped out by neutron bombardments. I couldn't bear to live with everything I knew gone... That's when I heard about a possible solution here, so I came.

> **安静的宾客：**还有呢？战友全走了，故乡也在几场中子辐射轰炸里融化了。哼，全没了，实在是活不下去啊…但听说这地头还有人有法子，我就过来了。

**Robin:** How heart-wrenching... I hope The Family has been able to help you.

> **知更鸟：**太遗憾了…希望家族能为你排忧解难。

**Quiet Guest:** They have, and I'm truly grateful for that. They provided me with a comfortable room, the most advanced life-support devices in the Cosmos, and a stellar team of caregivers.

> **安静的宾客：**家族确实帮了我许多，我要对此表示万分感谢。他们为我安排了一个舒适的房间、全银河最先进的维生装置，还有足足一个加强排的护理师。

**Quiet Guest:** My physical body is now in the Dreampool, sustained by life support. The me you see here is whole, rational, and no different from any other person... but I can't say the same for the "me" in the hotel room.

> **安静的宾客：**现在我的身体固定在入梦池中，依靠维生装置存活。你见到的我是完整的，神智清醒，和正常人没有区别…但酒店里的我不是这样。

**Quiet Guest:** My true appearance... Well, I hope you never have to witness it, Robin.

> **安静的宾客：**真实的模样…呵，知更鸟小姐，希望你永远不会见着。

**Robin:** So... you'll be living forever in this Dreamscape, right?

> **知更鸟：**所以…你要永远活在这片梦境里了，对吗？

**Quiet Guest:** Heh, just being able to live at all is good enough for me. Whether it's in this Dreamscape or not, well, I don't really have much say in the matter.

> **安静的宾客：**呵呵，能活着就是万幸了。梦不梦的无所谓，毕竟我没得选。

**Quiet Guest:** My world has been torn apart, and my life could end any second. So... even if this whole place is an illusion, it's still my paradise, and I'll treasure every moment I spend here.

> **安静的宾客：**我的世界本来就是残缺的，说不定下个瞬间就会消失不见，所以…就算梦境再怎么虚假，对我来说也是乐园。我珍惜在这里的每时每刻。

**Quiet Guest:** Heh... how I envy those everlasting things...

> **安静的宾客：**哈…真羡慕这些永恒不变的东西啊。

**Robin:** That old man's story... it's so tragic. Fortunately, this sweet dream gives him joyful memories to hold onto for the rest of his life.

> **知更鸟：**那位老人的故事…太令人悲伤了，好在这座美梦确实为他的余生留下了幸福的念想。

**Sunday:** That's precisely why this sweet dream in Penacony exists.

> **星期日：**这就是匹诺康尼的美梦存在的意义。

**Robin:** However, even the sweet dream has its limitations. While it provides solace to the disillusioned, it can't completely eliminate pain in reality.

> **知更鸟：**但美梦终究是有局限的。它只是失意者们避风的港湾，并不能从现实的源头根除痛苦。

**Sunday:** There will be a way out. Penacony is already on the right track.

> **星期日：**总会有办法的——匹诺康尼已经走在正确的道路上了。

### Meet the other "Robin"

> *During your exploration, you encounter an eye-catching woman... She is disguised as you and strutting brazenly down Glaux Avenue. You cannot fathom what kind of scheme she is brewing. For the sake of Penacony and to protect your identity, you must have a firm discussion with her.*

**???:** Look who we have here? A lovely young lady.

> **？？？：**看看——这位可爱的小姐是谁呀？

**Robin:** Wait, is that... me?

> **知更鸟：**咦，那是…我自己？

[Recap] Sparkle reappeared to provoke Sunday and Robin by pointing out the flaws in Penacony's dreamscape, showing that the dreamscape wasn't so beautiful after all. She handed the final two buttons to the siblings.

*(Talk to ???)*

**???:** Brother! What a surprise to see you again.

> **？？？：**哥哥！好巧，我们又见面了。

**Sunday:** Show yourself. Your trick won't work on us.

> **星期日：**现出真身吧，你的诡计于我们无用。

**Robin:** I've heard that a skilled Masked Fool received an invitation too... That must be you, right? Did you enjoy yourself?

> **知更鸟：**我听说有位善于欺诈的假面愚者也收到了邀请…看来就是你了。玩得还愉快吗？

**Sparkle:** Barely. The people here are way too gullible. A little bait is all it takes for them to bite, and they run away at the slightest hint of danger. In other words, they're naive and cowardly!

> **花火：**勉勉强强吧。这里的人都太好骗了，稍微给点甜头就会上钩，有危险的时候又缩得飞快——简单说，就是人傻钱多还怕死呗！

**Robin:** Now that you've had your fill, it'd be wise to leave before it's too late. The music of the Harmony doesn't tolerate discord.

> **知更鸟：**既然你玩够了，那还是趁早离开为好。「同谐」的乐章不能容忍杂音。

**Sparkle:** What? Now that you have the real Robin, I'm useless? Oh, how disheartening! I've done so much for The Family.

> **花火：**怎么？本尊回来了，我就没用了？亏我还帮了家族那么多忙，真是太令人心寒了呀。

**Sparkle:** You should be thanking me because if it weren't for me cleaning up this mess... Penacony would still be in shambles. Don't you think?

> **花火：**你们该好好感谢我，要不是我肯收拾这个烂摊子…现在匹诺康尼早就乱成一锅粥了哦？

**Sunday:** That was a personal request from the head of the Iris Family, and it has nothing to do with us. Step aside and stop causing trouble for the Charmony Festival.

> **星期日：**那是鸢尾花家主的私人请求，与我们无关。退下，别再给谐乐大典添乱了。

**Sparkle:** The Charmony Festival? Heh, you think you can scare me? You think I've no idea what you're planning?

> **花火：**谐乐大典？哼哼，吓唬谁呢，以为我不知道各位在打什么主意吗？

**Sparkle:** I don't care what you're thinking, chicken wing boy, but I'm pretty sure our lovely Robin won't be appearing on stage. After all, you're well aware of what a sorry state this Dreamscape is in under the banner of Harmony...

> **花火：**且不提你是怎么想的，鸡翅膀男孩，我们这位可爱的知更鸟小姐现在应该是打定主意不登台了吧？毕竟你也看到了，这座美梦在「同谐」的运作下有多难看……

**Sparkle:** Penacony, the Land of the Dreams... is this truly the paradise you desire?

> **花火：**「梦想之地」匹诺康尼…你们兄妹二人想要的乐园就长这副模样吗？

**Sunday:** ...Stop it!

> **星期日：**…住口！

**Sparkle:** What's the rush, chicken wing boy? Did I get to you?

> **花火：**急啦，鸡翅膀男孩？戳到你痛处了？

**Robin:** Our paradise is none of your concern, Masked Fool. Leave now, or The Family won't tolerate you anymore.

> **知更鸟：**我们的约定与你无关，愚者。赶紧离开，否则别怪家族不客气。

**Sparkle:** Alright, alright. I'll go, but Robin, I suggest you seriously consider this...

> **花火：**好，好～我走就是啦。不过，知更鸟小姐，我还是得奉劝你再仔细琢磨琢磨——

**Sparkle:** ...Do you really believe those living in dreams can escape pain and find true happiness?

> **花火：**——活在梦里的人们，真的能够远离痛苦，收获真正的幸福吗？

**Robin:** ...

> **知更鸟：**……

**Sparkle:** Well, I've done my part, and now I'm simply waiting for the fireworks to begin.

> **花火：**呵呵，我该做的事都做完了，接下来就只等看烟花咯。

**Sparkle:** Here, the last two gifts for both of you, and don't lose them. If, by some unfortunate chance, the Charmony Festival starts against all odds... remember to use them during the show.

> **花火：**最后的两份礼物就送你们啦。如果谐乐大典还是不幸召开了…千万记得在演出现场使用，可别弄丢了哦。

**Sparkle:** And it'll be thrilling! Bang!

> **花火：**砰——会很刺激的！

### Greet the "Dreammaster"

> *You chase off the Masked Fool who revels in chaos and hear a murmuring of ravens coming from the distant skies... The "Dreammaster" has come, that you are certain. For the sake of Penacony's future, go forth and greet this past benefactor.*

**Sunday:** I heard a raven cawing in the distance... It seems the Dreammaster will arrive soon.

> **星期日：**远方有渡鸦的叫声…看来梦主马上就要到了。

[Recap] Sunday and Robin waited for the Dreammaster. Robin questioned whether the dream granted or stripped away the guests' future. Sunday replied that not everyone could walk toward the future, that some would inevitably fall from reality.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Sunday:** Let's wait here for the Dreammaster to arrive.

> **星期日：**这里正合适，我们就在此等候梦主到来吧。

**Robin:** Okay. By the way, brother, I heard you no longer have a sweet tooth. Back when we were kids, you used to steal my desserts...

> **知更鸟：**嗯。话说回来，哥哥，我听说你现在再也不碰甜食了。明明小时候还经常和我抢餐后甜点……

**Robin:** Seems like a lot has changed during my absence. What exactly happened?

> **知更鸟：**总觉得，我离开的这段时间，很多事都变得不一样了。到底发生了什么？

**Sunday:** Well, someone has to stay awake even in this sweet dream.

> **星期日：**即便在美梦中，也必须有人时刻保持清醒。

**Robin:** But that "someone" doesn't have to be you or anyone in particular. You're carrying too much on your shoulders, brother.

> **知更鸟：**但那个人不应该是你，也不应该是任何特定的某个人。哥哥，你给自己的负担太多了。

**Robin:** The paradise in our dreams... it shouldn't be like this.

> **知更鸟：**我们约定中的乐园…不该是这样的。

**Robin:** Penacony is nothing more than a dream. It can't erase the worries and pain of reality or bring true happiness... It only offers an escape from reality. Nothing more.

> **知更鸟：**匹诺康尼只是一场梦。它无法消除现实中的烦恼和痛苦，给人带来真正的幸福…它能做的无非为人们提供一个逃避现实的去处，但也仅此而已。

**Sunday:** Remember the old man we met earlier? Without this dream, he might have completely lost himself.

> **星期日：**还记得刚才那位老人吗？如果没有这场梦，他可能已坠入万劫不复的境地了。

**Robin:** That might be true, but... even without Penacony, he could have chosen another path. As far as I know, the Intelligentsia Guild has been promoting their rehabilitation techniques for a long time now.

> **知更鸟：**诚然…可即便没有匹诺康尼，他也有可能走上另一种生活。据我所知，博识学会早就在推广相应的康复治疗技术了。

**Robin:** That path may have been more ordinary and challenging, but now he's in a comatose state, and the "sweet dream" has become a sort of end-of-life care. His fate... is sealed.

> **知更鸟：**尽管那种生活会平凡、艰难许多，可现在，他在昏迷中接受名为「美梦」的临终关怀，他的结局…已经注定了。

**Robin:** Is Penacony granting these people a future, or is it taking it away from them?

> **知更鸟：**匹诺康尼究竟是给予了这些人未来，还是夺走了他们的未来？

**Sunday:** Well, don't forget this... not everyone really has a future.

> **星期日：**在那之前，你要知道…并非所有的人都能走向未来。

**Sunday:** The future for humanity is like the sky for birds. People mistakenly believe that flight is inherent to birds because they've never witnessed those birds crashing to their death.

> **星期日：**未来之于人，正如天空之于鸟儿。人们之所以误以为飞翔是鸟类的天性，是因为他们从没见过那些坠亡在地的鸟儿。

**Sunday:** Do you remember how we took in that little Charmony Dove when we were young?

> **星期日：**记得小时候收养的那只谐乐鸽吗？我们是如何对待它的？

**Robin:** Yeah, we took care of it — provided food and water, and groomed its feathers. And later... when I decided to leave Penacony, I opened the cage and set it free.

> **知更鸟：**我们认真将它在鸟笼里养大，每天喂食换水，梳理它的羽毛，后来…决定离开匹诺康尼的时候，我打开笼子的门，让它回到了天空。

**Sunday:** Well, I didn't mention what happened to it in my letters, because I didn't want to upset you.

> **星期日：**担心会让你悲伤，我没有在信件中提及此事。

**Sunday:** Shortly after you left, it crashed to its death right in front of your window.

> **星期日：**你走后不久，它就坠落在了你房间的窗前。

[Recap] Sunday believed that "the future" was simply another name for "self-worth." Though people yearn to be heroes, most will never become one. Yet the weak still deserve the right to a happy life.

**Robin:** ...I had surmised as much. I knew you wouldn't have avoided mentioning the bird for no reason.

> **知更鸟：**…我猜到了。若非如此，哥哥绝不会只字不提它的下落。

**Robin:** Despite that unfortunate outcome, I still believe it was the right decision. Birds aren't meant to spend their lives in cages... They belong in the sky, even if they can't fly.

> **知更鸟：**尽管结局令人遗憾，但我仍然坚信这个选择没错。鸟儿不是为了在笼子里度过一生才破壳而出的…就算它们无力飞翔，天空也是它们的归宿。

**Sunday:** But here's the thing. If there are birds in this world that can never fly, can we really assert that they belong in the sky?

> **星期日：**但这就是问题所在。如果这世上有些雏鸟，终其一生都无法飞翔，我们又怎能断言天空才是它们的归宿？

**Robin:** Are you implying... that the same goes for humans too?

> **知更鸟：**你想说…人类也是如此？

**Sunday:** Let's take the Astral Express as an example. The Nameless made tremendous efforts to bridge worlds, gaining fame across the universe... However, only a few extraordinary individuals can endure such a perilous journey.

> **星期日：**想想星穹列车吧，这正是个好例子。无名客为联结诸界付出了巨大努力，誉满寰宇…然而，能坚持在如此苦旅中奔波的无非寥寥数人，更非等闲之辈。

**Sunday:** That's because the pursuit of the Trailblaze exceeds the capabilities of ordinary humans. Otherwise, why would this Path be filled with broken rails, an abandoned Express, and even a fallen Aeon?

> **星期日：**因为「开拓」的事业绝非凡人能够承载。否则，这条命途又怎会一度落得银轨断绝、列车废弃、星神陨落的下场？

**Robin:** That's just sophistry. If that were true, then only the powerful would have the right to determine the future.

> **知更鸟：**歪理。要是按这个逻辑推导，未来岂不是变成了英雄们的特权？

**Sunday:** Unfortunately, that's exactly what happens. Another name for "the future" is "self-value."

> **星期日：**很遗憾，现实正是如此。「未来」的别名…正是「自我价值」。

**Sunday:** While this world has its fair share of heroes who inspire people and garner admiration for their heroic deeds, the majority of ordinary people will never become heroes in their lifetime.

> **星期日：**这世上固然存在英雄，人们向往、歌颂他们，但绝大多数普通人，终其一生也无法成为英雄。

**Sunday:** Some are born weak and vulnerable, some find themselves trapped in unfortunate circumstances, some fall victim to malice and cowardice. When it comes to survival, everyone is equal, and the weak can only watch as their value gets constantly diminished by external forces.

> **星期日：**有人生来弱小无助，有人陷于后天的不幸，有人向卑劣和怯懦屈服。在生存面前，他们同样平等，只能目视自己的价值不断被外物掠夺。

**Robin:** That's why we should care for the weak and support them as if their suffering were our own... That's what the Odes of Harmony have always taught us.

> **知更鸟：**所以我们才应当对弱者施以关爱和照料，给予充分的援助，如同那是我们自身…「谐乐颂」也始终是这么教导我们。

**Sunday:** While the Harmony holds noble aspirations, the strong will always be strong, and the weak will always be weak, even in this carefree dream... Human nature contains greatness, but it also harbors inherent weaknesses that can't be eradicated.

> **星期日：**「同谐」的志向固然远大，可即便在这无忧无虑的美梦中，也是强者恒强，弱者愈弱…人性如此，它固然存在伟大的一面，却也有无论如何都不可消弭的弱小。

**Sunday:** In the end, if people can't even secure their own survival, they won't care about the illusory future of equality. As long as the law of survival of the fittest prevails... there will always be fledglings crashing to their death.

> **星期日：**究其根本，倘若人们连生存都无法保证，更遑论那虚无缥缈的平等未来。只要世间尚存「自然选择」的法则…就注定会有坠落在地的雏鸟。

**Robin:** But if people don't live for the future, do they merely exist for survival? If even you, my brother, don't believe that the Harmony will save the weak, then which Aeon can make our dreams come true?

> **知更鸟：**如果人们不为未来而活，难道就只是为活而活吗？如果哥哥认为「同谐」也无法挽救弱者的困境，那又有哪位星神能实现我们的理想？

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Sunday:** People often forget that when the first bird took flight, the entire world envisioned a future where...

> **星期日：**人们总是会忘记，曾经，当第一只鸟儿飞上天际，那时整个世界对它的期许……

**Sunday:** No more fledglings would ever crash to their death.

> **星期日：**是自此以后，不再有任何雏鸟坠亡大地。

**Trustful Boy:** Are you reading, sister? What are you reading?

> **天真的少年：**妹妹，你在读书吗？在读什么？

**Guileless Girl:** Mr. Gopher Wood gave me a picture book! It's about the story of the Harmonic Strings!

> **烂漫的少女：**歌斐木先生给了我一本画册！讲的是「谐乐众弦」的故事。

**Guileless Girl:** If I could become a Chordmaster, I'd like to summon... Dominicus the Harmonious Choir! I want to sing with everyone and spread our wishes so that all can feel happiness and joy.

> **烂漫的少女：**如果能成为调弦师，我想要召唤…「齐响诗班」多米尼克斯！我想要和大家一起歌唱，把我们的愿望传递出去，让大家都能感受到幸福和喜悦。

**Trustful Boy:** I see. Then... I would summon the Harmonious Choir, too.

> **天真的少年：**这样啊。那…我也选「齐响诗班」好了。

**Guileless Girl:** Don't you... have a wish of your own, brother?

> **烂漫的少女：**哥哥…没有自己的愿望吗？

**Trustful Boy:** Of course, I do. It's just that... it includes your wish and everyone else's. I long for a true paradise where everyone can find peace.

> **天真的少年：**当然有啊。只是…那其中也包含了你的愿望，大家的愿望。那会是一片真正的乐园，所有人都能从中获得安宁。

**Guileless Girl:** Then let's build a stage there and invite everyone to our performance so that both our wishes come true, through the power of the Harmonious Choir.

> **烂漫的少女：**然后，我们就在其中搭一个舞台，邀请所有人来看我们的演出，这样我和哥哥的愿望都能实现了，就用「齐响诗班」的力量。

**Trustful Boy:** It's a deal then.

> **天真的少年：**好啊，那我们约好了。

**Guileless Girl:** Yeah, it's a deal! But how can I become a Chordmaster...?

> **烂漫的少女：**嗯，拉勾！不过，要怎么做才能成为调弦师呢……

**Trustful Boy:** ...

> **天真的少年：**……

**Sunday:** Maybe you'll have to become a star first.

> **星期日：**也许，要先变成星星才行吧。

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 075　Seabiscuit　奔腾年代

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Dreamflux Reef　|　角色：Trailblazer; Gallagher; Himeko; Firefly; March 7th; Aideen; Argenti; Gopher Wood; Clockie; Origami Bird; Hanu; Welt　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''The Only Path to Tomorrow'' completed

[Recap] Himeko suggested pursuing the Stellaron, which required getting closer to the Grand Theater. March 7th proposed earning qualification through the open audition. After Gallagher took his leave, Firefly joined the team, and your group set out to compete.

8 system hours until Charmony Festival - Dreamflux Reef

**Gallagher:** You're back sooner than I thought... Any results?

> **加拉赫：**回来得比想象中更快啊…有结果了？

**Himeko:** Yes... And now, it's up to us to forge ahead.

> **姬子：**嗯…现在，轮到我们开辟前路了。

**Gallagher:** Hmph, since his last will has now been conveyed, my final mission is complete.

> **加拉赫：**哼，既然他的遗愿已经带到，我的最后一项任务也完成了。

**Gallagher:** But, pardon me if I sound curt — it's good to have determination, but the path Mikhail left for you is not an easy one to tread...

> **加拉赫：**不过，别嫌我说话不好听，有斗志是好事，但米哈伊尔留给你们的路可不好走……

**Gallagher:** Why else would he have chosen to sleep in solitude, staking everything on some Nameless in the future?

> **加拉赫：**否则，他又何必在孤独中睡去，对你们这群未来的无名客孤注一掷呢？

**Gallagher:** But you have the numbers, and in numbers comes strength. So that might just delay your inevitable a little more.

> **加拉赫：**不过你们人多。呵，倒是有机会输得慢点。

**March 7th:** Got any more encouraging words?

> **三月七：**你还挺会鼓励人的哈……

**Himeko:** As I see it, relying on Welt's negotiations alone is far from enough. Regardless of whether the other party will be compliant, negotiating simply allows us to meet them as equals, it won't grant us an upper hand.

> **姬子：**依我看，光靠瓦尔特参与谈判肯定远远不够。且不论对方是否会乖乖就范，谈判这件事本身只能给我们创造一个和对方分庭抗礼的机会，不能带来任何优势。

**Himeko:** Penacony is our rival's home turf, and we already have very few chips left to play with. Rather than idly sit around while The Family's got us blocked off, an offensive approach might be a wiser course of action.

> **姬子：**匹诺康尼是对手的主场，我们可用的筹码本就少之又少。与其在家族的封锁下坐以待毙，不如选择主动进攻。

**Himeko:** We're more familiar with the Stellaron's properties than most, and since it's the key to stabilizing the sweet dream, it's vital to The Family's interests. By attacking their core interests, they're bound to retaliate hastily... and as the saying goes: Haste makes waste.

> **姬子：**我们熟悉星核的特性，而星核作为稳定美梦的核心，对家族至关重要。触碰对方的核心利益，他们必定会急忙反扑…而「急忙」恰恰意味着「破绽」。

**Firefly:** That's right — as long as we pose a threat to the Stellaron, either with words or otherwise, we have a chance at gaining the upper hand.

> **流萤：**没错。只要能威胁到星核，无论是谈判还是战斗，都有机会占据上风。

**Firefly:** But the problem is: On the eve of the Charmony Festival opening, how exactly are we going to get close to the theater? Family security will be airtight, and if we brute force it... Even if we succeed, it's too risky.

> **流萤：**但问题在于，正值谐乐大典召开前夕，我们要如何才能接近大剧院？家族势必会设下重重防守，强行突围…就算能做到，风险也太高了。

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**March 7th:** So... no one's gonna say anything? Then I'll raise my hand — I know the answer to this question!

> **三月七：**诶…居然没人说话吗？那我就举手了！这题我知道！

- ▸ You, of all people?

> ▸ 竟然是你？

**March 7th:** What's that supposed to mean!? You'd better believe it!

> **三月七：**什么话啊？信不信由你！

- ▸ Of course, you do! I never doubted you for a sec.

**March 7th:** The Express crew would've been disbanded long ago if it weren't for me!

> **三月七：**哎～没了我，这个列车组怕是要散啦。

- ▸ Looks like we have one more ace up our sleeves. Time for the masterstroke!

> ▸ 原来我们还有一支奇兵没用…

**March 7th:** Why are you speaking like the general of the Luofu?

> **三月七：**你怎么说话和那位罗浮将军似的……

**March 7th:** So, I heard that before the Charmony Festival begins, there will be a pageant to kick off the festival. It's called the... SoulGlad™ Festivity Auditions or something, and it's going to be held in the Moment of Scorchsand.

> **三月七：**是这样的，我听说谐乐大典开始前，会开放给盛典预热的选秀会场，叫什么…「苏乐达™盛会海选」，就在「热砂的时刻」。

**March 7th:** As long as we clinch the top spot, we'll be able to attain the title of Festive Superstar and be able to personally bask in the graces of Miss Robin... Not that that's important. What's crucial is that we can enter the Grand Theater before the audience arrives!

> **三月七：**只要拿下第一名，我们就能夺得盛会巨星的称号，受到知更鸟小姐的亲自接见…不过这个不重要啦，重要的是我们能比普通观众更早进入大剧院！

**Firefly:** So how do we go about participating in these Festivity Auditions?

> **流萤：**那…要怎么才能参加这个「盛会海选」呢？

**March 7th:** Heh-heh, I've already procured special invite tickets from Miss Robin's fan club!

> **三月七：**嘿嘿，其实我从知更鸟小姐的粉丝群里拿到了几张特邀门票！

**March 7th:** To tell you the truth, I'd been preparing to join the auditions all along. But now it looks like even if I scrape through, I probably still won't have the chance to shake Miss Robin's hand...

> **三月七：**实不相瞒，我一直在筹备参加这场选秀呢。但现在看来，就算能脱颖而出，大概也没机会和知更鸟小姐握手了……

**Gallagher:** So they're still running this thing eh... It was originally just a publicity stunt set up by Mikhail to drum up attention, but it looks like it might be worth a shot.

> **加拉赫：**原来这玩意还在办啊…明明最早是米哈伊尔为了引人注目特意搞的噱头。倒是有一试的价值。

**Himeko:** We'll follow March's plan. Mr. Gallagher, will you be joining us?

> **姬子：**就按小三月说的来吧。加拉赫先生，你会和我们同行吗？

**Gallagher:** ...I'm afraid I won't have the time. As a "virtual character"... I've already completed my final mission. Whether Penacony can awaken from this dream is all down to you.

> **加拉赫：**…恐怕我没那个时间了。作为「虚构人物」…我已经完成了自己的最后一项任务，匹诺康尼能否从梦中醒来，就全看你们的了。

**Himeko:** Should we ever cross paths again, I'd love for you to visit the Express.

> **姬子：**如果还能再见面…请一定要来列车上坐坐。

**Gallagher:** Alright. I'll have to add to that data bank of yours you've got on the Express.

> **加拉赫：**好啊。我会给你们的列车智库再添上几笔的。

**Himeko:** And Miss Firefly, we thank you for all your support. We're faced with a formidable enemy. As long as the Astral Express and Stellaron Hunters' objectives are aligned, we're willing to cooperate with you.

> **姬子：**而流萤小姐，感谢你此前提供的帮助。大敌当前，只要星核猎手和星穹列车的目的一致，我们也愿意与你合作。

**Firefly:** We've already come this far together. I'd like to join you for the rest of your journey on Penacony. I'm pleased that we can finally fight shoulder-to-shoulder — I couldn't ask for a better ending.

> **流萤：**已经走了这么远的路，就让我和你们一起走完匹诺康尼的旅途吧。很高兴最后我们能并肩而立，这确实是最好的结局了。

**Himeko:** This is also the spirit of the Trailblaze. Now, everyone, let's prepare to move out.

> **姬子：**这也正是「开拓」之旅的意义。那我们就出发吧，各位。

[Recap] You and your companions arrived at the live studio, where you were interviewed by a reporter and prepared to participate in the open audition challenge.

**Audition Broadcast:** Ladies and gentlemen, welcome to Scorchsand Hall! This is where challengers thrive and Dreamchasers shine, all under the watchful eye of the entire universe!

> **海选主持人的广播：**女士们先生们，欢迎来到热砂分会场！这里是挑战者的盛宴，逐梦客的华典，全宇宙目光聚集的中心！

**Audition Broadcast:** If you have ambition, dreams, and the drive — dear friends, the next superstar of Penacony's Charmony Festival could be you!

> **海选主持人的广播：**只要你有雄心，有梦想，肯奋斗——亲爱的朋友，下一位匹诺康尼的盛典巨星，就是你！

**March 7th:** Wow! They've really outdone themselves! I'm starting to get excited!

> **三月七：**哇！搞得有模有样的！我都有点激动起来了！

**Audition Broadcast:** Behold, here come four friends with spirits high and full of zeal! Are you aspiring to be the superstars of the festival?

> **海选主持人的广播：**看哪，现在走来的是四位斗志昂扬的朋友！你们想成为盛典巨星吗？

**March 7th:** Uh... Is he talking about us?

> **三月七：**咦…是在说我们吗？

**Audition Broadcast:** Yes, you! There's no doubt about it. I see you have all the potential of a superstar in you!

> **海选主持人的广播：**对！不要怀疑，从你们身上，我看到了巨星的潜质！

**Excited Journalist:** Excuse me, you four, what are you hoping to get out of all this?

**Calm Journalist:** As the last group of contestants, how confident are you in overcoming all of the challenges?

**Underperforming Journalist:** Would you be open to a brief exclusive interview with us? It'll be quick!

The Hunt's Journalist: Your journey is long and fraught with peril, yet under a sky blanketed by banners, you vie for the crown!

### Go to the first stage entrance

> *You successfully made it to the SoulGlad™ Festivity Auditions in Scorchsand Hall. Those who dream of becoming huge stars gather here and compete in a ferocious competition in hopes of becoming the one and only "Festive Superstar." As the sponsor of the Festivity Auditions, the entire venue is covered in SoulGlad™ memorabilia: Beverage advertisements and billboards litter every square inch of the streets, and buildings and statues in the shape of SoulGlad™ bottles surround you as far as the eye can see. Unfortunately, this is not the time to be having fun and playing games... Steel your nerves, use all your strength, and think of a way to speed around this SoulGlad amusement park!*

### Pass the second "School of Acting" test

[Recap] Through expressive body language, you vividly depicted survival in the desert and business struggles, successfully conveying the script's emotions and receiving high praise.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Body Language Judge:** Here, you two are required to skillfully utilize body language to portray the story context I've laid out for you.

> **肢体评委：**在我这，二位需要用精湛的「肢体语言」诠释我描绘的故事情境。

**Body Language Judge:** Picking up from where we left off, a heavy downpour saves you both stranded in the desert. This rain quenches the anger in your heart. You look to your companion, now completely devoid of fighting spirit, wanting to comfort her...

[Recap] The Dreammaster received Robin, Sunday, and Welt, claiming to be unaware of the Stellaron's existence. The Dreammaster asked Sunday to invoke the power of the Harmony to question him and prove his innocence.

**"Dreammaster":** ...You mean to say, that for the longest time, there have been scoundrels who would use the Charmony Festival that I have bequeathed to the masses... as a tool to realize their ambition?

**Robin:** Indeed, Dreammaster. Once the Charmony Festival begins, the Stellaron's powers, along with the song will be broadcast across the entire planet of Penacony... And then, everyone in their dreams will be unable to awaken.

**"Dreammaster":** Hmm, this is indeed surprising to me. The Dreamscape is maintained by the collective effort of the Five Families. If someone were to use the Charmony Festival to recklessly disseminate the power of the Stellaron, this individual must hold a position of great authority...

**Welt:** I'd like to ask: Did you really not know of the Stellaron's existence?

**Welt:** If I have offended, the Astral Express extends its sincere apologies... but the current circumstances are dire, and leave no room for meticulous inquiry. We're doing this out of concern for the Dreamscape's safety... so if you could, please alleviate our concerns.

**Robin:** Dreammaster, it's just to prove that the Charmony Festival has nothing to do with the Stellaron. If we're being overly cautious... I will return to the stage to offer tribute in song, just per the arrangement.

**"Dreammaster":** Hmm...

**"Dreammaster":** Sunday, Robin, I've watched you two grow up, and know your dispositions like the back of my hand. Both of you right now, can surely be lauded as THEIR most devout advocates... I already know your resolve.

**"Dreammaster":** The magnitude of this matter is enormous, and cannot be taken lightly. Since Mr. Yang has asked with such earnestness, I will personally respond in kind. If there is a need, the entire Oak Family will be mobilized to heed your call.

**"Dreammaster":** Sunday, might I ask you to beseech THEM to cast THEIR light unto me, and question me in THEIR stead, so that no lies may be concealed?

**Sunday:** I will do as you command.

**"Dreammaster":** Robin, could I entrust you to be present as a witness, to document the truth, and to proclaim my innocence, so that all slander may be utterly dispelled?

**Robin:** I will do as you command.

**"Dreammaster":** May thy will be carried out on earth... just as it is in the heavens.

**Sunday:** "Oh, Triple-Faced Soul, please sear his tongue and palms with a hot iron, so that he will not be able to fabricate lies and make false vows."

**"Dreammaster":** Let us begin. There is nothing else to prepare.

**Sunday:** Understood.

**Sunday:** "Question: Have you devoted your life to your God, never worshiping other Gods?"

**"Dreammaster":** Naturally.

**Sunday:** "Do you love your God as you do yourself, always heeding THEIR admonishments?"

**"Dreammaster":** Naturally.

**Sunday:** "Have you strayed from the path expected by your God, betraying THEIR name?"

**"Dreammaster":** Never.

**Sunday:** "Have you ever been inordinate with your asks of your God, coveting more than the foundation of the creation itself?"

**"Dreammaster":** Never.

**Sunday:** Then, a final question...

**Sunday:** "Do you swear to fulfill all vows, past, present and future?"

**"Dreammaster":** With the Aeon as my witness, if I do not deliver on my words, or if I renege on my vows, may I be cursed in accordance with divine law.

**Sunday:** ...THEY have seen your faith, and have endorsed your faith. With this, it can be evidenced—

**Welt:** Just a moment.

**Sunday:** What is it, Mr. Yang?

**Welt:** ...I have another question I hope to have answered.

**Welt:** To my understanding, The Family's harmony and prosperity have never relied on so-called "divine laws"...

**Welt:** The "God" you both mentioned... are THEY truly Xipe?

**"Dreammaster":** Mr. Yang should know that those belonging to The Family toil together as if they were kin, embracing solidarity and unity under THEIR Light. All duplicity is laid bare before the Harmony.

**"Dreammaster":** Such a delicate and complex symphony, which other God could perfectly harmonize this if not for the Great One, Xipe?

**Welt:** "Perfectly harmonize"... Therein lies the problem. It isn't an outsider lurking in the shadows who changed the Harmony, but a dissonance that has surreptitiously emerged from within this very symphony itself.

**Welt:** In the distant past, there existed an Aeon. With one flick of the wrist, THEY crafted the laws of the cosmos. THEIR followers formed the Beyond the Sky Choir, spreading solemn and reverent hymns throughout the universe.

**Welt:** Later, THEY fell. The route traversed by this Aeon clashed with the Harmony, ultimately being absorbed and fused into it. The chorus that once reverberated across worlds fell silent, and when it echoed anew, it was transformed into the hymn of Harmony...

**Welt:** Though an Aeon may perish, paths with no masters still linger. In the all-forgiving Harmony... echoes of bygone dissonance may subtly arise.

**"Dreammaster":** Mr. Yang...

**"Dreammaster":** Being overly astute can be a detriment, especially when you find yourself alone, and without allies.

**Welt:** Hmph... so this is how it is.

**"Dreammaster":** For the sake of our grand cause, Sunday, please afford these two an opportunity to rest.

**Robin:** What...?

**Sunday:** Sorry, Robin, it's just you... I did not wish for you to know this. It's a pity that things have turned out this way.

**Robin:** So this is the true reason I can't sing? The shadow that envelops Penacony is actually...

**Himeko:** So you've decided to resurrect a dead Aeon? No one's ever done such a thing.

> **姬子：**你们打算复活一位已死的星神？从来没有人做到过这件事。

**Sunday:** If Miss Himeko is interested, let's draw back the veil and speak candidly. I've always firmly believed that people can understand one another through peaceful means.

> **星期日：**既然姬子小姐有兴趣，我们就打开天窗说亮话吧。我始终认为，人们可以通过和平的方式理解彼此。

**Sunday:** I'm willing to divulge the unembellished truth as to the intentions of the Order's Pathstriders, so that you will make better judgment for the Astral Express, for Penacony, and for this stretch of the universe. Words can hardly do justice to the beauty of that ideal...

> **星期日：**我愿意将「秩序」行者的志向如实告知各位，以便你们做出对星穹列车，匹诺康尼，和这片宇宙更好的判断。语言苍白无力，难以描绘出那理想的面貌……

**Sunday:** So come with me, everyone. Let us retrace our steps and see once again where this road leads.

> **星期日：**所以随我来吧，各位。让我们一起重走来时的路，再看看这路将要通向何方。

[Recap] Everyone entered Sunday's inner world, where he used his own experiences to help the Crew understand his beliefs on a more direct level.

> **三月七：**咦，他怎么消失了？

> **星期日：**欢迎，这里不是匹诺康尼梦境的任何一角。而是我的内心世界。面前的景象之所以没有变化，是因为各位的意识提取了相似的概念予以补全。

> **姬子：**你…也对瓦尔特做了同样的事吗？

**Sunday:** It's a "tuning" process, stronger in effect and more draining on the mind. (Trailblazer) has experienced it before, so he/she should understand what it entails.

> **星期日：**这是一种「调律」，效果更强，也更费神。开拓者此前经历过，他/她应该能明白。

**Sunday:** Tuning allows you to intuitively grasp my feelings, which also means that I cannot hide anything from you.

> **星期日：**透过调律，各位可以更直观地理解我的情绪，这也意味着我将对你们毫无隐瞒。

**Sunday:** Now, everyone, please look at the huge screen. The road we once took begins here.

> **星期日：**接下来，我想请各位观看大屏幕，我们来时的路，就从这里开始。

[Recap] He recounted a childhood memory of rescuing a baby bird, which led to his reflections on fate and choice.

**Sunday:** From this point on, you will witness the numerous decisions I've faced. I've selected a portion of these to share with you.

> **星期日：**从这里开始，各位将看到我经历过的诸多抉择。我选取了其中一部分与你们分享。

**Sunday:** I believe after going through similar predicaments, you'll be able to better understand my thoughts.

> **星期日：**我想，在经历了共同的困境后，各位一定能够理解我的想法。

**Sunday:** Let's begin. The first decision: A story about a baby bird.

> **星期日：**开始吧。第一个抉择，与一只雏鸟的故事有关。

[Recap] As children, Sunday and his sister took in a baby bird, but their well-meaning decision ultimately led to tragedy.

**Sunday:** This story happened when Robin and I were very young. We were victims of the Stellaron Disaster, and The Family's Mr. Gopher Wood — who would later become the Dreammaster of Penacony — saw that we siblings had no one to turn to and took us in.

> **星期日：**这则故事发生在我和知更鸟很小的时候。我们是星核之灾的受害者，家族的歌斐木先生——也就是日后的匹诺康尼梦主——见我们兄妹两人无依无靠，便收养了我们。

**Sunday:** Later on, Robin and I lived a time with nary a care in the world. One day, after dinner, while my younger sister and I were lounging about in Mr. Gopher Wood's yard, we spotted a fledgling Charmony Dove all on its own.

> **星期日：**那之后，我和知更鸟度过了一段相对无忧无虑的时光。某一天的晚餐后，我和妹妹在歌斐木先生的庭院里意外发现了一只孤单的小谐乐鸽。

**Sunday:** That baby bird was tiny, it didn't even have all of its feathers, and it couldn't sing. When we found it, it was already on its last breath, having fallen into a shrub — probably abandoned by its parents.

> **星期日：**那只雏鸟还小，连羽毛都没长齐，也不会唱歌。我们发现时，它落在草丛中奄奄一息，似乎是被父母遗弃了。

**Sunday:** We decided to build a nest for it right there and then. However, thinking back, that winter was unusually cold, with fierce winds at night in the yard, not to mention the many poisonous bugs and wild beasts in the vicinity...

> **星期日：**我们当即决定为它搭建一座鸟巢。可思来想去，那年的冬天很冷，庭院夜间的风很大，还有许多毒虫和野兽出没……

**Sunday:** It was clear that if we left the fledgling in the yard, it stood no chance of surviving until spring. So, I suggested we take it inside, place it on the shelf by the window, and asked the adults to fashion a cage for it.

> **星期日：**毫无疑问，如果将雏鸟留在庭院，它绝无可能坚持到春天。于是我提议带它回去，放在窗前的木架上，并拜托大人造个鸟笼。

**Sunday:** We decided that when it regained its strength enough to spread its wings, we would release it back into the wild. The tragic part — something that we'd never considered — was that this bird's fate had already been determined long before this moment...

> **星期日：**我们约定，等到它能依靠自己的力量展翅翱翔时，再将它放回天空。可遗憾的是，我们万万没有想到，这只鸟儿的命运早在那之前就已经注定——

**Sunday:** Its destiny was determined by our momentary whim.

> **星期日：**它落得何种下场，只在我们的一念之间。

**Sunday:** Now, I pass the power of choice to you all. Faced with this situation, what choice would you make?

> **星期日：**现在，我将把选择的权利交给各位。面对此情此景，你们会如何抉择？

**Sunday:** Stick to the original plan, and build a nest with soft net where the Charmony Dove fell?

> **星期日：**是采取我们最初的方案，用软垫在原地为小谐乐鸽打造鸟巢？

**Sunday:** Or build a cage for it, and feed it, giving it the utmost care from within the warmth of a home?

> **星期日：**还是选择为它打造鸟笼，在温暖的屋宇中精心饲养鸟儿？

**Sunday:** I eagerly await your answer.

> **星期日：**我期待你们的答案。

**Firefly:** Even if I was gonna release it back into the sky, it'd have to be strong enough to fly first. If I left it where I found it... I fear it'd never get the chance to fly ever again.

**Sunday:** From what I've observed, there are at least three predators in that yard that prey on small birds: The Vossickle Scorpion, Asdana Wolverine, and the Huntington Winged-Snake...

> **星期日：**就我观察到的结果，至少有三种以小型鸟类为食的野兽在那片庭院中出没：伏氏镰尾蝎、阿斯德纳狼獾，还有亨廷顿翼颈蛇……

**Sunday:** Even if they shy away from humans, these animals are still near-apex predators in a fenced location like a yard. In such a location, only one fate awaits that little Charmony Dove: A painful death.

> **星期日：**尽管无法伤及人类，但这些生物在小小的一方庭院里仍是上位掠夺者。在这种地方，等待那只小谐乐鸽的命运只有一种——痛苦的死亡。

**Sunday:** I'm happy to see that you made a choice similar to ours. If your mind is made up, let me reveal the outcome of this choice.

> **星期日：**很高兴看见你和当年的我们做出了同样的选择。既然你心意已决，就让我来揭晓这一选择的结果吧。

**Sunday:** We passionately nursed it back to health, preparing only the best food for it every day. We even preened its feathers. Later, on the day that Robin left Penacony, we opened the cage door and let it fly back into the sky.

> **星期日：**我们认真将它抚养大，每天都备好精致的食料，还为它梳理羽毛。后来，在知更鸟离开匹诺康尼的那天，我们打开笼子的门，让它回到天空。

**Sunday:** I watched it for a long while by the window, probably about three or so days. In those three long days, the little Charmony Dove tried again and again to spread its wings to fly into the sky, but fell to the ground... only to keep trying.

> **星期日：**我在窗前看了它很久，大概三天左右吧。在那漫长的三天里，小谐乐鸽一次又一次地试图展开翅膀，飞向天空，又落向地面…周而复始。

**Sunday:** Finally, on the hundred and thirty-seventh attempt, it succeeded. But its attempt did not go perfectly... After flying unsteadily for a while, it fell to the ground, unable to grasp the direction of the air currents.

> **星期日：**最后，在第一百三十七次尝试时，它终于成功了。但这一次尝试并不圆满…在摇摇晃晃地飞行了一段时间后，它因无法准确掌握空气的流向而坠落在地。

**Sunday:** The fall shattered its wings. It writhed helplessly in my embrace, but it was all for naught, finally succumbing to a painful demise. And in that instant, our tender care, our given love and hopes... they all became the inevitable push that sent it to its death.

> **星期日：**这一坠彻底摔断了它的翅膀。它在我的怀中苦苦挣扎，却无济于事，最终迎来痛苦的死亡。而我们的悉心照料、寄予的关爱和厚望，在这一瞬间都成了葬送它的推手。

**Sunday:** I deeply regret the choices we made... Next, let us head to the second decision. This time... it's the story of a Dreamchaser.

> **星期日：**对于我们做出的选择…我深表遗憾。接下来，让我们进入第二个抉择吧。这次…是一位逐梦客的故事。

[Recap] Sunday recalled once sheltering a Dreamchaser, only for that kindness to spiral into greater tragedy.

**Sunday:** This story happened when I was appointed as Bronze Melodia — a position exclusive to the Oak Family, charged with listening to the problems and vexations of Dreamscape residents, and providing them with the relevant guidance.

> **星期日：**这则故事发生在我任职「铎音」期间。铎音是橡木家系特有的职位，负责聆听梦境居民的困惑和迷茫，给予他们相应的指导。

**Sunday:** It was during that period that I had the opportunity to hear voices from all corners of the Dreamscape: Joy, sorrow, arrogance, regret... The complex tapestry of human nature that formed the world, and I was fortunate to catch a glimpse of it.

> **星期日：**也正是在那段时间，我得以听取来自梦境各方的声音：有欢喜、有忧愁、有傲慢、有悔恨…复杂的人性编织起世界，而我有幸窥见其中一斑。

**Sunday:** He was a Dreamchaser, and an illegal stowaway. Just like the rest of them, he came to Penacony in search of a better life, except that, to most people, the price he paid... I suppose you could say it was "everything".

> **星期日：**那是一位逐梦客，也是一名偷渡犯。和其他所有人一样，他怀着对美好生活的向往来到匹诺康尼，只是他付出的代价对于凡人而言…也足以称作「倾尽一切」。

**Sunday:** He told me "I sold everything I could at home." — the house, the land, even his two children... He said he could not afford to raise them, and that at least they could eat if they lived as slaves.

> **星期日：**他这么对我说，「把家里能卖的全都卖了」——房子，土地，还有两个孩子…他说自己无力抚养他们，做奴隶至少还能混口饭吃。

**Sunday:** He had a plan in place: He would buy back his children once he had made his fortune, and enjoy Penacony's beautiful dream with them. Alas, his plan to smuggle himself was somewhat clumsy — and he was sniffed out by those pigheaded Hounds.

> **星期日：**他也做好了计划，准备发家致富后就把孩子赎回，共享匹诺康尼的美梦。只可惜他的偷渡计划有些笨拙，被那群冥顽不灵的猎犬们嗅出了气息。

**Sunday:** After hearing the Dreamchaser's story, I immediately appealed to the Bloodhound Family to cease their pursuit. That way, at least he could live peacefully. But I was still too naive to the ways of the world — I did not anticipate that what I thought was a kind gesture would later lead to dire consequences...

> **星期日：**听过这位逐梦客的故事，我当即请求猎犬家系停止追捕，这样他便能安心生活。可惜我的眼界太浅，不曾想这自以为是的善举竟在日后酿成了苦果……

**Sunday:** I'll tell you the outcome soon. For now, I'd like you all to make a choice—

> **星期日：**答案稍后公布。现在，我希望各位做出选择——

**Sunday:** Will you do as I did, and try to convince the Bloodhound Family to stop their pursuit, so that the Dreamchaser may live peacefully and realize his wishes?

> **星期日：**是做出和我一样的决定，说服猎犬家系停止搜查，以便那位逐梦客能够生存下去，实现他的愿望？

**Sunday:** Or will you remain silent, leaving him to languish while the Hounds are hot on his heels, until his inevitable judgment arrives?

> **星期日：**还是保持沉默，任凭他在猎犬的追踪下苟延残喘，直到注定的裁决来临？

**Sunday:** I look forward to everyone's decisions. Who knows, perhaps they might even alter the outcome of this tragedy.

> **星期日：**期待各位的选择，也许能够扭转那桩悲剧也说不定。

[Recap] The Dreammaster then revealed the truth behind Robin's injury: She was wounded by gunfire while spreading the ideals of Harmony on the battlefield. This revelation shook Sunday's belief in the Harmony, planting doubt in his heart.

**Sunday:** This story happened the day I was appointed the Oak Family Head. At that time, Mr. Gopher Wood was the current Dreammaster, and as per his wish, we had a private conversation.

> **星期日：**这则故事发生在我就任橡木家主当天。那时歌斐木先生已成了如今的梦主，应他要求，我们进行了一场私人对谈。

**Sunday:** What surprised me was that the Dreammaster had only come to deliver a letter to me. He let me read its contents. It was a letter... from my sister.

> **星期日：**令我诧异的是，梦主只为我捎来了一封信。他让我读读信中的内容，而那信件…来自我的妹妹。

**Sunday:** The letter contained the usual pleasantries, anecdotes from her travels — nothing out of the ordinary. Just as I started wondering how this letter related to our discussion, the Dreammaster began to speak.

> **星期日：**信中无非是日常的寒暄，捎带她游历诸界的种种见闻。正当我心生疑惑，这封信与会谈有什么关系时，梦主开口了。

**"Dreammaster":** "Do you know who wrote this letter?"

> **「梦主」：**「你知道么，这封信出自何人之手？」

**Sunday:** "My sister, of course. But why would you personally visit me to hand me a letter from my sister containing mere trivialities?"

> **星期日：**「当然是家妹的手笔。梦主为何要为我兄妹的日常琐事登门拜访？」

**"Dreammaster":** "To help you grasp the full scope of this issue. Do you know where Robin is at this moment?"

> **「梦主」：**「为了让你深入了解此事。你知道知更鸟如今正身在何方么？」

**Sunday:** "From what the letter indicates... She must be in Kasbelina-VIII, correct? She's touring there right now..."

> **星期日：**「依信中内容来看…应该是卡斯别林亚特-Ⅷ吧？她正在那里巡游……」

**"Dreammaster":** "Correct. Has she mentioned anything about a stray bullet?"

> **「梦主」：**「不错。她可提到身中流弹一事？」

**Sunday:** "A stray bullet? What..."

> **星期日：**「流弹？什么……」

**"Dreammaster":** "A war has broken out on that planet. It is because of this very reason that Robin chose this destination... To spread the word of the Harmony, and to save the lives of that planet, she personally made for the frontlines."

> **「梦主」：**「那颗星球爆发了战争。正因如此，知更鸟才会将那里选作目的地…为了传扬『同谐』，挽救星球上的生命，她亲自奔赴前线了。」

**"Dreammaster":** "She hoped to ease the people's suffering with song, and was willing to brave mortal danger to deliver the IPC's medical supplies... Unfortunately, stray bullets show no such compassion."

> **「梦主」：**「她希望用歌声平复人们的痛苦，也愿意用自己的血肉之躯，为公司的救援物资输送提供援护…可惜，流弹无情。」

**Sunday:** "Is she alright!?"

> **星期日：**「她现在怎么样了？！」

**"Dreammaster":** "If the operation was successful, she should probably be recovering in the field hospital."

> **「梦主」：**「若手术成功，现在应该在野战医院里休养吧。」

**"Dreammaster":** "By the Aeon above, the bullet struck her neck directly... Yet, possibly as a reward for her consistent deeds of Harmony, it didn't hit any vital arteries. Once you've attended to your outstanding tasks, it'd be advisable to write her back as soon as possible."

> **「梦主」：**「星神在上，那枚子弹直接打进了她的脖子…不过，或许是平日践行『同谐』善举的回报，子弹没有伤及命脉。等你处理完琐事，尽快给她回信为好。」

**Sunday:** "Those... damned savages! I'll pack my bags right away... My gratitude for bringing this to my attention, Mr. Gopher Wood!"

> **星期日：**「那群…该死的野蛮人！我现在就收拾行李…感谢您告诉我这些，歌斐木先生！」

**Sunday:** Now, you understand why she always wears such elaborate neck ornaments, don't you?

> **星期日：**现在，你们知道她为何要时常佩戴那样繁琐的颈饰了吧？

**March 7th:** How could this happen... Miss Robin...

> **三月七：**怎么会这样…知更鸟小姐……

**Sunday:** It's all in the past, so please don't worry. I share this in the meager hope that you will understand the Harmony's limitations and predicament. As grandiose as "the strong defending the weak" sounds, many times, it is nothing more than wishful thinking.

> **星期日：**已经是过去的事了，各位不必在意。我分享此事，也只是希望你们理解「同谐」的局限和困境。「以强援弱」的愿景再伟大，多数时候也只是一厢情愿。

**Sunday:** Likewise, I've prepared one last question, one last choice. But rest assured, this choice will not have any grave consequences.

> **星期日：**同样地，我为各位准备了最后一道课题，最后一次选择。但请放心，这次选择不会带来任何沉重的结果。

**Sunday:** Because this is merely a figment of imagination, a nightmare that has haunted me through countless nights. If you ever had the opportunity to make a choice like I did...

> **星期日：**事实上，什么事都不会发生。因为这只是一个空想，一道纠缠了我无数个夜晚的梦魇——如果各位有机会像我一样做出选择……

**Sunday:** Would you still support Robin's journey on the Path of Harmony?

> **星期日：**你们还会支持知更鸟踏上「同谐」的旅途么？

### Decide Robin's destiny

*(Talk to March 7th, optional)*

**March 7th:** I can't believe that happened to Miss Robin... "The strong defending the weak" is a great mantra, but if I had to pay such a price... I, I don't know what I'd do...

*(Talk to Himeko, optional)*

**Himeko:** I often feel like I've dreamt of similar scenes on certain nights. In the dream, I see blurry faces. I don't know who they are, but I sympathize with all of them, fighting for survival against some unfathomable force...

> **姬子：**总觉得，我在某些夜晚梦见过类似的场面。在梦中，我看见一群模糊的面孔，虽然不知道她们是谁，但我们情同家人。一直在与某种超然的存在抗争……

**Himeko:** Their confusion and fear are lucid to me... But I also remember they chose never to give up — just like Miss Robin.

> **姬子：**我清楚地记得她们的迷茫、恐惧…但也记得，在那梦里她们从未选择放弃——就像知更鸟小姐一样。

**Himeko:** (Trailblazer), if Mr. Sunday's question leaves you puzzled, you should find the answer from your own experiences.

> **姬子：**开拓者，如果你对星期日先生的问题感到迷茫，就从自己的经历中寻找答案吧。

**Himeko:** With each Trailblaze, dangers and tribulations will surely follow, but would you ever back away? Would you stop March and Dan Heng from reaching their next destination? I believe you have an answer of your own in your heart.

> **姬子：**每一次的「开拓」之旅都伴随着艰难险阻，但你真的会打退堂鼓吗？会阻止三月和丹恒前往下一站吗？我想你心中一定有属于自己的回答。

*(Talk to Firefly, optional)*

**Firefly:** Miss Robin's courage is admirable. And here I was, thinking she was just another superstar celebrity...

> **流萤：**知更鸟小姐的勇气令人敬佩，我本以为她只是在舞台上绽放光芒的明星……

**Firefly:** But the fact that she's also Mr. Sunday's younger sister... No, I doubt he'd wish harm on his own flesh and blood, no matter how grand the ambition.

> **流萤：**但她也是星期日先生的妹妹…即便拥有再伟大的理想，他一定也不希望至亲为此献身吧。

- ▸ Choose "Support Robin to go on her journey"
- ▸ Choose "Stop Robin from going on her journey"

[Recap] You were then asked to decide whether Robin should continue her journey, a choice through which Sunday posed a final challenge to your ideals.

*(Regardless of choice made)*

Will you make this choice?

- ▸ Make the decision.
- ▸ Think it over some more.

**Sunday:** Heh... I see.

> **星期日：**呵…原来如此。

[Recap] He laid out the vision of his seven-rest-days utopia, aiming to replace the Harmony with the Order, and sought to persuade the Astral Express crew to accept his philosophy.

**Sunday:** I am now aware of everyone's stances.

> **星期日：**各位的主张，我已明了。

**Sunday:** Raising these questions merely serves to illustrate one point: The plight of Penacony cannot be salvaged by the Harmony. The true foundation for a Sweetdream Paradise can only be established through the Order where the strong govern the weak.

> **星期日：**提出这些问题，只是为了阐明一件事：匹诺康尼的困境无法由「同谐」拯救，真正能建立起美梦乐园的——唯有以强制弱的「秩序」。

**Sunday:** I know the suffering of being tormented, the turmoil of losing your way, how sorrow... and even despair, set in when matters don't work out. All of this causes me unending pain, because this is not what "happiness" is at all.

> **星期日：**我晓得人遭受折磨时如何痛苦，迷失道路时如何茫然，事与愿违时又如何沮丧…甚至绝望。这一切都令我痛苦，因为这样根本不能算是「幸福」。

**Sunday:** We must teach the weak how to live a happy life. And this life isn't some noble propriety that the upper crust preaches, but in definitive terms, a way of survival that belongs to everyone.

> **星期日：**我们必须教导弱者如何幸福地生活。而这「生活」并非名流贵族挂在嘴边的讲究，而是绝对意义上的，属于人的生存之道。

**Firefly:** So, what is your definition of living a happy life?

> **流萤：**在你看来…怎样才算是幸福地活着？

**Sunday:** Good question. Human consciousness is fundamentally an illusion, a cage known as "self-worth". People lured in by this illusion, make mistakes, yet still ask that external influences bear the burden.

> **星期日：**好问题。人类的意识本质上是种幻觉，是一座座名为「自我价值」的监牢。人被这幻觉诱导，犯下错误，后果却要由外物承担。

**Sunday:** When one mistake after the next permeates the masses, they become impossible to trace... Thus, the amassing of these individual cages culminate to form a prison, a place dictated only by the rule of "survival of the fittest."

> **星期日：**当一重又一重的错误充满人群，变得无从追溯…这一座座监牢便共同组成了一幢监狱，一条名为「适者生存」的自然法则。

**Sunday:** Nature is always accompanied by predation and sacrifice... Its antithesis is known as Order.

> **星期日：**而「自然」总是伴随着掠夺与牺牲…它的反面，叫做「秩序」。

**Sunday:** That is what I want to do: Unite people's happiness under the banner of Order. They won't need to make bitter choices any longer, nor face the weaknesses of humanity. They can cast aside their primal instincts, to build a haven for mankind.

> **星期日：**我要做的正是这样的事：将众生的幸福归于唯一的「秩序」之下。人们不必再做出苦涩的抉择，不必再直面人性的弱点，抛却野兽的痼习，才能建立属于人的乐园。

**Sunday:** Simply describing thoughts is far too abstract, so allow me to provide a simple example: As you all may know, there are societal norms like "weekends" and "long weekends" that exist on some worlds.

> **星期日：**单单描述思想还是太过抽象，让我举个简单的例子吧。各位也许知道，在某些世界存在着名为「双休日」或「三休日」的社会运作制度。

**Sunday:** During these hard-earned rest days, people are given the chance to extricate themselves from the stresses of everyday life, allowing a certain tranquility to return to their souls.

> **星期日：**在来之不易的休息日里，人们得以从生活的重压中解脱，回归灵魂的平静。

**Sunday:** And it is only on these days, that people do not have to adhere to the law where the strong prey on the weak — they can live out their lives happily during these brief intermissions. It's just a pity... that two or three days are still too fleeting compared to the span of a lifetime.

> **星期日：**也只有在这样的日子里，人们不必面对弱肉强食的法则，能够在这短短数日中「幸福地活着」。只可惜…两三个日夜相较漫长的人生还是太过短暂。

**Sunday:** From where I stand, society's ideal system should be "seven rest days". Following Sunday, there should ensue a second, a third, and indeed an infinite procession of Sundays. This should be the face of the new world — idyllic, eternal, peaceful days.

> **星期日：**在我看来，社会的理想制度应当是「七休日」。在星期日的明天，是第二、第三、乃至永远的又一个星期日——这就是新世界的面貌，无所事事的永恒安宁之日。

**Sunday:** And thus, every person can return to their base selves in this utopia. Some gaze in reverence at the stars, pouring their whole beings into calculating the distance between us and the isolated world of Pegana. Meanwhile, some seek refuge in quiet corners, holding one another, unencumbered by the chains of unwelcome obligations...

> **星期日：**由此，每个人都能在乐园中回归自己原本的位置。有人瞻仰银河，全神贯注地计算孤绝世界「裴伽纳」离我们的距离，有人在角落彼此相拥，不必承担多余的职责……

**Sunday:** There would be no need to bear the hardships of reality. Only in this way can humanity face the inevitable end with the purest of spirit. Living a life of dignity — this is what it is to live in bliss.

> **星期日：**无需再承受现实之苦，唯有如此，人类才能以最高洁的姿态面对命中注定的结局，度过充满尊严的一生。这就是「幸福地活着」。

**Sunday:** Miss Firefly, you who are stricken with Entropy Loss Syndrome — you of all would surely understand this?

> **星期日：**流萤小姐——患有失熵症的你，一定能理解这意味着什么吧？

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**March 7th:** It sounds... like a flawless theory...

> **三月七：**听起来…好像无懈可击啊……

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Firefly:** But... what is the price to attain all this?

> **流萤：**那…这一切的代价是什么？

**Sunday:** The cost is minute, merely a personal... and eternal sacrifice. If this paradise is to be maintained for everyone, someone must remain trapped in solitary wakening, until the end of the cosmos.

> **星期日：**代价微不足道，只是一场属于我个人的…永久殉难。如果要为万众维持这座乐园，总得有一人陷入孤独的清醒中，直到宇宙的尽头。

**Firefly:** Wakening... Which means that this so-called paradise is still a dream. Stepping into this paradise means forsaking reality, correct?

> **流萤：**清醒…也就是说，那乐园仍是一场梦。踏入乐园，便意味着要放弃现实，对么？

**Sunday:** It is not forsaking, but transcending. Flesh, blood, sorrow, weakness. If the physical is the root of spiritual suffering, it is only logical that we defeat it.

> **星期日：**这并非放弃，而是超越。血肉苦弱。如果物质是精神苦难的根源，那我们理应战胜它。

**Firefly:** But in this supposed "bliss," people won't have defeated their demons. The chance to overcome their tribulations would be forever lost to them. In other words... it is an escape.

> **流萤：**但在这样的「幸福」中，人们从未战胜苦难，也永远失去了战胜苦难的机会。换一种说法…这是「逃避」。

**Sunday:** That's another way of understanding it, but there is no shame in escape. On the contrary, the seeds of escape exist in everyone's hearts.

> **星期日：**你可以这么认为，但逃避并不可耻。恰恰相反，每个人心中都有逃避的种子。

**Sunday:** Don't you agree, Miss Firefly? And as to why we sleep? It is because we are afraid to awaken from our dreams.

> **星期日：**流萤小姐不也这么觉得吗，生命因何而沉睡？是因为人们害怕从梦中醒来。

**Sunday:** But this is not in conflict with the grand plan. Only in acknowledging this, can we truly understand the frailty of human nature, and from there, show compassion and protection.

> **星期日：**但这与伟大的事业并不冲突。唯有承认这点，我们才能理解人性的软弱，进而包容，进而庇佑。

**Firefly:** I... admit that you are a born leader. Your perspective on humanity brims with pessimism, yet you express compassion for all, even when your heart pities them.

> **流萤：**我…认可你是一位天生的领导者。你对人类充满悲观，却依然怀抱着否定的心，予以众生平等的怜悯。

**Firefly:** But unlike you — I live for the "self". From my perspective, individuals making choices for themselves is their birthright.

> **流萤：**但我和你不同，我是为「自我」而活的。在我看来，人为自己做出选择，是理所应当的行为，也是他们与生俱来的权利。

**Firefly:** The want to escape may be innate in the weak, but whether they are weak or not... It is not up to another to decide.

> **流萤：**也许逃避是弱者的天性，但谁是弱者…不应由他人来定义。

**Firefly:** Perhaps in your mind, you also view me as "weak"?

> **流萤：**难道在你眼中，我也要被归类为弱者吗？

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Firefly:** Because I don't think so.

> **流萤：**我并不这么觉得。

**Himeko:** Since Miss Firefly has said her piece, the Astral Express will also naturally give you our answer.

> **姬子：**既然流萤小姐已经表明了自己的观点，星穹列车自然也会给出我们的回答。

**Himeko:** We'll leave it to you, (Trailblazer). Just as Mr. Mikhail instructed before: Tell him our choice...

> **姬子：**交给你了，开拓者，就像米哈伊尔先生嘱咐的那样，告诉他我们的选择吧——

*(Begin Sub-Mission Then Wake to Weep)*

## 077　Then Wake to Weep　等醒来再哭泣

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：A Child's Dream　|　角色：Trailblazer; Misha; Himeko; March 7th; Clockie; "Seafarer" Mikhail; Mikhail; Falcon Amundsen; Granholm; Micah; Gallagher; Xipe; Sunday; Razalina; Tiernan

[Recap] Misha entered the dream bubble, returning to his childhood home and gradually recovering his identity through memories. He revealed that he is in fact the childhood echo and successor of the Watchmaker.

Not long ago...

**Misha:** What is this place...?

> **米沙：**请问，这里是……

**Himeko:** Does this place ring any bells, Misha?

> **姬子：**你对这里有印象吗，米沙？

**Misha:** I... don't know, but I feel a sense of deja vu. What is this place?

> **米沙：**我…说不上来。但好像有种似曾相识的感觉。这是哪儿？

**Himeko:** It's the realm within a dream bubble. This was left to the Astral Express by a Nameless, but weirdly when we entered it, it was completely empty.

> **姬子：**这是一枚梦泡，我们的意识进入了其中。这是一位无名客留给星穹列车的礼物，但奇怪的是，我们打开时却发现里面什么都没有。

**Himeko:** Dr. Edward from the Dreamscape Sales Store told me that dreams are formed from memories, and a dream bubble can't take shape if its core is empty.

> **姬子：**梦境贩售店的爱德华医生告诉我，梦境由记忆凝聚而成，如果内核空无一物，梦泡是无法成形的。

**Himeko:** So I thought you might be able to help us in unraveling this mystery, Misha? As a hotel doorman, you know Penacony best among us.

> **姬子：**所以我想，作为酒店门童，星穹列车的朋友中最了解匹诺康尼的人，小米沙，你应该能帮我们解开这个疑惑？

**Misha:** Hmm, I don't know much about dream bubbles, but if you want to figure out what this mansion is... I'll do my best!

> **米沙：**唔，梦泡的事我懂的不多。但如果各位想弄明白这幢大房子是什么…我会努力试试的！

**Himeko:** I'm counting on you then.

> **姬子：**那就拜托你了。

**March 7th:** Himeko... I still don't get it. Why were you so sure that Misha had a connection with this dream bubble?

> **三月七：**姬子姐…我还是很疑惑，为什么你就断定米沙和这枚梦泡有关呢？

**Himeko:** I wasn't sure. It was just a hunch, but since Misha feels familiar with this place... my hunch might be correct.

> **姬子：**我无法断定，只是有些猜测。但既然米沙对这里感到熟悉…说明我的猜测可能是正确的。

- ▸ This place looks a bit familiar...

> ▸ 这个场景有点眼熟…

- ▸ This place feels a bit nostalgic...

> ▸ 这个场景有点怀念…

**Himeko:** Exactly, this is where you and Firefly encountered "Death," which we now know as "Dormancy." Considering its connection to Dreamflux Reef, it's not surprising it appeared here.

> **姬子：**没错，你和流萤小姐就是在这里遭遇了「死亡」——现在该叫它「沉眠」了。考虑到它和流梦礁的关联，出现在这倒是不难理解。

**Himeko:** The question now is — Who brought you here? Based on the clues we have so far, it's unlikely to be that Masked Fool, so identifying them is crucial to us.

> **姬子：**现在的问题是，是谁把你们带进了这里。依照目前的线索来看，可能并不是那位愚者，那么他是谁就十分关键了。

**Himeko:** We're drawing closer to the truth once more. Let's give Misha some time, as I believe he'll unveil the secret of this dream bubble.

> **姬子：**我们已经再次接近真相的核心。给米沙一点时间吧，他一定能为我们揭开梦泡的秘密。

**March 7th:** Alright, but there are doors all over the place... Which one should we choose...? Do you have any idea, Misha?

> **三月七：**好吧。可这里到处都是门，该从哪扇门出去呢…米沙，你知道吗？

**Misha:** Hmm... I guess... maybe this way?

> **米沙：**唔，我想…可能是这边？

### Follow Misha and help him recover his memories

> *Time reverses to the point before you left Dreamflux Reef. Himeko returns from Golden Hour with a powerful deduction: There may be some inextricable link between the empty dream bubble that the Watchmaker is holding and Misha the bellboy. In order to prove it, you take Misha back into Mikhail's dream bubble... If everything goes as expected, the truth may be lurking here in this long dream of the child.*

*(Follow Misha)*

**Misha:** I... I'm not entirely sure, but... let's give it a try.

> **米沙：**我不确定，但…试试看吧。

*(Enter the room at the end of the corridor)*

**March 7th:** Wait, you managed to choose the right door on your first try?

> **三月七：**竟然一下子就选对了？

[Recap] Misha recalled scenes of childhood, sharing memories of the fireplace and the toy room, where the interweaving of memory and reality began to stir deep questions.

*(Approach Misha)*

**Misha:** Weird, this place is quite different from the hotel, but I just... I feel like I've been here before and... even lived here for a while.

> **米沙：**奇怪，这里明明不是酒店，差别也很大，但我总觉得自己来过，而且…住过一段时间。

**Misha:** If I remember correctly, there should be a fireplace down that hallway. Clockie and I used to sit by the fire, listening to the crackling of firewood.

> **米沙：**如果没记错，沿着那条走廊向前，有一座温暖的壁炉。我和钟表小子常常在那烤火，听木柴噼啪作响。

**Misha:** And... And the room on the other side was... the toy room. I loved spreading out all the toys from the box on the floor and making up stories for each of them.

> **米沙：**而另一边的房间是…是放玩具的屋子。我喜欢把箱子里的东西一股脑儿在地上摊开，然后挨个给他们编故事。

**Misha:** Hold on, this doesn't make sense. Didn't I grow up in Dreamflux Reef? So, what is this place...?

> **米沙：**不对，不对，我不是在流梦礁长大的吗？那这里又是……

**Himeko:** This could be a case of amnesia. But don't worry, Misha, it's common for everyone to forget certain aspects of the past. Those memories haven't vanished. They're just lying in the depths of your mind. We can surely get them back.

> **姬子：**这可能是一种名为「失忆」的现象。别担心，小米沙，每个人总会忘记一些过去。但它们并没有消失，只是沉睡在了脑海深处。同样地，我们也能把它们找回来。

> **米沙：**好。那，就去我刚才说的两个房间看看吧。

**Himeko:** That's the key to the mystery, March. Now, take a moment to recall...

> **三月七：**……

**March 7th:** Wait... no way...

> **三月七：**不会吧……

**Misha:** That's the answer, March 7th. This dream bubble is the place where I was born, and I... I'm a dweller in this dream just like a Memory Zone Meme.

> **米沙：**答案就是如此，三月七小姐。这枚梦泡是我诞生的摇篮，我…是一位和忆域迷因无异的梦中人。

**Misha:** I should have stayed here and waited for you, but when reality and memories merged, I unconsciously pushed open that door and left the bubble with Clockie.

> **米沙：**我本应留在梦泡中等待各位到访，但现实和记忆重叠在一起，让我不自觉地推门而出，带着钟表小子离开了这里。

**March 7th:** So, it's not that the Watchmaker's dream bubble is empty, but rather... the stuff inside ran away?

> **三月七：**所以「钟表匠」留下的梦泡空无一物，不是因为没有内容，而是因为…其中的内容擅自离开了？

**March 7th:** And the whistle you heard was the sound of the Express arriving at Penacony?

> **三月七：**你听到的鸣笛声，就是列车抵达匹诺康尼的声音？

> **姬子：**这的确是一种解读。但它背后应该还有一段更为漫长的故事。我想，一切的来龙去脉，迷惑难解之处，还是由他本人来解释吧……

**Himeko:** How about we start with your name? Now, should we call you Misha? Or...

**Misha:** Thank you all for helping me rediscover my true self. Now, please allow me to reintroduce myself.

> **米沙：**感谢各位帮我找回这段漫长的旅程，容我重新做个自我介绍吧。

**Misha:** I was born on Lushaka in the Przesmir system, adopted by seafarers Mikhail and Char. They gave me a treasure, a name that carried their hopes...

> **米沙：**我出生在普热斯米尔星系的露莎卡星，是航海家米哈伊尔先生和夏尔太太的养子。两位老人给了我一件宝物，一个承载了他们希望寄语的名字——

[Recap] Misha's true identity was thus revealed as a fragment of Mikhail the Watchmaker's life. He passed on the spirit of the Trailblaze to you, marking a new chapter in your journey through Penacony.

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** Mikhail Char Legwork, or simply... Misha.

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔，或者更简洁的…米沙（米哈伊尔-夏尔）。

**"The Watchmaker":** If you prefer, you can call me by a more familiar name, the Watchmaker.

> **「钟表匠」：**如果你们希望，用人们更熟知的名字——「钟表匠」来称呼我，也并无不可。

**March 7th:** So, you're the Watchmaker himself?

> **三月七：**原来，你就是「钟表匠」本人？！

**"The Watchmaker":** Unfortunately, that legendary figure is no more. I'm only a reflection of his life.

> **「钟表匠」：**很可惜，那位人人憧憬的美梦大亨早已不在了，我只是他人生的一个缩影。

**"The Watchmaker":** As for the child who has been with you, he's the innocent protagonist of Misha's childhood dream, a friend of Clockie, a young apprentice, and a future mechanic on the Express...

> **「钟表匠」：**而陪伴各位同行至今的这个孩子，是他童年美梦中懵懂无知的主角——「钟表小子」的好朋友，一名年轻的学徒，一位未来的列车机修工……

**"The Watchmaker":** And this also marks the beginning of his journey devoted to the Trailblaze.

> **「钟表匠」：**同时，也是他一生「开拓」的起点。

**"The Watchmaker":** At the end of the journey, I left this little flame, which I so cherished, in my deepest dreams, hoping to pass it on to the Nameless of future generations.

> **「钟表匠」：**行遍人生旅途的最后，我把这一点自视珍贵的火苗留在最深的梦里，希望交给后世的无名客们。

**"The Watchmaker":** However, he somehow left the dream bubble and forgot all about his task. I apologize for all the confusion this caused.

> **「钟表匠」：**可不知怎的，他竟然擅自从梦泡里跑了出去，还把使命全都忘光了。抱歉，真是让各位看了一出笑话。

**"The Watchmaker":** Well, I have a sarcastic friend who says I always take big detours and end up back where I started... Perhaps that's what every Nameless has to go through.

> **「钟表匠」：**呵呵。我有个损友，总说我一辈子弯弯绕绕，最后又回到了起点…可能这就是每个无名客都要经历的阶段吧。

**"The Watchmaker":** But in the end, you've found me. I'm sure you're all wondering what my legacy is. I believe my hound has mentioned the Stellaron and my wealth...

> **「钟表匠」：**但最后你们还是找到了我。言归正传，各位寻到这儿，想来一定也很关心「『钟表匠』的遗产」究竟是什么，我的猎犬应该提到了星核，还有大亨的财富……

**"The Watchmaker":** If I may apologize, the Stellaron part is real. As for my wealth, however, it's nothing more than a baseless rumor.

> **「钟表匠」：**容我致歉，星核确有其事，但米哈伊尔的财富不过是街谈巷议的传言罢了。

**"The Watchmaker":** I left my homeland as a child and embarked on the journey of Trailblaze. I traveled to various planets until finally reaching Asdana, where my friends and I built the original Penacony and fought for its future ever since...

> **「钟表匠」：**我在孩提时代告别故乡，踏上「开拓」的旅途，路过一站又一站，最后在阿斯德纳停下。我和朋友建设了最初的匹诺康尼，又为它的未来奋斗至今……

**"The Watchmaker":** I've been moving forward all my life, doing what I could to overcome the obstacles in my path. But ultimately, my journey reached its end, and I left behind no possessions worth entrusting...

> **「钟表匠」：**我的一生都在前进，尽己所能冲破那些拦住去路的障碍。但最后，我的路也走到了尽头，身躯就像一节破破烂烂的车头，身后也没留下任何值得托付的财产……

**"The Watchmaker":** So, if you ask what's left within this worn-out train engine that can be called a "legacy"... I suppose it's the things that are still burning in the engine's furnace.

> **「钟表匠」：**所以，要问这节破旧的列车头里还剩什么能被称作「遗产」的东西…我想也只有那些依旧还在引擎炉膛里燃烧的事物了。

**"The Watchmaker":** Now that you're well aware of the current situation of Penacony, I certainly hope that you'll help me get this world back on track. But I'll leave that decision to you, for the Path of Trailblaze is never paved by others.

> **「钟表匠」：**匹诺康尼的现状，你们已然知晓。我当然希望有人来帮助这个世界重回正轨。但这个决定应当由你们来完成，因为「开拓」的道路从来不由他人铺就。

**"The Watchmaker":** All I have for you is a story and two gifts...

> **「钟表匠」：**所以，我为各位留下一个故事，和两件礼物——

**"The Watchmaker":** I want to give you my pocket watch. It has accompanied me throughout my long journey, guiding that naive child forward, and has been blessed with the presence of so many great people up to this day.

> **「钟表匠」：**我想把它给你们：我的「怀表」。它陪我走过漫长的旅程，指引那个一无所知的傻孩子不断向前，有幸和这么多伟大的人一起走到了今天。

**"The Watchmaker":** And my hat, too. The one who navigated for me placed it on my head and planted a fanciful thought in my mind — the Trailblazing expedition will never end.

> **「钟表匠」：**还有我的帽子。那个为我领航的人把它扣在我的脑袋上，从此安下一个不切实际的念想：「开拓」之旅永远也不会结束。

**"The Watchmaker":** Now, it's time for you to make your choice. Once you've made up your mind, open that door and enter the long dream of an old man.

> **「钟表匠」：**接下来，就该你们作出选择了。如果下定决心，就推开那扇门，走进一位老人长长的梦吧。

**"The Watchmaker":** I'll be waiting for you at the end of this corridor of time.

> **「钟表匠」：**我会在这条时光走廊的尽头，等候各位的到来。

[Recap] The Express Crew resolved to carry on the legacy, pushing open the door at the end of the dream and stepping toward a future of the Trailblaze.

### Advance towards the end of the long dream

> *With the passing of an extremely long night, Misha has finally regained his identity: The oblivious main character in the childhood dream of the Watchmaker, Mikhail Char Legwork — Clockie's friend, a young apprentice, a future Express maintenance engineer... Simultaneously, the origin of an old Nameless' Trailblazing journey. He has completed his mission and the Watchmaker has fulfilled his final, long-awaited wish. Now, the future of Penacony is in your hands.*

- ▸ Open Door

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**昨天晚上，我做了个很长的梦，梦见我们相遇的那天。我想把那个梦记录下来。

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**可是你知道吗？直到爷爷把它交给我的那天，我才恍然大悟，那其实不是什么怀表……

### Take a rest in the moonlight...

> *Dear future Nameless: I have been waiting for you all this time, future Nameless. Though I know not where you come from, what you look like, or even your name... For now, please allow me to call you the "Trailblazer". I am sure you have learned of who I am by now... A former Astral Express maintenance engineer, an apprentice of little promise, and an old man who lived an entirely unremarkable existence. I set out on the Trailblazing journey so I might learn about life itself and everything within. However, the more I learned, the more I found myself lost in confusion and distress. I thought that I would be able to learn how to live a better life, but all I learned was how to elegantly face the ending fate has paired you with... "Nihility". Everyone in life will eventually find their own answer — to resist or to obey fate — but these questions do not belong to Trailblaze. Although, I fear this question may simply be too profound. I fear even Akivili THEMSELF would not be able to provide a single answer that is unique to Trailblaze. However, as a Nameless... as a person... we have the right to reflect. We have the right to act. We have the right to define our own endings. We have the right to move forward. As we advance down the road to our own ending, there are many things that people are capable of, and our endings will thus take on a completely new meaning. A Nameless does not indulge in temporary dreams, nor would they forsake their birth rights due to pain and suffering. Do you still recall the question I left in my invitation letter, Trailblazer? "Why does life slumber?" "People slumber so that, eventually, they can awaken from their dreams." ...This is the answer that an old Nameless devoted his entire life to finding.*

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** ...

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**……

- ▸ Rest under the moonlight

> **钟表小子：**哦，那等你休息好了，我们再出发？

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**不，我应该会留在这里。然后…就结束了。

> **钟表小子：**结束？米沙，这是什么意思？
