> **钟表小子：**你明明说过，「开拓」之旅永远也不会结束。

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** Yeah, that's what I said...

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**是啊，我是这么说过……

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** So now, it's up to you to decide your next destination.

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**所以现在，该你决定自己的下一站了。

**Clockie:** My next destination? What's that supposed to be? I've been following you...

> **钟表小子：**我的下一站？那应该是哪里？我从来都是跟着你的……

**Clockie:** Misha? You're acting weird today! If you're feeling down, we can just do what we usually do... with the clockwork!

> **钟表小子：**米沙？你怎么了，今天的你好奇怪！如果不开心，我们可以像平时那样——施展「钟表把戏」！

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** No, I'm not feeling down. As for clockwork... yeah, it resolves all problems in this dream.

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**不用了，我没有不开心。至于「钟表把戏」…是啊，在这片梦里它仿佛能解决一切问题。

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** So, do you know what clockwork actually is?

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**那么你知道，「钟表把戏」究竟是什么吗？

**Clockie:** Hmm? I'm not quite sure.

> **钟表小子：**是什么？我不知道。

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** Well, everyone gets lost at times. They may hesitate and doubt which way to go. That happens in this Dreamscape and beyond.

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**每个人都会有迷路的时候，犹豫不决，不知道该去往哪个方向。它存在于这片梦境中，也存在于梦境之外的任何地方。

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** But don't worry, everyone goes through moments of uncertainty and hesitation. Eventually, they gather the courage to make bold decisions...

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**但无需害怕，正如人们会感到迷茫，在某个瞬间，他们也会下定决心，做出一个大胆但又了不起的决定……

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** Whether it's calming, happy, angry, or sad, all they need is a little nudge to take that step toward where they truly belong.

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**无论那是镇静的、欢欣的、愤怒的、还是悲伤的，他们需要的只是一道小小的推力，然后就能迈开步伐，走向属于自己的前方。

**Mikhail Char Legwork:** I'm leaving that little nudge with you, and I hope you'll share it with others...

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**我把这小小的力量留给你，并期待你将它带给更多的人……

> **拉格沃克•夏尔•米哈伊尔：**这就是「钟表把戏」，名为「开拓」的意志。

## 078　Everything that Rises Must Converge　上升的一切必将汇合

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：SoulGlad™ Scorchsand Audition Venue　|　角色：Trailblazer; March 7th; Firefly; Himeko; Sunday; Dominicus; Blade; Gallagher; Acheron; Boothill; Dan Heng; Black Swan; Jing Yuan; Dan Heng • Imbibitor Lunae; Gopher Wood; Tiernan　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Beauty and Destruction'' completed

### Speak with your companions and sort the information you have

> *Sunday invites you to decide the victor on the Penacony Grand Theater's stage. How will you decide? Since this is a major decision, it would be good to talk to your companions first.*

[Recap] Sunday issued his final challenge, marking a complete ideological break with the Express Crew as the decisive confrontation approached.

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Sunday:** I can't believe that Aeon would cast a glance at Penacony at a time like this... Is it because of the resonance from the legacy of the Trailblaze? Or perhaps the bond between you is so strong that it even impresses an Aeon?

> **星期日：**那位星神竟会在这种时候向匹诺康尼投来瞥视…是「开拓」的传承产生共鸣了么？还是说，各位的默契连星神都能打动？

**Himeko:** Well, there might be another possibility. Perhaps THEY want to witness, on behalf of the fallen Aeons, who will hold the future of Penacony.

> **姬子：**在我看来，倒是还有一种可能性——或许祂也想知道匹诺康尼的未来会掌握在谁的手中，才会代已死的星神前来见证。

**Sunday:** If that's the case, on behalf of the Dreammaster of Penacony and the 107,336 members of the Oak Family, I'm extending a formal invitation to all of you...

> **星期日：**既然如此，我谨代表匹诺康尼的梦主，和橡木家系十万七千三百三十六位同胞向各位正式发出邀请——

**Sunday:** I'm cordially inviting you all to the Penacony Grand Theater to participate in the upcoming Charmony Festival. And of course, you won't be in the audience, but on center stage.

> **星期日：**我们诚邀各位莅临匹诺康尼大剧院，参加即将开幕的谐乐大典。当然，各位要登上的不是观众席，而是舞台中央。

**Sunday:** Since the future of the Stellaron, Penacony, and even the entire Cosmos is at stake, let's draw a conclusion there, in all fairness.

> **星期日：**事关星核、匹诺康尼、乃至整个银河的未来。公平起见，就让我们在那里一见真章。

**Sunday:** If you truly believe in Akivili's path, then show me THEIR courage and determination.

> **星期日：**既然各位笃信阿基维利的道路，就向我展现祂的勇气和觉悟吧。

[Recap] You and your companions prepared for the battle, vowing to inherit Mikhail's will and carve a future for Penacony.

> **流萤：**恐怕就是这个意思。

> **三月七：**好怪啊！我还一直防着大反派什么时候会搞事呢，结果他到最后还在说什么「公平起见」…该不会根本没把咱们放在眼里吧？

**Himeko:** Well, in my opinion, Sunday is deeply committed to his own philosophy and genuinely wants to prove that the Order is right.

> **姬子：**在我看来，星期日对自己的理想深信不疑，也是真心实意想向我们证明「秩序」的正确。

> **姬子：**从他身上，我感受到强烈的信念和支配欲，倘若不能堂堂正正地胜出，想必他也没法给自己一个交代吧。

**Himeko:** Now that we've taken up the mantle, we can't afford to fail them.

> **流萤：**但这对「秩序」而言也是一样的，他们的计划并非一朝一夕，它的背后是「盛会之星」孕育了数百年的庞大意识——

**Firefly:** A desire to dream, to slumber and escape reality... All those hidden emotions have given birth to the sweet dream of the Order.

> **流萤：**想要入梦的渴望，想要沉睡的怠惰，还有逃避、放弃…人们在无形中被催生的情绪，成了「秩序」美梦诞生的摇篮。

> **流萤：**利用一整个世界的意志，推动一位星神的降生…这场对决绝不是单纯力量的交锋。为了匹诺康尼的未来，不能只有你们在舞台上战斗。

**Firefly:** and whispered to me...

> **流萤：**向我低语呢喃……

**Firefly:** Like fireflies to a flame... life begets death.

> **萨姆：**飞萤扑火，向死而生。

**Firefly:** May we meet again in reality.

> **萨姆：**愿我们在清醒的现实再会。

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] Firefly faced her fate with acceptance, viewing death as a choice. She resolved to face the end as a human, not a weapon.

...

**Blade:** After today, Jepella's name will disappear from cosmic history, and the Ever-Flame Mansion will take its place. In the not-too-distant future, you'll receive an invitation...

> **刃：**今天过后，耶佩拉的名字就会从银河历史中消失，而永火官邸将取而代之，在不远的未来收到一封邀请函……

**Blade:** That's your next stop.

> **刃：**那就是你的下一站。

**Firefly:** Land of the Dreams, Penacony.

> **流萤：**梦想之地，匹诺康尼。

**Blade:** I hope you find whatever you seek there, be it answers... or salvation.

> **刃：**祝你在那里找到想要的答案…或者，解脱。

**Firefly:** ...

> **流萤：**……

**Firefly:** You mean... my three "deaths"?

> **流萤：**你说的…是那三次「死亡」吗？

**Blade:** Silver Wolf told me about it. It's such a shame that it's not part of my script.

> **刃：**是银狼告诉我的。我只是遗憾它们不在我的剧本里。

**Firefly:** While I want to live, I'm never afraid of death. The opposite of death is eternal life, and that's... that's something I'll never desire.

> **流萤：**我想要活下去，但我不害怕死亡。死亡的反面是永生，那从来…不是我的所求。

**Firefly:** People die, and I am no exception. Death is like a script, a fate that cannot be defied. But that's exactly why...

> **流萤：**人终有一死，我也一样。死亡就像剧本，是无法违抗的命运。但也正因如此……

**Firefly:** We have to choose where we want to rest forever.

> **流萤：**我们才要为自己选择埋骨之地。

**Blade:** Do you exist just to perish?

> **刃：**你的生存，是为了灭亡？

**Firefly:** Are you not the same, Blade? The end you desire is not one dictated by others.

> **流萤：**你不也一样吗？刃，你渴望的终结…从来不是由他人定义的。

**Firefly:** If I were to die now, I would only be a weapon. I believe... I should die as a human.

> **流萤：**如果现在死去，我就只是一件「兵器」。但我想…我应该要以一个「人」的身份死去。

**Firefly:** Though its definition escapes me, isn't this the answer that ordinary people look for their whole lives? A name that can be carved onto their tombstone.

> **流萤：**尽管它的定义离我还很遥远，可普通人终其一生寻找的，不也是这么一个答案吗？一个能在墓志铭里留下的…短短的名字。

**Firefly:** The tombstone that belongs to me once bore the inscription "Glamoth's Iron Cavalry," then it changed to "Stellaron Hunter," but one day...

> **流萤：**属于我的那一块，它曾经刻着「格拉默铁骑」，如今刻着「星核猎手」，而总有一天……

**Firefly:** It will bear the name "Firefly" and the brilliance she showed at the end of her life.

> **流萤：**它会写下「流萤」的名字，和她在生命尽头绽放的华彩。

...

[Recap] Gallagher mourned for Mikhail, revealing his old sentiments and grudges, then disappeared silently after raising a toast.

In the meantime... somewhere at Dreamflux Reef...

**Gallagher:** That's quite unexpected, old man. Who would've thought your crazy plan would actually work? Do all you Nameless fools just act on a whim?

> **加拉赫：**没想到啊，老头，你那没头没脑的计划真成了。难道你们无名客全都是些只会意气用事的傻瓜么？

**Gallagher:** I can sense that this false sweet dream is coming to an end. The Nameless may be young, but they had the ability to achieve this goal... just like you did in your time.

> **加拉赫：**我能嗅到，虚假的美梦就要结束了。那群无名客虽然年轻，但确实有能力做到这件事…就像你们当年那样。

**Gallagher:** It's a shame you won't be able to see it firsthand. Heh, maybe I won't either... Once something fictional is seen through, it ceases to exist.

> **加拉赫：**可惜啊，没能让你亲眼见证这一幕。恐怕我也没这个福分了…「虚构」的事物被看穿，也就相当于不存在了。

**Gallagher:** Yeah, not just those Nameless, even Mr. Wings is just like you... Stubborn, won't listen or give up, no matter what... Well, fate is unpredictable, I guess. If we weren't bound by those cursed Paths, maybe we could've had some good talks.

> **加拉赫：**哼，不谈那群无名客，那头上长翅膀的小子也跟你一模一样，死心眼儿，不见棺材不落泪…天意弄人啊，要不是这该死的命途，咱几个没准真能聊到一起去。

**Gallagher:** But in the end, we managed to do it, and now we can find solace. Remember how those idiots cursed us? They said, "Go to hell, you worthless traitors!" Heh...

> **加拉赫：**不过，咱最后到底是狠狠出了口恶气。这下舒服了。还记得那帮混蛋当年是怎么咒咱们的吗，嗯？他们说：「下地狱去吧，该死的叛徒」……

**Gallagher:** Well, I don't know if they really meant it, but if longing for freedom means going to hell...

> **加拉赫：**米哈伊尔啊，米哈伊尔，我不知道他们说的是不是真的，但如果心向自由就活该要下地狱……

**Gallagher:** Then I'll be joining you soon, you old fool.

> **加拉赫：**那我很快就要下去找你了，老东西。

**Gallagher:** Let's get together and have supper again in hell...

> **加拉赫：**让我们在地狱里再次共进晚餐吧……

**Gallagher:** Oh, I almost forgot. There's one more thing...

> **加拉赫：**哦，差点忘记了，还有件事……

**Gallagher:** Here's to you, a glass of "Hello and Goodbye"... (Trailblazer)...

> **加拉赫：**用这杯「聚散有时」向你致意…开拓者……

**Gallagher:** To the imperfect... tomorrow.

> **加拉赫：**敬不完美的…明天。

[Recap] Acheron recalled her past travels. The thirty days she spent with her deceased friend became the last warmth in her memories.

**???:** It's warm here, isn't it? You're lucky to have found shelter from the rain, let alone fresh berries, in this desolate place.

> **？？？：**真暖和啊。这死海边平时连个活物都见不着，你倒是幸运，找到了避雨的地方不说，还有新鲜浆果，真不容易。

**Acheron:** I was just following the scent of life. It's particularly strong in a place like this.

> **黄泉：**只是循着「生命」的气息来了。在这种地方，这气息格外分明。

**Acheron:** It's a shame these berries don't have much flavor.

> **黄泉：**只可惜，尝起来实在是有点淡。

**???:** Seriously? In case you didn't know, this fruit is pretty juicy. The only downside is that when you chew it...

> **？？？：**真的？你可能不知道，这种果实倒也算得上多汁，唯一的问题只是放在口中咀嚼时……

**???:** ...It becomes extremely spicy.

> **？？？：**…会产生极其辛辣的刺激。

**Acheron:** ...

> **黄泉：**……

**???:** Have you... lost your sense of taste?

> **？？？：**你…没有味觉了么？

**Acheron:** I can still taste certain things... like a faint sweetness.

> **黄泉：**有些味道还是能尝见的…比如微微的「甜」。

**Acheron:** Before coming here, I stopped by a place called Orkron. It had barren cliffs and nights lit by bonfires. Burgundy snow would fall from the sky, and when it landed on my tongue... it tasted like raspberries.

> **黄泉：**来到这里前，我的上一站叫俄尔刻龙。那里也有荒无人烟的山崖，也有火堆照亮的夜晚。天上会下紫红色的雪，含在嘴里…有树莓的味道。

**Acheron:** The flavor wasn't exactly sweet, but it left a lasting impression. When I think back on my past, I realize that what's tying everything together... isn't the big events, but rather these small yet unforgettable moments.

> **黄泉：**那味道算不上甜美，却令人记忆犹新。每当我回首时，总会发现串联起来时路的…不是刻骨铭心的起承转合，而是这么一个个难忘的瞬间。

**Acheron:** Don't worry about it. Losing oneself is a reality that every Self-Annihilator must face. At least, I haven't completely lost my senses and memories yet.

> **黄泉：**别在意。逐渐丧失自我的存在…是每个自灭者都要面对的现实。至少，我还没有完全失去感官和记忆。

**???:** Well, congratulations on adding another footnote to your journey.

> **？？？：**那就祝贺你又为旅途添上了新的注脚吧。

**???:** By the way, are you always alone?

> **？？？：**话说回来，你一个人？

**Acheron:** No, I had a companion in Orkron. She's a short Nameless girl who aspired to explore IX... She always said she'd walk a path "deeper and farther than Akivili's."

> **黄泉：**不，我在俄尔刻龙还有个同伴。她个头小小的，是个无名客，想把自己发射到「IX」里去…她总说自己要走一条「比阿基维利更深、更远的路」。

**???:** Quite an ambition for such a small girl... So, what happened?

> **？？？：**个子不大，野心不小…那结果呢？

**Acheron:** She... became stagnant water.

> **黄泉：**她…变成了一潭死水。

**???:** Well... my condolences.

> **？？？：**呵…节哀。

**Acheron:** Condolences? I don't need them. The girl left with a smile. She never regretted her choice and most definitely wanted me to say goodbye with a smile... So that's what I did.

> **黄泉：**哀伤么？我不这么认为。那女孩是笑着离开的，她从未对自己的选择感到后悔，也一定希望我能笑着和她道别…我的确是这么做的。

**???:** That's proof that you're grieving for her.

> **？？？：**这就是你在为她感到悲伤的证明。

**Acheron:** Or perhaps I'm just afraid?

> **黄泉：**或许是害怕呢？

**???:** Afraid? I rarely sense that emotion from you. What do you fear?

> **？？？：**害怕？我很难从你身上感受到这种情绪。你怕什么？

**Acheron:** I'm afraid I'll forget the thirty days I spent with her, just like all the other days in my life. Most of them have already washed away with the rain, disappearing into an unseen realm.

> **黄泉：**我怕会忘记和她一起走过的三十天，就像我生命中的每个三十一天。它们中的大部分已经同雨水一起逝去，消失在看不见的彼岸。

**Acheron:** I fear that those vivid red memories will fade away too. There isn't much color left, and besides this faint, warm red, there's almost nothing.

> **黄泉：**我怕这些鲜红的记忆也离开我。我能看到的颜色已经不多了，除了这一点淡淡的、温暖的「红」，我几乎一无所有。

**???:** Hard to imagine... a "Ranger" accustomed to bloodshed, destruction, and chaos finding warmth in the red color.

> **？？？：**真是难以想象…一个看惯了鲜血、破灭和混乱的「游侠」居然能从红色里看出温暖。

**Acheron:** Because I have experienced this warmth many times. Long ago, I promised someone that I'd bring it to more people, and that for every remaining moment of my life, I'd strive for... a better ending for all.

> **黄泉：**因为这样的温暖，我也拥有过许多。很久以前，我和他人约定过，要把它带给更多的人，在余生的每一刻都去追寻…「对所有人都更好的结局」。

**Acheron:** As long as this red color still lingers, I have a chance to fulfill that promise. It represents a burning fire, a blooming flower, the berries in this cave... and life itself, fleeting yet still dazzling.

> **黄泉：**只要这一抹「红色」尚在，我就还有机会履行约定。它可以是燃烧的火，是盛放的花，是这岩洞里的一丛浆果…它就是生命本身，转瞬即逝、却足够夺目。

**Acheron:** In the end, it will lead me beyond the Horizon of Existence, and on the other side, I will... cut off Nihility.

> **黄泉：**最后，它会引领我跨越「存在的地平线」，在彼岸的尽头…斩断「虚无」。

**???:** The one blessed by the sleeping and shapeless is considering how to kill THEM? That's truly... pure Nihility.

> **？？？：**身受沉眠无相者的祝福，却想着要如何杀死祂？这可真是…彻头彻尾的「虚无」啊。

**???:** But you're right about one thing — After spending so much time near this stagnant water, only when I look at this vibrant red fire do I realize that I'm still alive.

> **？？？：**不过，有一点你说得对。在这阴雨绵绵的死水边待久了，只有望着这团鲜红的火时，我才发觉自己原来还活着。

**Acheron:** When will this rain... ever stop?

> **黄泉：**雨啊…什么时候才会停呢？

**???:** Perhaps... when the sorrows of the departed have finally quieted down, the sky will clear up.

> **？？？：**也许…等亡者的怨念悉数平息，天就放晴了吧。

[Recap] Acheron speculated The Family's plots. Dan Heng was determined to use the jade abacus of allying oath to seek help from the Luofu to salvage the situation.

4 system hours until Charmony Festival - Astral Express

**Acheron:** ...

> **黄泉：**……

**Black Swan:** Have you heard of a planet named Byari Skamandros, Dan Heng? It's one of the paradises under the influence of the Harmony, a sought-after wonderland for the inhabitants of the Dardanu Major and Minor systems.

> **黑天鹅：**丹恒先生，你听说过比亚里-斯卡曼德洛斯星么？那是「同谐」影响下的地上天国之一，大小达耳达努星系居民们趋之若鹜的人间天堂。

**Black Swan:** Half an Amber Era ago, The Family held an unprecedented festival there, and after that... everyone on the planet became part of The Family.

> **黑天鹅：**半个琥珀纪前，家族在那里举办了一场空前绝后的庆典，而宴会过后…星球上的每个人都成了「家族」的一员。

**Dan Heng:** Do you think the same thing will happen on Penacony?

> **丹恒：**你认为同样的事会发生在匹诺康尼？

**Black Swan:** Yes, or how else can we explain it? The Family deliberately used the Watchmaker's invitation to keep all the Pathstriders here but banished the Emanator of the Nihility...

> **黑天鹅：**不然要如何解释呢？家族特意借「钟表匠」的邀请让一众命途行者滞留其中，却唯独放逐了「虚无」的令使……

**Acheron:** Because of the Nihility, I'm rarely affected by the power of other Paths, but somehow I can unconsciously infiltrate them... Maybe that's the "risk" they're trying to avoid.

> **黄泉：**受「虚无」影响，我很难受到其他命途力量的影响，反倒能无意识地侵染它们…这或许就是他们不想看到的「变量」。

**Dan Heng:** I would disagree. Byari Skamandros is not part of the credit system or connected to the silver rail. It's nothing more than a remote civilization sheltered by the Harmony... But Penacony is different.

> **丹恒：**恕我难以苟同。那颗星球既没有加入信用体系，也没有连通银轨，是「同谐」庇护下的偏远文明…但匹诺康尼不一样。

**Dan Heng:** If The Family messes with Penacony, that would be like declaring war on almost half of the factions in the Cosmos. They have no reason to do that.

> **丹恒：**这么做意味着向全银河近半数的派系宣战，家族没有理由这么做。

**Acheron:** No, they don't... if they truly serve the Harmony, that is.

> **黄泉：**前提是…他们真的心向「同谐」。

**Dan Heng:** What do you mean?

> **丹恒：**什么意思？

**Acheron:** The Path in Penacony is impure. The Harmony here has impurities.

> **黄泉：**被笼罩在匹诺康尼光芒下的命途并不纯粹，这里的「同谐」中混入了杂质。

**Black Swan:** Do you remember the ancient Swarm Disaster? Tayzzyronth the Propagation brought endless havoc to the universe, and it eventually evolved into a fierce battle among all Aeons.

> **黑天鹅：**还记得那场古老的「寰宇蝗灾」么？「繁育」塔伊兹育罗斯给宇宙带来无尽浩劫，而这场浩劫最终却在混乱和迷茫中演变为列神之战。

**Black Swan:** Two Paths lost their Aeons in that war — the Propagation and the Order. Coincidentally, their downfall is related to a certain Aeon...

> **黑天鹅：**共有两道命途在这场大战中失去了星神：「繁育」和「秩序」。巧合的是，这两道命途的转折都与某位星神有关……

**Acheron:** ...Xipe the Harmony. Legend has it that THEY participated in the crusade against the Imperator Insectorum and devoured Ena the Order for unknown reasons.

> **黄泉：**…「同谐」的希佩。据说祂参与了列神对虫皇的讨伐，又出于不明原因吞纳了「秩序」太一。

**Boothill:** Holy forkeroni... So, you're saying that the two leaderless Paths are working behind the scenes?

> **波提欧：**他小宝贝的，好家伙…你们是想说可能是两道无主的命途在暗中捣鬼？

**Dan Heng:** But I don't see any descendants of the Propagation in Penacony. Could it be that the remnants of Beyond the Sky Choir are hiding within The Family, trying to resurrect a fallen Aeon?

> **丹恒：**可匹诺康尼并未出现「繁育」的子嗣。我可否理解为是「天外合唱班」的残党潜伏于家族中，并且想要复活一位陨落的星神？

**Acheron:** I can't say for sure, but they're definitely planning something for the Charmony Festival.

> **黄泉：**尚不能断定，但至少能确定他们准备利用「谐乐大典」做些什么。

**Boothill:** Holy Wubbaboo! This is getting way too complicated. Is this why you want us to leave Asdana right away? Are you giving up?

> **波提欧：**我了个呜呜伯，这么复杂啊。那你要求我们立刻离开阿斯德纳的意思是？不会是没辙了吧？

**Acheron:** The Charmony Festival will start soon. There's one thing that... I need to confirm no matter what. A warp jump is the best way to do so.

> **黄泉：**谐乐大典开幕在即，有一件事…我无论如何都需要求证。跃迁是最有效的手段。

**Dan Heng:** Time is running out...

> **丹恒：**不，正因为时间紧迫……

**Dan Heng:** I have another plan.

> **丹恒：**我会立即动用其他手段。

**Boothill:** Hold on, are you thinking of using the Jade Abacus of Allying Oath?

> **波提欧：**你不会是要用「结盟玉兆」吧！

**Dan Heng:** Exactly. The assistance from the Luofu Cloud Knights... would be enough.

> **丹恒：**正是。有罗浮云骑支援…应当够了。

**Boothill:** Think it over carefully. You can only use that once in your lifetime.

> **波提欧：**那东西一辈子只能用一次，你最好想清楚了。

**Dan Heng:** I have considered it thoroughly. My companions are... They're also once-in-a-lifetime treasures.

> **丹恒：**我想清楚了。我的伙伴们…也是一辈子只能拥有一次的。

[Recap] Sunday faced destiny on his sister's behalf. After speaking with the Dreammaster, he decided to betray the Harmony and have the Order descend upon Penacony.

3 system hours until Charmony Festival - Penacony Grand Theater

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**"Dreammaster":** Are you the only one here, my child?

> **「梦主」：**在这里的只有你么，孩子？

**"Dreammaster":** The Nameless is quite the diplomat. Our secrets have spread like wildfire within The Family, and IPC starships are gathering towards Asdana...

> **「梦主」：**那无名客确有一手，我等的秘密已在家系间不胫而走，公司的星舰也在向阿斯德纳集结……

**"Dreammaster":** This is a crucial moment for us. So, where is the chosen one who harmonizes the varied sounds?

> **「梦主」：**眼下正是关键时刻，试问，那位调和众音的神选者在哪里？

**Sunday:** What do you mean, Master?

> **星期日：**这是什么话，先生？

**Sunday:** I'm right here in front of you, aren't I?

> **星期日：**我不是正好端端地站在你面前么？

**"Dreammaster":** You know, she was supposed to be the star of the Charmony Festival in our plan.

> **「梦主」：**你知道，在我们的计划中，她才是谐乐大典的主角。

**Sunday:** But the plan has changed. As her brother, I know she doesn't want to sing for the Order, so I'll take her place.

> **星期日：**可计划有变。身为她的兄长，我知道为「秩序」献唱必不是她的本愿。这里有我足矣。

**"Dreammaster":** Heh, you've always been wise beyond your years. I'm sure you understand the consequences of your choice.

> **「梦主」：**呵。你从小就智慧过人，想必一定明白，自己此刻的作为要付出何种代价。

**Sunday:** If you consider this a betrayal...

> **星期日：**如果您认为这是一场「背叛」……

**Sunday:** Well, there can't be two suns in the sky. I'll step up and take down the other sun if necessary.

> **星期日：**天无二日。如有必要，我会出手将太阳击落。

**"Dreammaster":** Do you believe in karma?

> **「梦主」：**你相信报应吗？

**Sunday:** If karma exists, then everyone has their own karma. You have yours, and I have mine.

> **星期日：**如果报应真的存在，那么众生皆有报应——你有你的，我也有我的。

**Sunday:** And my karma has nothing to do with you, Mr. Gopher Wood.

> **星期日：**而我的报应与你无关，歌斐木先生。

**"Dreammaster":** ...

> **「梦主」：**……

**"Dreammaster":** Alright, since you're willing to sacrifice yourself for her, I'll grant your wish.

> **「梦主」：**无妨。既然你愿意代她牺牲，那我就成全你吧。

**Sunday:** Well, the compromise came sooner than expected. Why?

> **星期日：**让步比我预想中来得更快。为什么？

**"Dreammaster":** You and your sister were born as twins of the Order, and one of you is destined to follow this path to the end.

**Jing Yuan:** Why of course you're welcome! They've mentioned that your team is a trusted ally of the IPC in Penacony, so there's no reason not to welcome you.

> **景元：**怎么会，当然欢迎！他们表示各位是公司在匹诺康尼的可靠盟友，没有不欢迎的理由。

**Jing Yuan:** Besides, if there can be reliable observers from the Astral Express present, discussions will go more smoothly. So, what do you all think?

> **景元：**况且，若是像星穹列车这般可靠的观察员在场，讨论想必一定能进展得更加顺利。怎样，各位意下如何？

**Himeko:** Well then, we shall oblige.

> **姬子：**那我们就恭敬不如从命了。

**March 7th:** ...I'm a kind of allergic to those types of situations. I think I'll just head back to my room and start packing.

> **三月七：**噫…本、本姑娘对这种场合有点过敏，我还是先回房间打包行李好了。

**Welt:** Not to worry. Himeko and I will take care of things.

> **瓦尔特：**没关系，去吧。这里由我和姬子小姐出面就可以。

**Dan Heng:** I'm afraid I'll also have to return to the Express first. The conductor is worried about us. It's best I go and explain the situation.

> **丹恒：**我恐怕也要先一步回列车了。列车长很担心我们，我还是去说明一下现状为好。

**Himeko:** Thank you. What about you, (Trailblazer)? Will you join Welt and me, or have you got other plans?

> **姬子：**嗯，拜托你了。开拓者，你呢？你想跟我和瓦尔特一起去，还是说你也有别的事要做？

- ▸ I'll go with Miss Himeko.

**Jing Yuan:** Haha, great.

> **景元：**哈哈，这敢情好。

- ▸ I HAVE TO help March pack her suitcase...

**March 7th:** What? I can pack it myself, you know...

> **三月七：**呃…其实我一个人就可以收拾好啦……

**Jing Yuan:** Haha, if that's the case, our Galactic Baseballer can join us.

> **景元：**哈哈，既然如此，那还是请我们的「银河球棒侠」一道来吧。

- ▸ Dunno why, but something tells me I only have one real option here...

**Jing Yuan:** Haha, good guess!

> **景元：**哈哈，猜得不错！

**Jing Yuan:** Although I'm not too sure of the reason, the representatives from the IPC have insisted on (Trailblazer)'s presence — allow me to lead the way, follow me please!

> **景元：**虽然我不清楚个中缘由，但公司的代表们表示务必要让开拓者前来旁听——我为各位带路，这边请！

### Go to the Lobby and meet with the IPC representative

The General and various IPC dignitaries have agreed to hold talks regarding the Charmony Festival incident — and they have invited the Express Crew to be present... You in particular, (Trailblazer). You have no reason to turn down this offer, right?*

### Speak with the IPC and Intelligentsia Guild representatives

*(Approach quest location)*

**Himeko:** Mr. Aventurine and Miss Topaz are here too... And, who is that over there?

> **姬子：**砂金先生和托帕小姐都来了啊…还有，那边那位是？

**Welt:** The Intelligentsia Guild's Dr. Ratio. This assembly is quite something.

> **瓦尔特：**博识学会的拉帝奥教授，阵仗真不小啊。

[Recap] Your party discussed with the IPC representatives about the rebuilding of Penacony. The various groups gave up on their rivalry and joined hands to work together, with the Interastral Peace Corporation declaring to unconditionally forfeit their sovereignty over Penacony.

*(Talk to Aventurine, Dr. Ratio, or Topaz)*

**Aventurine:** It's been a while, my Astral Express friends.

> **砂金：**久违了，星穹列车的各位。

**Aventurine:** I would also extend my sincere thanks to you, General of the Luofu. The presence of everyone here assures that the talks will likely reach a conclusion that satisfies all sides.

> **砂金：**也向您致以诚挚的谢意，罗浮将军。能有各位在场共同见证，想必此次谈判一定能以令各方都满意的结果圆满落幕。

**Jing Yuan:** Oh? Looks like everyone has come with expectations. Care to share?

> **景元：**哦？看来各位已经有所预期了。不妨说来听听？

**Aventurine:** Of course. Topaz, if you please?

> **砂金：**当然。你来吧，托帕？

**Topaz:** Sure, leave it to me.

> **托帕：**嗯，交给我吧。

**Topaz:** In summary, it's good news — after much deliberation from the Strategic Investment Department's council, the absolute majority of members have agreed to the following resolutions:

> **托帕：**总的来说，是好消息——经过战略投资部「重大事务组」会议审议表决，以绝对多数成员同意通过了以下决议：

**Topaz:** "In light of long-term considerations for interastral peace, and by authority of Pier Point HQ, the Strategic Investment Department — on behalf of the Interastral Peace Corporation — will permanently relinquish its claim on Penacony sovereignty, and offer unconditional support of The Family's rebuilding efforts on Penacony."

> **托帕：**「基于对星际和平的长久考虑，经由庇尔波因特总部授权，战略投资部将代表公司永久放弃对匹诺康尼主权之宣称，并无条件支援家族对匹诺康尼的重建工作。」

**Himeko:** Uh...

> **姬子：**这……

**Jing Yuan:** Heh, now that's something.

> **景元：**呵，有点意思。

- ▸ So, the IPC does see the big picture, huh?

**Aventurine:** "All in the name of peace" — that's our motto.

> **砂金：**「一切为了和平」——这就是我司的基本价值观。

- ▸ What's in it for you guys?

**Aventurine:** To be honest, it does nothing to benefit the IPC... but it is extremely beneficial to the long-term development of the entire universe.

> **砂金：**老实说对公司没有任何好处…但对全宇宙的长远发展却是非常有利的。

- ▸ Doesn't that mean Aventurine took a hit for nothing?

**Aventurine:** Haha, if that's what it takes to bring peace to the entire universe, it's a price I'll gladly pay.

> **砂金：**哈哈，要是这样能为全宇宙带来和平，那也算值了。

[Recap] The Intelligentsia Guild and the Genius Society pledged to assist with Dreamscape research and support rebuilding Penacony.

**Dr. Ratio:** Has the IPC finished sharing all its thoughts? Then it's our turn.

> **真理医生：**公司的意见聊完了？那轮到我们了。

**Dr. Ratio:** The Guild, much like the Genius Society, has taken a keen interest in the recent calamity in Penacony. Ultimately, both parties have agreed to a comprehensive collaboration, offering technical support for the reconstruction of Penacony...

> **真理医生：**学会和天才俱乐部同样对匹诺康尼此次灾难报以高度关注。最后，我双方决定达成全面合作，对匹诺康尼的重建予以技术支持……

**Dr. Ratio:** ...The floor is yours for the finer points, Mr. Screwllum. Enlighten us, please.

> **真理医生：**…具体内容还是听听这位天才的说法吧。轮到你了，螺丝咕姆先生。

**Screwllum:** Organic life's unrelenting search to understand the realm of inner spirituality is something I both admire and envy. Inorganic life has no mechanism to evoke dreams. But when my mechanical impulses are activated, my inspiration circuits will start to operate, and I will enter a state defined as "imagination."

> **螺丝咕姆：**有机生命对内在精神世界的不懈探索既令我赞叹，又令我羡慕。无机生命没有做梦的机能。但当智能脉冲激活，灵感回路开始运作，我会进入被定义为「想象」的状态。

**Screwllum:** Every time, within the realm of imagination, there emerges a fire from the shadows. It is warm, bright. I frequently ponder, this flame might represent the essence of intelligence — a cluster of inspiration ignited by high temperatures. The future direction of the universe may well lie within it.

> **螺丝咕姆：**每一次，想象之中都有一团火从黑暗中升起。它温暖，明媚。我时常思考，那火焰或许就是智能的本质——一簇因高温激发的灵感。宇宙未来的方向或许就在其中。

**Screwllum:** Alas, they are nothing but projections of my thought system to me — desired, but unattainable. But after learning of Penacony's accomplishments, I have come to realize that the flame is not beyond my grasp.

> **螺丝咕姆：**可惜，它对我而言不过是思维系统折射出的虚像，可望而不可即。但在了解到匹诺康尼如今的成就后，我终于意识到，那火焰并非不能攫取。

**Screwllum:** After deliberations with my partners, we have decided to defer the progress of the Simulated Universe project, and instead assist the Intelligentsia Guild as technological consultants in the research of the Dreamscape and Memory Zone, so that these assets may be better used to serve humanity.

> **螺丝咕姆：**在与几位合作伙伴讨论后，我们决定暂缓对模拟宇宙项目的推进，并将以技术顾问的身份支持博识学会对梦境与忆域的研究，以便这种物质更好地为全人类服务。

**Dr. Ratio:** Not only that, we've also established contact with the Garden of Recollection through the IPC, and they've pledged their support for our research endeavors...

> **真理医生：**不仅如此，我们还通过公司与忆庭取得联系，他们也承诺会协助开展研究……

**Dr. Ratio:** I am truly happy for the Dreamchasers on Penacony — the cosmos's brightest and, let's admit, dimmer intellects are now at their service.

> **真理医生：**呵，真为匹诺康尼的逐梦客们感到开心——全宇宙最智慧和最愚钝的头脑现在全都为他们所用了。

- ▸ Hooray! It's a big win for everyone!

**Welt:** But... *sigh* Never mind. At the end of the day, this is a positive outcome.

> **瓦尔特：**但…罢了，无论如何结果是好的。

- ▸ I keep getting this feeling that this is all too good to be true...

**Welt:** Agreed. It's hard to imagine members of the Genius Society taking an interest in such mundane research...

> **瓦尔特：**确实，尤其很难想象天才俱乐部的成员也会对俗世的研究感兴趣……

**Welt:** *sigh* Never mind. At the end of the day, this is a positive outcome.

> **瓦尔特：**…罢了，无论如何结果是好的。

[Recap] You and Welt head back together. The trip in Penacony has come to a fruitful end.

**Himeko:** No wonder everyone insisted that he/she be there.

> **姬子：**难怪各位一定要他/她出面呢。

**Jing Yuan:** It heartens me to learn that everyone is willing to put aside their differences for Penacony's plight. I trust that everyone will surely reach consensus in the upcoming negotiations.

> **景元：**能了解到各位都愿为匹诺康尼排忧解难，这可真是再好不过了。相信各位一定能在接下来的谈判中达成共识。

**Jing Yuan:** Looks like Penacony's future is decided. I'm wondering, is there anything else the Crew is concerned about?

> **景元：**看来匹诺康尼的未来已成定局。不知列车组的各位是否还有所牵挂？

**Himeko:** Peace is our greatest wish — beyond that, we desire nothing else.

> **姬子：**和平就是我们最大的愿望，除此之外别无所求。

**Jing Yuan:** Haha, well that's good. Now that everyone's minds are at ease, I shall take my leave. You may now depart with peace of mind — the Alliance will deal with all subsequent procedures.

> **景元：**哈哈，好。既然各位心无挂碍，那景元就在此拜辞了。各位安心登程，后续相关事务交由我联盟便是。

Some time later...

**Himeko:** If that's the case, it appears that we have nothing else to worry about on Penacony... Looks like it's time for us to embark on a new voyage.

> **姬子：**既然这样，看来我们在匹诺康尼也没什么后顾之忧了…我们是时候踏上新的旅程了。

**Welt:** Sounds good to me.

> **瓦尔特：**嗯，我赞成。

**Himeko:** You two head back to the Express first. I'll pick up March and deal with the check-out procedure. Oh also, Miss Black Swan, you have a matter to discuss with me, yes?

> **姬子：**那你们两个先回列车吧。我去接小三月，顺便办理退房——还有，黑天鹅小姐也找我有事，对吧？

**Black Swan:** Nothing escapes your attention, Miss Navigator.

> **黑天鹅：**不愧是领航员小姐。

**Welt:** You've been with us this whole time, huh. In any case, (Trailblazer) and I will be waiting for you and March on the Express.

> **瓦尔特：**原来你刚才一直都跟在我们身边啊…无妨，总之，我和开拓者就在列车上等你和小三月了。

**Welt:** Let's go, (Trailblazer). Our time on Penacony... has come to a fruitful end.

> **瓦尔特：**我们走吧，开拓者。匹诺康尼之旅…到这里就算圆满结束了。

### Return to the Astral Express

> *The IPC decides to develop Penacony alongside The Family, and scholars from all over the universe will assist in every way they can... It is a lively scene where all's well that ends well. With this, your journey to Penacony, the "Land of the Dreams" has come to an end. It's time to embark on a new adventure.*

**(Trailblazer):** (Penacony's journey ends here...)

### Join the navigation meeting

*(Teleport to Parlor Car)*

*(Obtain Achievement If We Walk in the Light)*

**Pom-Pom:** (Trailblazer), the navigation meeting is about to start. We're all waiting for you!

> **帕姆：**开拓者乘客，航线会议就要开始了，就等你啦！

**(Trailblazer):** (When did the Express become so lively...?)

> **开拓者：**（列车什么时候变得这么热闹了…？）

[Recap] Black Swan and Boothill would travel with the Express and head on new journeys together.

*(Approach Black Swan, Boothill, and Pom-Pom)*

**Pom-Pom:** Ahem... looks like everyone's present. Let the navigation meeting commence then!

> **帕姆：**咳咳…这样人都到齐了帕。那我们现在开始航线会议吧！

**Boothill:** This a meeting to decide on our next stop? How we doin' this, by show of hands?

> **波提欧：**是要决定下一站去哪儿的会呗？怎么说，投票还是？

**Black Swan:** Hold your horses, cowboy. It's for those to decide.

> **黑天鹅：**别心急，牛仔。客随主便，交给他们定夺吧。

- ▸ Boothill, why are you here?

**Boothill:** Hah, I'm just catching a lift. Don't sweat it!

> **波提欧：**嗐，搭个便车嘛，别紧张！

- ▸ Black Swan, how come you're here too?

**Black Swan:** Shouldn't this be expected of a Memokeeper?

> **黑天鹅：**这对一位忆者来说不是很正常的事么？

- ▸ What in tarnation are you guys doing here?

**Himeko:** Allow me to explain: Mr. Boothill and Miss Black Swan submitted a request to temporarily travel with the Express for their own personal reasons...

> **姬子：**让我来解释吧。波提欧先生和黑天鹅小姐出于各自需要，提出了想要暂时与列车组同行的请求……

**Himeko:** As you all may also know, the Astral Express never declines any passenger whose heart yearns for the distant stars. Therefore, they will be traveling with us for a while, until they reach their destinations.

> **姬子：**各位也知道，星穹列车向来不会拒绝任何心向远方的旅客。所以，他们接下来将和我们一起旅行一段时间，直到抵达目的地为止。

**March 7th:** Whoa... The Express is going to be much livelier now. But Miss Black Swan, you'd better not use your Memokeeper abilities to pull any pranks!

> **三月七：**喔…这下列车里要热闹起来了。不过，黑天鹅小姐，你可不能在列车里用忆者的能力恶作剧哦！

**Black Swan:** Understood, Miss March. I promise you, you'll never see me in your room while you're taking a break.

> **黑天鹅：**好啊，三月七小姐。我答应你，休息时间里你绝对不会在自己的房间里看见我。

**March 7th:** Uhm... Don't, you're freaking me out...

> **三月七：**噫…不要吓我呀……

**Pom-Pom:** All right. All right. Now that everyone's met everyone, we can continue our navigation meeting!

> **帕姆：**好了，好了。既然各位乘客都相互认识过了，那我们就继续航线会议了帕！

[Recap] The Express Crew held a meeting and held a vote to decide the next stop on your trailblazing journey.

**Pom-Pom:** Firstly, Pom-Pom wishes to thank everyone. If it weren't for you all unearthing the truth about Penacony, Pom-Pom would have never known where Mikhail and the rest had gone...

> **帕姆：**首先，列车长要好好感谢大家。如果不是你们挖掘出了匹诺康尼的真相，我可能再也没法得知米哈伊尔他们的下落了……

**Pom-Pom:** What they had to go through was regrettable, but I reckon they all fulfilled their wishes... And it was thanks to all of you — thank you, everyone.

> **帕姆：**虽然他们的经历有些遗憾，但我想他们应该也了却了各自的心愿…这都多亏了你们。非常感谢各位帕。

**Pom-Pom:** Now then, we come to the crux of this navigation meeting: We must decide on the Express' next stop! Let me introduce our current options.

> **帕姆：**然后，就是本次航线会议的重点了——我们需要决定列车的下一站帕！由列车长来为各位介绍一下目前的选项。

**Pom-Pom:** The first choice is from Himeko: The oceanic planet of Lushaka — a planet composed entirely of water. Many aquatic races reside there. Of course, it's also the home planet of the venerable Nameless, Mikhail.

> **帕姆：**第一个选择来自姬子，是海洋星球露莎卡星。那是一颗完全由液态水构成的行星，有许多水生种族在那里定居。当然，那也是老无名客米哈伊尔的故乡帕。

**Pom-Pom:** The second choice is the "Agate World" Melustanin, suggested by Welt. It's famed as one of the initial sites of the Stellaron Disaster and the place where "the Beauty" Idrila ascended. Today, it's celebrated as a planet of undying allure.

> **帕姆：**第二个选择是瓦尔特提供的「玛瑙世界」梅露丝坦因。据说那里是星核之灾的原爆点之一，也是「纯美」伊德莉拉的飞升之地，现在是一颗永存不灭之美的美丽星球帕。

**Pom-Pom:** The third choice is Edo Star, a planet nestled within a vast ion storm region, currently under assault by the Antimatter Legion. However, the distress signals from there have recently ceased, prompting the IPC's wish for us to check in on the situation.

> **帕姆：**第三个选择是江户星。那颗行星藏身在一片广阔的离子暴潮区中，正在遭受反物质军团的侵略。但近期那里的求救信号突然中断了，公司希望我们能去确认状况帕。

**Pom-Pom:** The last choice, courtesy of Black Swan, is the "Glass Belt" Patrevia, a massive belt of asteroids that was turned to glass by the Lord Ravager Zephyro... These days it's apparently known to house one of the branches of the Mourning Actors troupe.

> **帕姆：**最后一个是黑天鹅提供的「琉璃光带」帕特雷维尼齐亚。那是一条因为绝灭大君「焚风」完全玻璃化的巨大行星带…现如今似乎是悲悼伶人的剧团知名分部之一帕。

**March 7th:** Ooh... So many options. I'm seeing stars already.

> **三月七：**喔…有好多选择啊，感觉要挑花眼了。

**Himeko:** Next up, everyone will select the destination that they wish to visit. And then, we'll put it to a vote.

> **姬子：**接下来，大家各自挑选自己想要拜访的目的地吧。然后，我们进行投票表决。

- ▸ Oceanic planet of Lushaka.

**Himeko:** Little Misha and Old Mikhail's homeland... Having been through so much in Penacony, as an aspiring Nameless, I believe a visit to his home world is a must.

> **姬子：**小米沙和老米哈伊尔的故乡…在经历了匹诺康尼的种种后，我想身为未来的无名客，无论如何都得去他的家乡看看。

**Welt:** Count me in. Still, I imagine that planet must have changed entirely after the Stellaron Disaster. I've heard that its landmasses no longer exist, and the native population has completely changed...

> **瓦尔特：**嗯…我也赞同。不过那颗星球在星核之灾后已经彻底变样了吧？听说那里的陆地已经不复存在，主要住民也全都洗了牌……

**March 7th:** Hmm... I'm not against Miss Himeko's choice, but Edo Star sounds like it's in danger. As Nameless, should we not extend a helping hand?

> **三月七：**嗯…姬子小姐说的我是不反对啦，但江户星听起来遭遇了危险。身为无名客，我们更应该向他们伸出援手吧？

**Dan Heng:** March is right. Though the fact that the distress signals have ceased means we're probably too late... but I still think we should investigate the situation there.

> **丹恒：**三月七说得没错。虽然「求救信号突然中断」意味着我们多半来不及了…但我觉得还是先去确认一下为好。

**Pom-Pom:** Three votes for Lushaka, two for Edo...

> **帕姆：**露莎卡星三票，江户星两票……

**Pom-Pom:** Looks like we have a winner. Next stop: Lushaka, the ocean planet!

> **帕姆：**看来我们已经得出结果了——下一站就是海洋星球露莎卡星了帕！

- ▸ The Agate World, Melustanin.

**Welt:** This trip to Penacony has allowed me to see another side of the Stellaron Disaster... In terms of symbolism, the state of the Dreamscape bears a striking resemblance to the Agate World. Visiting it might deepen our understanding of the Stellaron.

> **瓦尔特：**这次的匹诺康尼之旅让我看到了星核之灾的另一面…就表征而言，梦境的状况和「玛瑙世界」非常相似，前往那里或许有助于我们加深对星核的认识。

**Himeko:** Mm... that makes sense. If that's the case, I vote for the Agate World too.

> **黑天鹅：**「光锥」是用于封装凝固事象的光之切片，这枚空光锥也一样。

> **黑天鹅：**它能够将你的记忆以最鲜活的形式刻录下来，然后…为我欣赏把玩，成为独一无二的回忆。

> **黑天鹅：**世间万物皆出自精神和灵性的力量——这力量正是「记忆」。要想使我们免于被世界遗落在身后，便要让世界记住我们…或者，用我们的「记忆」重塑世界。

[Recap] Black Swan gazed into a shooting star, claiming that it could bring a wish into countless worlds. You felt very relaxed, and Black Swan suggested telling you a little bedtime story.

In the year 2158 of the Amber Era, the first year of the new epoch, the universe resumed its intended trajectory. The kindling of conspiracy smoldered in Penacony, the Land of the Dreams. Failing to erupt into a blaze, it instead flickered briefly on Qlipoth's anvil, before vanishing in the blink of an eye.

The dead and those fated to die remain in their eternal slumber, while the living find solace in deep sleep. All clamored in a cacaphony of silence, and then went about their own ways. The cosmos emananted a vitality characteristic of a new era, all for the modest price of a brother and sister's mild grief.

Babies are born as stars extinguish. The silver rail unfolds. The story of the Astral Express comes to a close, yet it also embarks anew.

Time marches forward, heralding the arrival of a new chapter in the history of Trailblazing expeditions.

The Dreammaster and Sunday's plans were destroyed, and the Aeon of Order slumbered once again. Penacony's Stellaron Crisis was temporarily removed, but there are still hidden currents flowing under the Land of the Dreams' peaceful veneer.

In the first year of the AE 2158, a fiery conspiracy erupted in the Land of the Dreams, but soon faded in chaos and destruction.

Whispers carried the tale of those fateful 48 system hours, when a sun teetered on the precipice of collapse, a paradise stood on the brink of destruction, and a world was poised to surrender to its new master. Amidst it all, a body decayed, a pack of vultures gathered, and a brother and sister were doomed for long separation.

And so, an Aeon succumbed to slumber once more. Some celebrated this fall, while others mourned. Among the insignificant witnesses, mere specks in the vast tapestry of the universe, it was said that this time, the Aeon met THEIR demise with dignity.

As the Cosmos bathed in the radiance of a pure dawn, a tempestuous storm brewed on the horizon. The chant of "Everything for the Amber Lord" grew ever louder. Yet, no matter how one contemplates it, time inexorably swings Qlipoth's colossal hammer in eternal cycles.

The tale of the Astral Express reaches both its conclusion and a new beginning. Time marches forward, heralding the arrival of a new chapter in the history of Trailblazing expeditions.

**Trustful Boy:** Where did that come all of a sudden?

**Guileless Girl:** Because the constellation that looks like a bird, the... Torrent Eagles... looks a bit dim lately.

**Trustful Boy:** It's the Torment Eagles. Don't worry, it's still there. It's just it's located in the inner ring of Penacony and can only be seen when spring and summer overlap.

**Trustful Boy:** As for the question you asked... I think stars do die, just like people.

**Trustful Boy:** But do you know, sister? No star actually belongs to the present. The light we see from them is from a long time ago.

**Trustful Boy:** Even after the stars perish, their light will travel millions of light-years, spanning countless years, to illuminate the night sky of another world.

**Trustful Boy:** In our paradise, I believe there will be a star like that, shining with the same light. Its radiance will last "forever," and its name... will be "happiness."

**Guileless Girl:** No, not just one star! We should have two stars... or maybe even more!

**Trustful Boy:** Yeah, you're right.

**Guileless Girl:** It's a deal!

**Trustful Boy:** It's a deal, then. This is our promise, and nothing will sway our ideals.

**Guileless Girl:** Yeah, you bet!

*(Credits roll)*

Penacony, Land of the Dreams The End Honkai: Star Rail Lead Actors (Trailblazer) Starring Dan Heng as Dan Heng ??? as March 7th Himeko as Himeko Joachim Nokianvirtanen as Welt Yang Firefly as Firefly, SAM Raiden Bosenmori Mei as Acheron Black Swan as Black Swan Kakavasha as Aventurine Mikhail Char Legwork as Misha, Watchmaker Penacony as Gallagher Boothill as Boothill Sparkle as Sparkle Veritas Ratio as Dr. Ratio Robin as Robin Sunday as Sunday "Dreammaster" as Sunday's Servant Gopher Wood as "Dreammaster" Cameo Appearances Silver Wolf Sampo Topaz and Jade Argenti Script Supervisor Elio In Loving Memory Razalina Jane Estella An ex-surveyor aboard the Astral Express and a young scholar, brimming with enthusiasm and curiosity. Hailing from the Zorina-Urd system — enlightened by Remembrance and dominated by academic cliques — she majored in memoria dynamics. To seek out broader academic possibilities, she joined the crew of the Astral Express to build Asdana and launched her research into memoria. Towards the end of the War of Independence, she piloted a shuttle on her own and disappeared into the Memory Zone in search of secrets about the Primordial Dreamscape. Her research results formed the theoretical basis for the development of the entire Penacony to this day, creating a profound impact on the affluence of later generations. She was the true founder behind the Land of the Dreams. Breukelen Tiernan The former guard of the Astral Express and an outstanding gunslinger. Hailing from the frontier world of Frati, his worship of local legend Oakley the Musketeer saw him board the Astral Express. During the war, he was one of Penacony's most accomplished generals and the sole winner of the Fanghunter Medal — an award conferred by Hanunue himself. While reopening the silver rail with the Lampmoth Family during the civil war, he was ambushed by the Swarm and disappeared only to be rescued by the Galaxy Rangers. He fought in the crusade against Lord Ravager Zulo and managed to survive that gruesome battle, then disappeared out of sight completely. That was until the day a Self-Annihilator found his relics. Truly, he was a pure, noble, and selfless warrior. Mikhail Char Legwork Former mechanic of the Astral Express, and the legendary big shot of Penacony, "the Watchmaker." Hailing from the oceanic planet of Lushaka, he was adopted by legendary seafarers, the Mikhail-Char couple. It was just by happenstance that he boarded the Express, making many travel companions along the way while contributing greatly to the prosperity and development of Penacony. Beyond mechanical repairs, he often humbly claimed to possess few skills, except for his knack for spinning tales. "The Watchmaker" was his greatest lie of all, and Penacony's "Festivities" was his final lie. He did not want his star to deviate from the path of freedom, so he intended to cleverly employ falsehoods and keep various Paths existing here for a long time. But he was unable to witness Penacony's end. He died a quiet death in a forgotten corner somewhere, with only a handful in attendance at his funeral. In line with the Nameless' tradition, his Crew ticket was returned to the starry seas. Meanwhile, Penacony remains resplendent in its glory, and the child's dream will continue in perpetuity. May This Journey Lead Us Starward

Aventurine verbally sparred with Jade and hinted that the IPC's plans have been completed. However, Boothill, the Galaxy Ranger, suddenly appeared and headed straight towards the key individual.

*(Return to reality)*

**Jade:** It's a pleasure to hear your voice again. Congratulations, you've become the... biggest winner of this festival.

**Aventurine:** Are you just calling to poke fun at me?

**Jade:** No, I'm just impressed. Not only did you venture alone into Penacony and discover the truth of Dreamflux Reef, but you also managed to escape with the help of that Knight of Beauty...

**Jade:** Remember the recording you received from your Trailblaze friend? It's now the most valuable chip in this game.

**Jade:** However, this came at a high cost... Losing a Cornerstone is a hefty price to pay. Diamond just called a meeting to discuss what to do with you.

**Aventurine:** Just as I expected. So, is Diamond planning to demote me, or kick me out of the Ten Stonehearts?

**Jade:** Why don't you take a wild guess?

**Aventurine:** Well, alright. Then I'll guess... he's going to promote me to P46?

**Jade:** Alright, what will you wager?

**Aventurine:** Are we talking about an actual bet here? I don't wanna wager anything just so that you can swindle it all in the end.

**Aventurine:** But if it's just a friendly bet, I'll put on the line what I did when we first met. I'll bet my life, ma'am.

**Jade:** Interesting. But since it's Diamond's call, no one can predict the outcome.

**Jade:** I'm on my way to Penacony. Once everyone is settled, we'll return to Pier Point for the final showdown.

**Aventurine:** Sounds like I'll be out of the action for a while. Finally, a chance to kick back and relax?

**Jade:** Yeah, leave everything to me and Topaz, child. Thanks to you, as soon as the Jade Stone was delivered to The Family's compound, we finished up our preparations.

**Jade:** The seeds we planted have taken root. Soon... it'll be time to "reap" the rewards.

**Aventurine:** ...Huh?

**Aventurine:** Let's wrap it up for now. Looks like I've got a visitor here...

**Aventurine:** Oh? So many surprises today. Didn't expect a Galaxy Ranger and wanted criminal to show up here...

**Aventurine:** ...One who managed to take out two IPC members under the noses of our fleet. Do you understand what that means?

**Boothill:** Screwubbaboo, I just put them to sleep! Don't try to intimidate me with that nonsense. Besides, I've taken down more IPC lackeys than the residual value you've squeezed, and I don't mind adding a few more zeroes to my wanted poster.

*(Boothill points his revolver at Aventurine)*

**Boothill:** I have a question for you. Be honest now, or I won't think twice about puttin' a bullet in your fudgin' head.

**Boothill:** Tell me... Where is Oswaldo Schneider?

To be continued...

*(Teleports to the guest room in The Reverie (Reality))*

*(Unlock Achievement In the End, We Awaken From Our Dreams)*

*(Unlock Dreamscape Pass Page 17: Stars of Morning and Night)*

*(Mission complete)*
